“Right. Weren’t you excited to visit your hometown after a long time? I heard you worked in the center of the river before enlisting in our merchant.”

“Right. It’s only been five years since I left the place... until then, it was a wasteland.”

“Uh? No way...”

“You! Would I eat expensive food and talk nonsense? I am telling you the truth!”

“If that is the case...”

The man glanced at Ma Cheol’s expression and looked ahead.

He answered as such, but inside he had an entirely different thought.

‘At least say something that makes sense.’

What he could see there was a large city. Then how could that city be built in just five years?

No, he had heard that it hadn’t even been three years since the merchants began to come and go. So in just three years, a city as big as that had been built; even a three-year-old child wouldn’t believe such a thing.

“Let us go first.”

“Yes, leader.”

But what could be done? If the superior said the beer was made with red beans, one had to believe it to work under him.

“Heheh, right.”

Ma Cheol shook his head as if he couldn’t believe it, no matter how much he looked, and led the merchant union towards the city that was right in front of him.

“...”

Ma Cheol opened his mouth wide this time as he reached the outskirts of the city.

When he actually entered the city, it was different from what he had seen from afar. Large buildings lined both sides of the wide street, catching his eye.

‘Doesn’t this look more bustling than the center I knew before?’

Of course, the buildings were all shiny and new. After all, this place was just a wasteland until a short while ago.

What surprised him was that the buildings were all large, and the number of people moving between them seemed greater than those on the streets.

In addition...

“Here, give me some alcohol! I have come a long way, and I am thirsty!”

“Yes, yes! Coming right up!”

“And the noodles will take time?”

“Oh my! I will be leaving soon! Just a moment! Just a moment!”

The entire hall was packed with people. It was amazing where all these people had come from.

And this complex and lively city didn’t even give Ma Cheol the time to enjoy it slowly.

Before he could even turn his eyes, two people appeared from somewhere and grabbed his left and right.

“Oh my, merchants! Thank you for coming! This way! Come this way! I will guide you to the best guesthouse on Plum Blossom Island!”

“Oh my! This guy is cheating again! How can your Godly Wind Guesthouse be the best on Plum Blossom Island? We have the Dragon Flower Guesthouse!”

“What, you moron! Are you talking about this after catching an old man who ran away last week because you gave him a hard time? Merchants, don’t listen to this nonsense! If you go there, you will end up eating tasteless food! Our Godly Wind Guesthouse is the best for food!”

“Here comes another nonsense! Haven’t you heard the rumor that our inn hired a chef from Hwa-pyung House, the best hall in the world? Compared to the food prepared by the chef from there, your inn’s food is worse than dog food! Merchants, come this way! There is just one room left in our guesthouse!”

“How can these two sleep in one room, you brat!”

“Oh my! I made a mistake and said one when there were two!”

Ma Cheol, caught in the middle, was out of his mind.

He thought of yelling at them for being rude, but when he glanced at them, he saw another person in front of him in a similar situation. The other person who followed them was the same.

‘Is this normal here?’

What kind of guest house host came out on the street to get customers?

“This man!”

“Do you want to fight today?”

Ma Cheol shouted in surprise as the people rolled up their sleeves like they wanted to fight at any moment.

“D-don’t fight. We are going to stop by the deck first...”

“Uh? Even if you go today, it will take four days for the merchant ship to be able to move?”

“T-t-that long?”

“Oh, my leader. Is this your first time at Plum Blossom Island?”

“Y-yes.”

The server nodded like he understood.

“Then you can do this. Waiting for 3-4 days is a daily routine here. If not, why would all the merchants be staying around and resting?”

“Ah...”

“If you are lucky, it will take four days, and if you are unlucky, then ten days.”

“T-ten days? Do you mean when it rains heavily or storms?”

“No, no. The ships of Plum Blossom Island never stop operating, whether it rains, snows, or is windy. Even if you load them as such, when there are a lot of people, you will have to wait ten days. So it is better to find lodgings first.”

“... I-I want to check it with my own eyes first.”

“I, I said something useless then.”

“Such a shameless man. Oh, my merchant, of course, you should check out the condition first. Instead, if you come back, you know you won’t be scammed... no, don’t suffer any losses and come straight to the Dragon Flower Guesthouse.”

Ma Cheol nodded with a face that looked so lost.

“But... can I ask you one thing?”

“Uh? Feel free to ask.”

“Isn’t this the riverside?”

“Yes?”

“That... there is such a bustling place by the riverside... won’t the pirates come in then?”

“Uh? Pirates?”

The two of them looked at each other as if they had never thought of it before and then burst into laughter.

“AHAHAHAH! Pirates?”

“Hahahahah! Wow! It’s been so long since I heard something like this.”

Ma Cheol blinked his eyes, not knowing what was happening.

“What... did I say something odd?”

“It isn’t odd. It’s just that it’s been so long since I’ve heard that. Don’t worry. Since the Plum Blossom Island was established, the pirates never come near them.”

“Sometimes we pass them by the river, but we have never been attacked. They are aware of it, so how dare they invade the place guarded by the world’s best, the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

“This is the safest place on the Yangtze River. Isn’t that why so many merchants come to Plum Blossom Island every day?”

Ma Cheol nodded.

He had heard that and came here, but there was a huge difference between what he had vaguely imagined and what he saw with his own eyes.

“I-I understand. I will stop by the dock first.”

“Yes! Take a look around.”

“Dragon Flower Guesthouse! Dragon Flower Guesthouse!”

Even after barely turning down the two people, he had to endure more harassment from a few servers who had joined him, and only after dealing with all of them was he finally able to continue on his way.

“Hehehe. What kind of place is...”

As he walked comfortably without being harassed, the surrounding scenery became more clearly visible.

Nothing was missing.

A tavern, tea house, diner...

“A gambling house?”

“... there is everything here.”

This looked more like a bustling marketplace than a pleasure district. The only difference was that this was a practical place compared to places solely for pleasure.

As he passed the tavern and bars and approached the dock, he saw large warehouses lined up on both sides. As he passed the warehouses, he saw people and carts crowded together.

“It seems to be there.”

“Let us head there first.”

Ma Cheol approached the dock and spoke to the merchant standing nearby.

“I...”

“What?”

“I am someone who just arrived. Where should I stand in line to make a reservation?”

The merchant who was first in line glanced at Ma Cheol and gestured behind him.

“You can stand here.”

“Ah, thank you. Guys, pull the cart here.”

“Yes!”

Ma Cheol, who was standing in line, looked around again. The people who filled the dock and the five large ships anchored there. And the workers who were loading the ships with goods.

This was when they saw they were full of energy.

“This is amazing.”

“... there are five ships. Those big ones.”

“Even though there are five ships like that, we have to wait for more than four days. How much cargo comes to this dock?”

“All the goods from the Yangtze River are unloaded here, so there is no choice. It is a hundred times better than being robbed on the river. Still, we are lucky. How many people are crossing the river because the distance is too far, crying and eating seeds.”

“Right, leader.”

Ma Cheol still felt blank and let out a hollow laugh.

‘This is when the saying, ‘a mulberry field looks like a blue sea’ is used.’

Who would have thought that the world would change so much in just five years? No, not five years. It had been just three years...

That was when.

“What are you all doing? Why are you interfering?”

“Uh?”

Suddenly, a group of people swarmed and glared at Ma Cheol, threatening him.

“Interfering? We were just standing in line, though?”

“I was leaving my luggage to take care of personal business, and you took my place in the meantime. Stop trying to avoid this and move out of my way right now! Unless you want trouble!”

“What? What interference!”

“What? Interference? These people!”

The merchant ran up and grabbed Ma Cheol by the collar.

“If you stand in the wrong line once, and your turn is cut off in front of me, you’ll have to lose another day. Do you know how much you will lose because of it? Where are you going?”

“Well, then, shouldn’t you have just stayed in your place? Well, I will not move out!”

A merchant couldn’t afford to lose a fight.

Knowing that, Ma Cheol raised his voice, and the merchant who grabbed him by the collar glared.

“Ah, this is not how it’s supposed to be. You will be so done today.”

As their fight escalated, those watching from the side clicked their tongues.

“Uh! Excuse me! You!”

“... Uh?”

“What do you think this place is? You keep yelling here!”

“What do I think...”

The merchant who grabbed Ma Cheol by the collar tilted his head, seemingly confused.

Then, the goat-bearded merchant who had intervened looked at the two people with a knowing look.

“You two don’t know much about Plum Blossom Island, do you? Is this your first time?”

“...”

“....”

As both people blinked, the goat-bearded merchant clicked his tongue and shook his head.

“If you get caught doing this, you will be kicked out of here right away and won’t be able to use their services for a month. You haven’t forgotten that this is Plum Blossom Island, right? If you don’t plan on risking your life crossing the river, then keep quiet here. If you two fight and it affects us, I will not stand still. You get it?”

Ma Cheol asked back, unsure.

“We create a mess, and we cannot transport anything?”

“Yes.”

“Ah, no. Does that make sense? Isn’t this a place that takes money and moves our luggage?”

“...what?”

“Hehe, seriously.”

“That’s why you people are acting like that.”

The merchants around him widened their eyes and clicked their tongues, while Ma Cheol turned red with embarrassment.

“Uh, first time?”

“...Yes, we are a small merchant group, and the previous leader quit because he got sick... so I moved in a hurry.”

“Tsk tsk. So you don’t know what’s happening. Look now. This is Plum Blossom Island. If you use your normal senses here, you’ll be in trouble. The rules of Plum Blossom Island are simple: Don’t cause trouble, don’t make a fuss, and do your math right.”

“...”

“The most important of those is not to cause trouble! Do you know how many merchants got kicked out because they broke the rule?”

“...”

“Don’t do anything useless and stay quiet now... damn it! I’m late!”

“Uh?”

It was then.

Whisper. Whisper.

Suddenly, the front turned noisy, and the merchants stepped back to the left and right. As the path opened up between them, a group of men walked ahead with terrifying gazes.

A huge man, who must have been 7 feet tall.

A beard with spikes sticking out.

Sharp eyes.

Where did he leave his shirt? His upper body was exposed, the muscles bulging as if they would burst. He must have been carrying luggage all the time, as thick sweat formed around the muscles and ran down.

“Uh...”

Just as Ma Cheol, who was completely terrified by that overwhelming sight, shrank his neck, the people who walked out looked at him with all the irritation in the world on their faces.

At that moment, Ma Cheol’s heart sank.

“This...!”

The man’s eyes turned bloodshot as he looked up and down at Ma Cheol’s group.

At the same time, Ma Cheol’s face turned pale.

‘I-I am dead now.’

Oh my, why did he come here...

As if he couldn’t hold back his anger, the man trembled with his large body and then opened his mouth like a roaring tiger.

“What is the problem, sir?”

“...”

“Is there something bothering you?”

“... Uh?”

From a face that looked like it could eat a human, the kindest tone emerged.

“T-that...”

No.

Just what on earth was this?

Just what...

Chapter 859: Mount Hua Will Get Stronger. (4)

“You are standing in line to make a reservation?”

“Yes. It was said here. But that person...”

“Just one minute.”

The man called Ma Cheol lowered his voice and then looked around as if searching for something. Suddenly, he let out a roar.

“Mok-oh! Where is that bastard, Mok-oh!”

“Oh my, big brother! I am coming now!”

As soon as he finished speaking, a man came running down.

‘What is that?’

A man with a huge knife wound on his face and a terrifying expression, unlike anything the merchants had seen before, ran in fear and stood in front of the large man.

“Now, hold on for a moment...”

“Kwang!”

Before he could finish speaking, the man’s fist struck Mok-oh on the head. Mok-oh, who was slammed into the ground, grabbed his head in pain.

“Kuaak!”

‘Woah...’

Did the ground shake for a moment when he was just hit?

“Get up, you useless moron!”

“Yes, yes, big brother!”

The man with the knife wound suddenly got up from the ground.

His rather long face seemed to have gotten shorter, but it wasn’t important.

“I told you to guide them, and you just left?”

“I really went to the office just for a bit! I mean it, big brother!”

“If you make one more mistake, I will peel your skin and use it as a snack for drinking! Got it?”

“Yes! Yes! That will not happen!”

“Such a useless thing!”

Jang Jeong glared at the man, threatening him, then turned his gaze to Ma Cheol.

‘Eik’

The moment he met those sharp eyes, his mouth went dry, and sweat started to pour out of his body. Anyway, it was true that he had caused a commotion, so he knew what was going to happen now.

But Jang Jeong’s actions this time were beyond his expectations.

“Oh my, I am sorry.”

“...”

“We should have informed you in advance, but this useless guy left... we will handle the situation right away.”

Jang Jeong began to bow, crumpling up his large body. Ma Cheol nodded in shock.

“T-thank you!”

“But.”

“Uh?”

At that moment, the man straightened his back.

“Whatever the reason is, you cannot fight or make a fuss on Plum Blossom Island. It seems like you are new here, so I will let this go, but please be careful in the future.”

“Uh? Ah... Yes! Yes! Of course!”

Not making a fuss.

How could you make a fuss when you saw those muscles? He had just seen people get knocked on the head and pinned to the ground.

“This is where the people who are going to the ship line up. The ship reservations are being accepted at the Pavilion in the back, so head there.”

“Uh? N-not here?”

No, that wasn’t the point, was it?

“Then, everyone here is leading today...”

Ma Cheol’s eyes widened.

There were so many merchants here that it was shocking. But that wasn’t all of them!

‘R-right! If I think about it, it is obvious.’

That was why there was a guesthouse and warehouse there, right?

“Yes. Go over there and make a reservation. Then we will set a date for your load to be moved.”

“T-thank you.”

Contrary to his appearance and size, like the prison guards of hell, his voice was incredibly friendly.

That was why it felt scarier.

“Yes, then.”

The man turned around and shouted.

“Don’t just watch and load! You moron! If the loading is delayed today, don’t even think about having meals at night! Get it?”

“Yes!”

“If you understand, then move!”

As Ma Cheol was watching the scene in confusion, he heard the talks around.

“That leader is in a good mood today. Look at this.”

“Right. Isn’t his expression looking so good today?”

“Hehe. I thought I was looking at Buddha. He must really be liking it.”

That? Was it his good look?

Were all these people’s eyes not working?

“Woah, is this how it’s always been?”

The merchant who was fighting with Ma Cheol was also shocked, opening his eyes wide and asking.

“What are you talking about?”

“Ah, no, that person...”

“Ah, that man? That is how it is.”

“...”

“He might look like an evil person on the outside, but he is incredibly kind to merchants. Actually, everyone here on Plum Blossom Island is like that.”

“... they say you cannot judge a person by their appearance.”

“That isn’t true.”

“Uh?”

The merchants were indignant.

“At the start, on Plum Blossom Island, people who treated merchants rudely got beaten up so badly by the Taoists that it ended up like this.”

“Ugh. I still remember that. I was watching from the side, and it was the first time in my life that I realized a person could be hit like that. What did he say? ‘This is how you beat people up as learned from the Taoist sect’?”

“Hahahah! That Taoist surely knew how to joke. He learned to beat people up from his sect. Hahahah.”

No, these guys! Was this a laughing matter?

“C-can a human be hit like that?”

“What is wrong with it? Those who got hit are lucky.”

“Uh?”

The merchant smiled and gestured to the mountain behind him.

“Do you see that mountain there?”

“... Yes. I have eyes, you know.”

“Some people got buried in that mountain after touching merchants’ goods.”

Ma Cheol’s eyes widened.

“B-buried?”

“Right. I heard it clearly back then. The Taoist smiled brightly and said, ‘Beat those bastards until they can barely breathe, and bury them alive.’”

“...”

“Then he apologized a hundred times to the merchants and paid them tenfold the price of their goods.”

“He could have just returned the goods, but he is such a great man.”

“Ah, the island lord is such a gentleman. He is a gentleman!”

Were these people insane?

Didn’t they understand what they were saying? People were buried, and they called him a gentleman? A gentleman?

“That... island lord must be a very scary person?”

“What did you hear? A gentleman? He is a great man!”

Ma Cheol gave up trying to understand them any longer.

Whether he was a gentleman or a backstreet man who buried people had nothing to do with him.

But the following words neatly shattered his thoughts.

“If you are curious, why don’t you just look at him?”

“Uh?”

“Didn’t you say you were going to make reservations?”

“Yes.”

“Since the island lord is the one taking reservations, go and have a look.”

“...”

“Ah, I am saying just in case. You have to be careful of what you say. The island lord is a gentleman, but the ones assisting him are not.”

“Hehehe, if you make a mistake, your throat should hurt!”

“That will be refreshing. Ah, right. Hahahah.”

Ma Cheol tightly closed his eyes.

On this Plum Blossom Island, there seemed to be no one in their right mind—not even a worker, merchant, or anyone.

“... this is huge.”

“Truly...”

“They say that the Plum Blossom Island sweeps up all the money in the Yangtze River.”

Everyone’s mouths dropped open in shock at the grandeur of the Pavilion before their eyes.

They couldn’t even see where the wall ended.

They had traveled all over the Central Plains and seen countless regions. Still, they had almost no memory of seeing a pavilion this big.

“The main merchant office also seems smaller than this.”

“R-right?”

“... and there are many people here too.”

People were bustling in and out of the Pavilion’s main gate. The only difference was that, unlike the docks earlier, no one had brought luggage there.

“It was right to find lodging and leave the carts behind.”

“I should have just done as I was told.”

The merchants who had been wasting their time looked at Ma Cheol with resentment. Ma Cheol’s face twisted.

‘Those people who didn’t even speak earlier...’

Whenever something went wrong, they always blamed themselves later. Merchants!

“Let’s go first.”

“Yes.”

When they passed through the main gate, a large main hall appeared. As they followed the guide in front of them and went inside, a rather unfamiliar sight unfolded before them.

In a large room, which must have been ten times the size of a decent house, there were rows of desks with people sitting in front of them, constantly writing something down.

“Where did the receipts that were here go?”

“Ledgers! Yesterday’s unloading ledgers! Who took the ones I brought in?”

“I told you today was collection day!”

“Oh my! I am losing my mind! The numbers don’t match up! Who was wrong?”

It was like a battlefield.

‘All those are ledgers?’

It was hard to imagine how many things had to come and go for all those people to be mobilized to write the ledgers. Even the biggest merchants in the Central Plains wouldn’t have to work on this many ledgers.

And they wouldn’t be working so tirelessly.

“This way. Line up here.”

After passing that place and going further inside, he saw people waiting in line, and at the end of it was a large desk.

“Oh...”

A large folding screen with ten symbols spread out behind the desk, and a man sitting between the desk and the folding screen was welcoming the customers with a gentle smile.

“...it feels amazing.”

“Really?”

A pure white scholar’s robe and a large crown on his head. The only flaw was that his eyes looked a little small, but thanks to the gentle smile on his lips, even that gave off a gentle feeling.

And there was a feather fan in his hand...

“... it looks like he is the incarnation of Zhuge Liang.”

‘Ah, right. It exactly feels like that.’

When he added those working in front, it really felt like that. It felt like the past works of the man would have been like this...

“But didn’t you say that the Plum Blossom Island lord was a bandit?”

Uh?

“Then they are all bandits?”

Uh?

Ma Cheol blinked and looked around again.

‘When I see it again...’

Something felt a little strange.

Everyone was wearing clean robes and diligently writing their accounts. Still, if one looked at their faces, they resembled more the kind of people you would see in a back alley than scholars.

‘No... did they all roll around on the ground with knives?’

Their wrists, slightly visible through the large sleeves, their necks visible through the collars, and even the sword marks on their faces made the onlookers feel uneasy.

‘What on earth are they doing here?’

Why were these people looking at the books? Why? Didn’t they do business in the back alleys?

“This side.”

Bewildered, they kept looking around, and soon, it was their turn.

“Yes!”

They went to the front as instructed, and a person in robes like Zhuge Liang greeted them with a bright smile.

“Welcome. I heard you are from the Great North Merchants.”

“Yes, yes! Right. It is my first time here, so I want to make a reservation for the ship...”

“Welcome. I am Im So-Byeong, the island lord of Plum Blossom Island.”

“Ah... Yes! I am Ma Cheol, the person in charge of the Great North Merchants.”

“Since it is the first time, I have invited you separately. I really welcome you to Plum Blossom Island. Wouldn’t it be great if we could have good ties, right? Please take care of me in the future.”

“Ah! Yes! Rather, we should be the ones requesting that from you! Please take care of me!”

“Haha. Don’t be so humble.”

Im So-Byeong smiled brightly.

There was absolutely no trace of anything gloomy or dangerous in that innocent smile.

‘Could it be a misunderstanding?’

This person buried someone?

How could a scholar who cannot even catch a rabbit do such a bad thing?

‘There must have been some misunderstanding....’

At that moment.

A man rushed into the hall and whispered something in Im So-Byeong’s ear. Im So-Byeong, listening to those words with a smiling face, raised his lips even more.

“Really?”

“Yes, island lord.”

“If you do something wrong, you need to pay the price. Just knead the person all night, and when the sun sets, throw them in the middle of the river to make him fish food.”

“He is so skilled at handcrafting, won’t he still get away?”

“Tsk, tsk. Do I even have to tell you that?”

Im So-Byeong’s face turned gloomy for a moment.

“Cut his tendons, tie his hands and feet with iron chains. Just in case, break his dantian. And put weights on his feet so he can never rise again. Ah, right, sprinkle salt on his wounds. It is too much sin to just wait around.”

“Understood.”

“Do it neatly, neatly.”

“Yes, island lord.”

“Well, they are born thieves, so if you are not careful, we will get in trouble. Tell them exactly what happens if they touch things recklessly. And tell them to watch everyone closely.”

“I will.”

Im So-Byeong waved his hand to send the man away, clicked his tongue, and looked at Ma Cheol with a bright smile again.

“I am sorry; where did we end our conversation?”

“...”

“Sir?”

“... please spare me.”

“Uh?”

It seemed... that they had come to a place they shouldn’t have stepped into.

Chapter 860: Mount Hua Will Get Stronger. (5)

Plum Blossom Island was bustling with merchants every day.

When Mount Hua first built the island, it was not small in scale, but it grew significantly over the years. Now, it was so large that even the biggest merchants couldn’t resist visiting.

It was only natural.

Plum Blossom Island monopolized the logistics between the South and North of the river.

Whether evil and justice factions were at war or simply drawing a line and snarling, people needed to eat to live and wear clothes to go out.

Whether they were merchants from the North of the River wanting to bring goods to the South or merchants from the South wanting to bring goods to the North, they all prioritized Plum Blossom Island. Now, it has become difficult to even fit ten ships there.

“Why would I resort to banditry when I can earn money so easily?”

“... Is this going to last a lifetime?”

“The problem with the first elder is that you are too negative.”

“The pine cicadas need to eat pine needles. The Lord of Green Forest these days is beyond speaking terms.”

“Don’t starve to death in search of dignity; just do your job well.”

“Ugh.”

“And the preparation?”

“... Almost finished. But is this really something we can do? We are the Green Forest. And for the Green Forest to escort the merchants...”

“If it is something worth the money, then there is no reason we cannot do it. Didn’t we collect the toll in the first place for that reason?”

“But the fundamentals of...”

“We need to change to survive.”

Im So-Byeong smiled.

As the Evil Faction, people became more and more rampant; even those who crossed Plum Blossom Island to travel were now often suffering damage. Large merchants could hire escorts, but the small ones had a hard time doing so.

Thus, they were preparing to organize a trip by ship with merchants crossing the island and provide a person to escort the trip with the Green Forest.

“If we can do this, more money will come in. These days, more people are playing around since we don’t do banditry anymore, so how can we miss this chance and make money by using those messing around?”

“... There will be people who don’t like it.”

“If we just make an example out of them and handle it a couple of times, it would vanish.”

The first elder gulped as he listened to the calm words.

He could be smiling on the outside, but at times like this, he realized that this person was the true king of Green Forest.

“Then Plum Blossom Island will also have a solid path.”

“It is just a temporary gain until the Four Precepts Alliance finds us.”

“So? Before that, let us eat everything we can. Do you know how much money we make in a day?”

“That is why it is unfair. We do all the work, but aren’t we getting just a portion of the money?”

“Tsk tsk. How foolish. If we had done this by ourselves, do you think the merchants would have stayed around Plum Blossom Island? They trust us because of the names of Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance. No, if we had taken this island for ourselves before that, the pirates and Ten Thousand People clan wouldn’t stay still.”

The first elder sighed deeply.

“I know. I know that... but don’t they make money too easily? Even if we eat a little more than what....”

“Elder.”

Im So-Byeong’s voice turned dreary at that moment. The elder straightened up.

“I-I am sorry, Green Forest King.”

“Do you know why people die?”

“... I don’t really know.”

“It is because they get too greedy. Like the guy who sank down into the river today.”

“...”

“I want to believe that the elder isn’t such a moron.”

“Yes, that much is obvious.”

“The elder is a bandit, so you probably are a bit greedy. But... there are things in the world you shouldn’t touch.”

Cold sweat ran down the elder’s back.

“... I will keep that in mind.”

“It is better to do everything within limits, within limits.”

Im So-Byeong returned to his usual smiling demeanor.

“But...”

His gaze shifted to the side.

Beyond the window on the wall, he could see the river flowing wildly and Plum Blossom Island.

“... as the elder said, I don’t know how long I can maintain this.”

The situation was worsening.

The plum tree growing on the island caught his attention.

“Plum Blossom Island isn’t named for the plums, but for the people who are like plums...”

A faint longing flashed through his eyes, and sighing softly, he said,

“Well, let us hurry up. If the Taoist doesn’t kick the door open and wreak havoc on the island, it will be none other than me or the first elder drowning in the middle of the river.”

“... that sounds horrible.”

“It was unbearable before, but now...”

Im So-Byeong shook his head as if he didn’t even want to imagine it.

“It is horrible, ugh, so disgusting.”

But the first elder didn’t miss the strange smile on Im So-Byeong’s lips and joked,

“Honestly, we are doing pretty well compared to those people running around sweating.”

“That is definitely true.”

“So let us not joke around. If that man hears us complaining, he will try to stab us in the forehead with a poisoned needle.”

Then the first elder’s face turned angry.

“... those people are so arrogant these days.”

“First elder.”

“I am just expressing how I feel. The complaints from the others are rising.”

“Phew.”

Im So-Byeong pressed his fan against his forehead as if he had a headache.

“Tell him to be as self-restrained as possible.”

“But...”

“I know. That is why I am telling you.”

“... yes, Green Forest King.”

The first elder bowed his head deeply.

“Then,”

“Right. Leave.”

After he left, Im So-Byeong looked out the window again with a stern face.

‘Is this the limit?’

On the outside, it was filled with only the best things. But Im So-Byeong, who was in the middle of it all, felt the cracks more keenly now.

‘The absence of Mount Hua is too great.’

Im So-Byeong and Tang Gunak were doing their best to stop it. However, if the situation continued a little longer, the Heavenly Friends Alliance might collapse from the inside.

If it hadn’t been for Mount Hua in the first place, there would have been no way that Green Forest would be standing with the Tang family.

“So slow. Tsk.”

A sigh escaped the lips of Im So-Byeong as he looked at the withered plum tree.

“Please hurry up a little, Taoist. I am at my limit now.”

A voice too small to reach the distant island rivers.

“I am reporting.”

Tang Gunak, sitting in the top seat, nodded quietly.

If things were as they were, the place he would have been receiving reports from would have been the house of the Sichuan Tang family. Still, the place he was currently sitting was not the Sichuan Tang family but a large room in a manor.

Tang Gunak, sitting there, stared ahead with a stern face.

Those who knew him could tell he had become a little more serious just by looking at his expression.

“There was another clash between the Evil Faction and the Justice Faction in Lu’an.”

Tang Gunak’s brows furrowed at the report he received.

“And...”

“Yes. I think it will spread a little more with time.”

Tang Gunak slowly tapped his finger on the table. His expression hadn’t changed much, but the noise represented his discomfort.

Tang Pae, who had caught that, sighed and said,

“Since Lu’an is an Anhui city, if it were normal, the Namgung family would step in to help...”

The Namgung family still locked their doors and stayed home.

They didn’t specifically declare a closed gate, but it was no different from it. The only difference was that the Namgung family didn’t reject visitors from outside. It was almost like they didn’t engage in external activities now.

In the past, there would have been no way for the Evil Factions to come and fight in Anhui, where the Namgung family was located, especially there.

But the world was now so different.

“Looking for those who aren’t there will not provide an answer.”

“Yes.”

“Send someone to help mediate.”

“... their momentum is so strong. It doesn’t seem like a situation where words can persuade.”

“Then send men to suppress them by force.”

“Yes, I understand.”

A sigh escaped from Tang Pae’s mouth.

Even though they claimed they didn’t engage in external activities, Anhui was clearly the land of the Namgung family. Sending troops there to suppress someone would be a burden.

But now wasn’t the time to delve into details.

‘It is happening too fast.’

The world had changed so much.

The most obvious change was that many of the sects that were around the South had moved to the North.

The sects that moved to the North have settled in and clashed not only with the existing Justice faction but also with the existing Evil sects residing there.

If they were to settle in someone else’s land, they should at least show some restraint by respecting the order. But would they be considered an Evil Faction if that were the case?

“...we could have stopped it a long time ago.”

“It cannot be helped.”

Tang Gunak shook his head with a serious look.

The reason why we didn’t stop the Evil Factions from crossing over to the North, even though they knew such things would happen, was simple.

“How can we stop them from wanting to live? Even if they are Evil Factions, they are made of people.”

“That is true, but...”

If they had come to invade the land, they would have been stopped. But they hadn’t moved to attack the place. The Evil Faction moving now has crossed the river to live.

The Four Precepts Alliance had swept around the Southern Lands, demanding only submission.

Either kneel or perish.

Most of the Evil Faction chose to kneel down and submit, while a small number resisted and met a miserable end.

The rest neither submitted nor resisted; they fled.

“I understand the situation... but shouldn’t they at least remain quiet?”

Tang Gunak shook his head again.

In the first place, a warrior sect was a place that could not produce a grain of rice on its own. If they could not secure their land and collect money, they were no different from beggars.

Therefore, the Evil Faction had come to the Northern side with no choice but to secure land and survive.

“Well, we need to ensure that the civilians aren’t harmed.”

“I will do so.”

“Anything else?”

Tang Pae looked at Tang Gunak’s face and said.

“... Lord, in the past month, we have intervened in more than seven big and small disturbances.”

“Right.”

“In the past, it was just two or three a year. It’s getting too much for us to handle.”

“I know.”

The places where the Evil Faction sects had settled were mostly Hubei and Anhui.

Those two places were where the Evil Faction had the hardest time growing in the past. They were managed by the Wudang and Namgung sects.

But now, with the two sects closing their gates, they had become the best places for the Evil Faction sects to settle down. There was plenty of food, and the land had no owners.

It wasn’t easy for small and medium sects and clans to deal with them. As a result, the Tang family had to run around and mediate.

“How long will this continue...”

“It is for the sake of the Alliance’s reputation.”

“I know, family lord. But the discontent in the sect is growing. Even though we are in the Alliance, the Beast Palace and Ice Palace haven’t done a thing in the Central Plains. We are the only ones handling this work, right?”

“...”

“Mount Hua started it, but we are handling the aftermath. At best, the Green Forest is helping... if they said openly that if we are going to take on such a worthless task, we should have never come to the Yangtze River.”

Tang Pae, who was speaking, went silent.

“And... I think the same. How many years has it been since we were back in Sichuan? Why are we the—”

“Enough.”

“Lord.”

“If we were only after profit, we wouldn’t be here. Do you want to become thieves like them?”

“...no.”

“Then.”

Tang Gunak said with a stern face.

“The one who is having the hardest time now isn’t us. It is Mount Hua.”

“...”

“Let us try a bit harder.”

“...I understand, Lord.”

“Good. Now go.”

“Yes.”

As soon as Tang Pae went out, Tang Gunak sighed.

‘It isn’t easy anymore.’

The world was tightening its grip on them more and more.

The Four Precepts Alliance, which had finished the unification, had begun internal reorganization. Once that ended, they could push into the North at any time. Knowing this, chaos was growing in the North of the river. Everyone must have felt that the inevitable war was coming.

In this situation, the sects that could be working to stabilize the land have given up on the issue, and the gap to fill was too big.

And it wasn’t easy for the Tang family to fill that gap.

And...

Even things that would have been resolved simply in the past have now turned into problems. The voices of discontent flowing out from within the family were growing louder.

Tang Gunak lightly rubbed his eyes.

‘It has been a thousand days now. Do you still need more time?’

A tired look crossed Tang Gunak’s face. He couldn’t help but realize the limits of his ability. How could he handle the Heavenly Friends Alliance when he couldn’t even control his internal discontent?

He sighed deeply and cast his gaze northward.

‘Taoist.’

Tang Gunak mumbled softly.

“Please hurry a little early. Just a little.”

From that place where the scent of plum blossoms would soon fill the air, no news had come yet.

Chapter 861: Even If You Don't Know Where You Are, Know Your Place (1)

“B-branch leader! We are in trouble! Another fight broke out!”

“What? Again?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face twisted in frustration.

“Those damned brats! Are they possessed by ghosts who died in fights? What are they trying to do, constantly fighting?”

The beggars flinched at his furious words and glanced around nervously.

“Where this time?”

“That...”

“Is it Hubei again? Or Anhui? No, no! Hunan? Hunan is still preoccupied with Shaolin, so that couldn’t have happened.”

“T-that is...”

“Tell me quickly! I am busy!”

“Shaanxi.”

“... Where?”

“Shaanxi...”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes widened in disbelief.

“Shaanxi?”

“... Yes.”

“Did you say Shaanxi? Right now? That Shaanxi?”

“Y-yes.”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face turned pale.

“N-no! Not there!”

He darted his eyes left and right, then shouted with a dark expression.

“Beggars! Damn fighting bastards! Who is available with empty hands now?”

“... What beggars? The ones who can fight have all been sent to Hubei and Anhui.”

“None?”

“Right now, none...”

“If there aren’t any, is that the end?”

Hong Dae-Kwang grabbed the bowl on the table and hurled it at the beggars in front of him.

“Eik!”

“If there is none, then make one! There is a fight in Shaanxi right now, you brats! Who wants to see it end in death?”

One of the beggars, observing the enraged Hong Dae-Kwang, spoke up hesitantly.

“No, branch leader. It isn’t like there is chaos, just there. What is so special about...”

“What?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s gaze bore into the beggar who dared to speak.

The beggar flinched under his stare but tried to avoid eye contact, speaking as if he had nothing to lose.

“Isn’t that the truth? Just leave it alone...”

“You brat!”

In the end, Hong Dae-Kwang leaped over the table and kicked the beggar in the chest with both feet.

“Ackk!”

As the beggar screamed and fell, Hong Dae-Kwang erupted with a roar that could put the Shaolin to shame.

“This brat doesn’t even understand the situation right now, or what!? Should I twist your arms to make it easier for you to beg? Huh?!”

“Oh my! Be patient, branch leader!”

“This guy hasn’t been here for long!”

“He is a country kid from Hunan and doesn’t know what Shaanxi is like!”

“Ugh, damn it!”

Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t hold back his anger and gritted his teeth.

“You brat! If one accident happens in Shaanxi, we are all done for. What? Evil Faction? Four Precepts Alliance? Those kids who are afraid of those ones haven’t even experienced true bitterness in life! Have you ever had your head broken, brat!?”

The beggar who got hit out of nowhere, Bong Oh, looked around, not knowing what was happening.

Even though Hong Dae-Kwang was talking such nonsense, the other beggars weren’t stopping him. They just tried to prevent any violence towards Bong Oh.

‘What is this?’

Why was the Beggars Union a Beggars Union?

Wasn’t the Beggars Union because they were beggars who had nothing and who were satisfied by speaking the truth even if their bowls were broken or they were beaten?

Those who said that something was wrong, no matter what, were the ones who were the Beggars Union. The fact that there was no objection meant these people were sympathizing with Hong Dae-Kwang.

‘W-what is with Shaanxi?’

What was so special about Shaanxi?

“Mobilize all the beggars we have!”

“Due to the recent call of a meeting in Hubei, all the beggars who can fight were taken! There are no fighters left here.”

“Then what are you, you beggar!”

Hong Dae-Kwang glared.

“Ah, no, we are...”

“If we aren’t here, who will collect information? I am not saying this because I don’t want to go.”

Hong Dae-Kwang let out a deep sigh.

“Beggars”

“Uh?”

“Think about it. How long has that bastard locked himself there?”

“Around... a thousand days?”

“Then will that Gate open soon or not?”

“... It will open.”

“Of course.”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes shone.

“If that bastard opens up the Gates and comes out to see Shaanxi in shambles, where do you think he will move to break heads?”

“... Southern Edge? Ack!”

Hong Dae-Kwang kicked the beggar who answered that way.

“Southern Edge? What Southern Edge, you brat! What will he do to find those bastards who have locked themselves in for 5 years now? Huh! This is where he comes, you bastard!!”

“Eik!”

“N-No...!”

“Amitabha! Amitabha! Amitabha!”

The faces of the beggars all turned dark then.

Only Bong Oh had a puzzled look, unable to understand the situation, and he asked.

“I... I don’t understand what you are talking about now... who is coming?”

“The devil.”

“Demon.”

“Beast.”

“... Uh?”

The beggar standing next to him put his arm around Bong Oh’s shoulder and whispered.

“Yah, fucking beggar.”

“Uh?”

“You haven’t been in Shaanxi for long, so you probably don’t get it. If you want to beg for food and survive in Shaanxi, you need to get one thing in your head, got it?”

“... What is it?”

“There is a demon living in Mount Hua.”

“...”

“If you get caught, you will be beaten to death, and then you will be beaten again, and then you will get your emergency funds taken away. If that isn’t enough, you will have to beg and pay him too.”

“That is if he is in a good mood! If you get caught by him in a bad mood... huh! Oh my, Jong Pal! Get your senses back!”

“Someone give that kid cold water! Who talked about Chung Myung in front of Jong Pal?”

The beggar, whose face became blue and eventually fell down, was dragged by other beggars.

“Well, there is no use in listening to it for a hundred days, and there is no need to understand. You will find out why anyway.”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who was getting anxious, scratched his head.

“Fucking Shaanxi is where they touched.”

This was happening every day in Hubei, Anhui, and Hunan. But now, in Shaanxi, of all places? This meant the entire Central Plains would be caught in this chaos.

“Damn those Nine Great Sects bastards!”

“Branch leader, if this keeps up, all the beggars will die. Shouldn’t we say something to the Nine Sects?”

“Then, what?”

“Uh?”

“You brat, don’t you think all of them are the same? How many sects are north of the river? The Southern Island Sect is stuck on the island beyond the southern side! The Danciang Sect is in Yunnan! Kunlun is at the end of Sichuan! Qingcheng and Emei are in Sichuan, in Sichuan!”

“....”

“Kwangdong is stuck way across near the North Sea! Since something happened in Hubei, should we tell those bastards to come and handle it?”

“... that is tough.”

“The land of the North is taken by Shaolin, Wudang, and Southern Edge! The rest is sucked up by the Namgung family. But aren’t three of them currently behind closed gates? And what are we to do? Should I tell the Hubei Peng family to come to the central plains and help?”

“C-calm down, branch leader.”

“I told everyone we should end things with the Nine Sects and join with the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

“Heavenly Friends Alliance isn’t that good either.”

“Ohhh.”

Hong Dae-Kwang scratched his head.

If it were as before, he could have asked the Nine Great Sects outside of the Central Plains for help. No, he actually did it.

But the answer he got was simple.

-Tell the sect which caused the problem to resolve it.

Of course, the answer, which came back in a long sentence, can be cut through small details; it was this. It meant that those who invaded the Yangtze River and returned in disgrace should handle it.

‘... Actually, that isn’t entirely wrong either.’

From the perspective of the other sects, it seemed as if they were caught off guard, like being struck by lightning out of a clear sky. Would they really travel all the way to Hubei and Anhui to assist Wudang and Namgung in their battles? What would they gain from it?

The war along the Yangtze River had also created a significant rift in the unity of the Nine Great Sects. It was hard to predict how many years it would take to mend this division.

‘How could the situation have deteriorated this badly?’

Observing it all was beyond absurd and shocking.

The ties between the Nine Great Sects had fractured, and the Five Great Families were no longer the same as before.

They were forcibly containing the riots and chaos occurring throughout the Central Plains. Still, if the situation worsened even slightly, chaos would spread like a breached dam.

“Branch leader.”

’What?“

“Is this alright?”

“What?”

“I’m talking about the Central Plains.”

Hong Dae-Kwang frowned at the unpleasant question.

“What are you suddenly talking about after having such an expensive meal?”

“N-No. Isn’t it true? Honestly, I didn’t think much of the Four Precepts Alliance. They are strong, but no matter how strong, if the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families unite, they should be able to defeat them, right?”

“....”

“But that’s not the situation. The Four Precepts Alliance hiding in the south of the river is growing stronger, while the Nine Great Sects are...”

The rest of the story didn’t need to be heard to be understood. It implied that they were divided despite not being united.

The emotional rift had a more significant effect than one might realize. Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t even imagine the Nine Great Sects moving in unison under someone’s command anymore.

“If this continues, the Four Precepts Alliance will suddenly advance on us...”

“...”

Something terrible would happen.

“I really don’t get it. The Four Precepts Alliance hasn’t even moved against us, so how could things have gotten this strange?”

“... it isn’t that they didn’t move and this happened, but because they didn’t move.”

“Uh?”

Hong Dae-Kwang laughed bitterly.

If the Four Precepts Alliance had pushed into the North and started a war after the non aggression treaty, the Nine Sects would have had no choice but to unite. They would have to put out the fire first and talk.

But since Jang Ilso didn’t move and kept hiding, they had time. And that time made emotional rifts happen within the Nine Great Sects.

“... he couldn’t have planned for this, right?”

“Uh?”

“N-nothing.”

Hong Dae-Kwang shook his head like he didn’t even want to think of it.

If Jang Ilso had thought of this and moved, it wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that the entire Central Plains was in his hands.

And if this ridiculous assumption was true...

‘This means that Jang Ilso must have a plan in the head to make the most of this situation.’

Hong Dae-Kwang shook his head quickly and rubbed his ears as if shaking off the thought.

That was too far-fetched.

“So what do we do, branch leader?”

“Kuak.”

Hong Dae-Kwang rubbed his face and said.

“If it happened once, it will happen again. There must be a limit to temporary patches.”

“I think so too.”

“Someone who can help... Ah! Hwa-Young clan! Yes! Contact them! I don’t know about Hubei, but if it is about protecting the civilians, they will surely come!”

“Yes! I will send an immediate letter!”

“Hurry up! You beggars!”

“Yes!”

After the beggars ran out as if their butts were on fire, Hong Dae-Kwang walked around and opened the broken door, and looked up at the sky.

“Shit.”

Up high, he could see the peak of Mount Hua hidden by the clouds.

“I don’t know where you are, but I know where you belong. You damned Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

It was a hundred times easier to deal with that person who caused trouble than this current time. At least, back then, he knew exactly what to do and how to do it.

Even if he didn’t know, that damned bastard would have grabbed him by the collar and dragged him away.

If that guy was there, it wouldn’t have been this frustrating...

“It is taking too long. That damned guy!”

The two years he had expected had passed. Even so, the closed gate of Mount Hua showed no signs of opening.

“Phew...”

Hong Dae-Kwang sighed deeply and turned his gaze far to the south.

‘Please...’

He prayed and prayed that nothing bad would happen before the tightly closed gate opened.

Chapter 862: Even If I Don't Know Where You Are, I Know Where You Belong (2)

“Ohh.”

“Oh my, merchant lord. Don’t be like this; we will carry it.”

“No. The path is too steep. We need the help of at least one more person.”

“Aren’t we normally the people who work?”

“Hahaha. This man. Do I just play around?”

“That isn’t what I am saying, right?”

“It is fine. I feel better this way, so just let me handle it.”

“... if that is the case...”

The porter looked at Hwang Jongi with eyes still clouded with anxiety. But Hwang Jongi had no intention of putting down his pouch and continued walking silently.

‘It is steep.’

He had climbed this mountain several times already, but each time, he gasped anew. The high mountains and rugged cliffs seemed to deny human entry.

The first time he climbed the mountain, he had literally cursed at it. But after climbing it several times, even though it was just as hard, he was happy.

Because there were people he was happy to see at the peak.

But now, Hwang Jongi’s heart was heavier than when he first climbed the mountain.

‘Mount Hua....’

He stared blankly at the peak hidden by the clouds.

Today, it felt even farther away.

“Kuak...”

“We have arrived.”

The workers who reached the top were panting, wiping the sweat from their foreheads.

The number of times they climbed must have been more than a few dozen, but this mountain never got easier, no matter how many times they climbed it.

“Now, now. Let us finish this and take it easy.”

“Yes.”

The workers unpacked their loads from their backs and carefully placed them in front of the gate.

Hwang Jongi silently watched them move their loads.

People could not live without eating just because they were confined to a place. No, because they were confined to a place they needed more food. If they hadn’t been confined, they could have gotten food by going up and down the vast mountains, but if they couldn’t leave the sect, they would have to depend entirely on outside sources for food.

That was why the Eunha merchant union brought food to Mount Hua disciples once a month.

“Phew.”

Hwang Jongi also put down the pouch on his back.

“Thank you for your hard work, lord.”

“You didn’t have to come this far; thank you so much.”

Compared to the amount of baggage he had brought, the pouch he was carrying was nothing special. However, the porters were thrilled by the fact that the famous leader of Eunha was carrying baggage with them and climbed the mountain with them.

After putting down the luggage, Hwang Jongi stood in front of the mountain sect gate without a word.

The gate that was always wide open to receive guests was firmly shut as if it had never been that way.

The signboard that was always clean was now covered in dust.

After watching the scene for a while, Hwang Jongi lightly rubbed the gate. The accumulated white dust had smeared on his fingertips.

“Helper Yu.”

“Yes, lord.”

“Did the people who were moving the baggage ever encounter the disciples of Mount Hua?”

“Yes. As far as I know, no one has ever seen them.”

“... right.”

Hwang Jongi’s lips held a bitter smile.

-“No, it isn’t like we committed a crime and closed the gate. We are just telling people not to come here because it is annoying, so it’s not like we cannot come out, right?”

“... even though you said that.”

He just got annoyed.

“Have you always stored your luggage here?”

“Yes. Since no one climbs the mountain, we leave it in front of the gate so that it is easy to carry in.”

“Right.”

“If there are any items they need, they leave a letter at the Gate when someone comes. Then, we will deliver them on the next visit.”

Hwang Jongi nodded.

Everyone knew this.

‘There are a lot of medicinal herbs and wooden swords.’

Both were troublesome things.

The need for medicinal herbs meant that there were a lot of people getting hurt, and ordering wooden swords indicated that they didn’t want to waste time carving them in this mountain full of trees.

Just by the fact that so many of those two items were being brought into Mount Hua, Hwang Jongi could guess what was going on inside.

“Phew.”

Taking a step back, Hwang Jongi looked at Mount Hua’s sect and the wall next to it.

A Closed Gate was a funny thing.

It was impossible to cut off the world and the sect with this small wall. If you put your mind to it, anyone could cross this wall and meet those inside.

But it was impossible.

What completed the Closed Gate wasn’t that low wall, but respect for the sect that declared the Closed Gate. Crossing that wall would mean an act of ignoring the will of Mount Hua.

That was why even Hwang Jongi, the best merchant in the world, couldn’t cross the wall.

“Maybe...”

“Yes, merchant lord.”

“Do you have a cloth to wipe this off?”

“A cloth? T... that, I didn’t bring anything like that. I do have a cloth to use when carrying the bundle.”

“Give it.”

“Yes.”

Everyone’s faces were filled with doubt, but no one questioned Hwang Jongi’s words. He was too high of a person to ask about the reasons for his actions one by one.

“Here it is, lord.”

A laborer came forward with a cloth and held it out. Hwang Jongi accepted it and nodded.

“Thank you.”

He then folded the cloth neatly and began to clean the Gate of Mount Hua. The Gate, covered in dust, gradually revealed its original color as it was cleaned.

“Lord. We will do it. Please give it to me.”

“No.”

Hwang Jongi shook his head.

“This is something I should do, so don’t worry about it.”

“Oh my, merchant lord. Such a noble...”

“I am not so noble.”

Hwang Jongi smiled broadly and said.

“And nowhere in the world does someone clean a friend’s house. This is just for my own comfort, so please let me.”

“... yes, merchant lord.”

Only then did the people step back.

Hwang Jongi, who had cleaned the main Gate of Mount Hua, then brought the luggage, climbed to the top, and even cleaned the signboard. Sweat formed on his forehead.

After a while, Hwang Jongi came down from his perch and wiped his forehead and looked up at the cleaned signboard.

The workers who came up behind him took a deep breath and looked at Hwang Jongi’s back with a sad expression.

“Young master... no, merchant Lord.”

“Hmm?”

The title of merchant lord still didn’t sit well in his mouth. That’s understandable. How many years had it been since Hwang Jongi was called the young master?“

After Hwang Mun-Yak passed away silently, Hwang Jongi, who took over the Eunha merchants, ran the merchant group very well. It was so great that he, who was watching, was impressed.

But, there were times when Hwang Jongi looked so lonely.

‘It must be so.’

How could he not be lonely when he lost his most trusted father and those he trusted the most after Mount Hua locked themselves into seclusion?

“Should we call them once?”

“Forget it.”

He spoke out of pity, but Hwang Jongi shook his head.

“But merchant lord. This isn’t just your own issue.”

“That is why it is worse.”

“Uh?”

Hwang Jongi sighed.

Actually, the current situation of Eunha wasn’t so smooth. Thanks to Hwang Jongi’s excellent management, there were no major problems on the surface. However, the business that had been expanding vigorously until Hwang Mun-Yak’s death had recently hit a halt.

No, even if Hwang Mun-Yak was alive, it would have been the same. It was a confusing time for all.

“Has there been no contact from the Ghost Clan yet?”

“Yes. Teaching the disciples is their priority...”

The one who took the biggest hit was none other than the Eunha Merchant Union. Even though the treaty was put in place, it applied only to the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families. Who would dare move expensive items into the South of the river, which is ruled by the Evil Faction?

Especially since it was the Ghost Clan, which could be considered a friend of Mount Hua, they had to be even more cautious. In the end, Eunha only transported goods to places other than the South of the River. Recently, however, the Ghost Clan wasn’t so cooperative with them.

The number of messengers sent alternately gradually decreased, so these days, they were short on manpower and couldn’t request more people either.

“I think it is a bit too much.”

“... too much. To them, we are just merchants. Weren’t we just trading from the start?”

“But...”

“If it wasn’t for Mount Hua, we wouldn’t have been able to use them as messengers. We are just going back to our place, so don’t blame them.”

“... Yes.”

Hwang Jongi opened his mouth.

“No answer from the North Sea yet?”

“Yes. They said they would send more when they were ready... but since we delivered the supplies to Mount Hua last year, we haven’t heard from them now.”

“Hmm.”

“Maybe we can send a person there...?”

“Leave it alone. Their hostility towards the Central Plains hasn’t vanished yet. If we rush them, we might be the ones in trouble.”

“... but the one who went to Yunnan to get the tea is also having trouble with the Yunnan people and isn’t able to receive the tea. The three largest sources of funding for the merchant union are all blocked. Merchant Lord, even though we are running it with the money we have earned now, in...”

“I know.”

Hwang Jongi sighed.

At one time, he thought that the existence of Mount Hua gave wings to the merchant union. He had enough ability, but he was unlucky and couldn’t go far until he met a good friend who could walk with him.

But as things turned out this way, he realized that what he thought was his ability was nothing more than the halo of Mount Hua.

‘Father was right.’

He could understand why Hwang Mun-Yak respected and was careful with Mount Hua. Hwang Mun-Yak had seen depths that his son couldn’t.

After experiencing all this, his thoughts about Mount Hua deepened.

Was it because he knew how much benefit Mount Hua provided him?

No.

The fact was he didn’t truly understand the value of Mount Hua, as told by Hwang Mun-Yak. How humbly Mount Hua had treated Eunha until then.

Even though it gave him so much benefit, they never once looked down on them and always held their heads high. As colleagues and friends, they treated him with a smile.

“They say people only realize the worth of others when they are lost.”

“... Merchant Lord.”

Hwang Jongi, who had been silently staring at the gate, shook his head.

“Let us head back. The Taoist said he wouldn’t be in for more than three years, so he will probably open the gate and come out soon. If we don’t want to get scolded for pushing the sect too hard, we cannot afford to rest.”

“... Yes.”

“Let’s go. We have to do everything we can.”

Even as he said that Hwang Jongi kept looking back.

‘Taoist.’

He kept missing it.

The sight of Chung Myung suddenly barging in, kicking the gate open, and yelling at him to bring out alcohol.

In the past, he felt it was rude, but now he knew. He acted like that because he thought of them as friends who had nothing to fear from each other.

‘It wasn’t rude; it was just a lack of formality.’

Now, he fully understood the smile Hwang Mun-Yak would make whenever Chung Myung visited. It was only after he carried all this on his shoulders.

“Look here, helper.”

“Yes, merchant lord.”

“Please get me a few more bottles of alcohol. Good ones.”

“Uh? Didn’t you already prepare more than ten bottles to give to the Taoists when Mount Hua lifted the Gate? They are all rare and exquisite alcohols from the Central Plains...”

“That is the alcohol the Taoist drinks.”

“... then this time...?”

Before answering, Hwang Jongi turned towards the gate, which was now a little far away.

“It is the alcohol that I, sitting in front of him, will drink.”

“....”

“Please.”

“I will do so, merchant lord.”

Hwang Jongi slowly nodded.

‘Taoist.’

The empty space felt vast, but Hwang Jongi didn’t want to rush Chung Myung.

Hwang Jongi knew. Chung Myung, in the past, filled their shortcomings. And now it was time for them to fill Chung Myung’s empty space.

‘Please come out after completing the grand plan. I will prepare plenty of congratulatory drinks.’

Hwang Jongi, who had been staring at the tightly closed Gate for a long time, forced his body, still feeling regretful, to turn around and start down the mountain.

Chapter 863: Even If I Don't Know Where You Are, I Know Where You Belong (3)

“Kill them!”

“Those dirty, Evil bastards!”

A bustling street where pedestrians walked. In the middle of it, people with menacing weapons clashed fiercely.

Clang! Clang!

Weapons collided, and blood splattered in an instant.

“Oh my! What is this!”

“Eik!”

The civilians fled in fear from the sword fights occurring in broad daylight. However, the merchants, caught in the melee, couldn’t escape and leave their goods behind and simply stood, trembling.

They eventually retreated to a corner, curled up, and watched the warriors swing their swords fiercely.

“What is this!”

“This isn’t just a day or two!”

Just a couple of years ago, it would have been unheard of to see warriors fighting in the middle of the streets. Unfortunately, such incidents have now become common occurrences not just in the Southlands but also in the North of the river.

“ACK!”

One of the men fighting fell, bleeding from a wound on his chest.

The merchants tightly closed their eyes at the horrific sight.

It was too difficult for those who had lived their entire lives in peace to witness someone swinging a weapon at another person and see blood splatter.

Thud!

The body of the fallen man landed on a stall. The stall was destroyed in an instant, and fruits were scattered everywhere.

“Oh my!”

To a merchant selling goods, it was like a bolt from the blue, but who wouldn’t know that their own lives were more important than those fruits?

“You fucking bastards!”

“Let us finish this today!”

Those who looked rough at first glance had killing intent in their eyes. Those who faced that fierce force didn’t back down and rushed at them.

But at that very moment.

“Stop!”

Kwaang!

Along with a majestic voice, a dull explosion, like a boulder falling down a cliff, echoed.

Those who had been fighting fiercely stopped for a moment and turned their heads.

Their faces displayed a myriad of expressions when they saw the warriors approaching.

It couldn’t be anything else.

There had to be a few people in the world who could remain at peace after seeing that yellow robe. There were countless temples and monks in the world, but there was just one place where they wore such robes.

Shaolin.

The leader of the Nine Great Sects One Union and the Northern lord of Kangho had appeared.

Step. Step.

Shaolin’s Hae Bang, who had brought the disciples, frowned as he looked at the street.

Soon, a deep sigh escaped his mouth.

If things were as they were, the right thing to say at this moment would be, ‘What is happening?’ but he couldn’t even say that.

This was because he knew exactly how things would turn out if he said it.

“Disciples, listen up.”

“Yes!”

“Suppress all those who are causing trouble and send them to Shaolin!”

“Yes!”

“Great monk!”

One of them shouted out loud when he heard those words. The character for ‘Righteousness’ was engraved on the left of his chest, covered in a white robe stained with dust.

“I am Gwak So-Jong of the Righteous Blood Clan! Those damned bastards of the Evil Faction are causing trouble, so how can you treat us like that without considering the pros and cons of the situation?”

The Righteous Blood Clan was one of the small and medium sects in Hanam. Although it wasn’t a branch of Shaolin, it had deep ties with them from the past.

When Gwak So-Jong, the clan leader of the sect, asked openly, a fatigued look crossed Hae Bang’s face.

“...Clan leader, didn’t you hear the message from the abbot? Those who cause trouble in the land will not be tolerated.”

“Damn it. So you want me to suck my thumb and watch those damned Evil Faction bastards trying to rob the civilians in the place where we exist?”

As Gwak So-Jong protested with anger, Hae Bang let out a deep sigh.

“Weren’t you instructed to inform Shaolin when something like this occurs?”

“How long will it take for you to arrive if we wait for Shaolin? I’ve reported this more than once or twice!”

Hae Bang shook his head.

“The monks of the main temple will determine what is right and wrong. I am merely following the abbot’s orders, so I hope the clan leader understands.”

“Damn it!”

Gawk So-Jong’s face twisted in frustration.

Although Shaolin arrived swiftly and the conflict was brief, there was still some damage. He could see several of his disciples bleeding.

In such circumstances, it wouldn’t be easy to lay down their swords and retreat, especially when they believed they were in the right.

But here, Shaolin’s command was absolute.

“Put down your weapons and retreat. For now, we will cooperate with Shaolin.”

“... Yes.”

The disciples, hearing the order, glanced at Hae Bang with eyes full of anger and discontent before turning away.

For a moment, Hae Bang felt disoriented.

Would Shaolin have faced such looks in Hanam a few years ago?

He gave a brief sigh of disapproval. Now wasn’t the time to be caught up in such feelings. Something might be happening elsewhere at that very moment.

“You should all come to the temple.”

Hae Bang’s gaze shifted to one side.

Unlike the Righteous Blood Clan, who were cooperative despite their discontent, the other group’s gazes were filled with nothing but killing intent.

“This damned monk, you speak so well. Why should we cooperate with you?”

Hae Bang sighed once more.

‘I cannot comprehend the abbot’s intentions with my limited understanding. Why does the abbot leave them be?’

The ones showing intent to fight were from the Evil Faction.

Of course, the Evil Sects were not unfamiliar to Hae Bang. Even in Hanam, where Shaolin held strong influence, the Evil Sect existed.

They should be called back street thugs rather than Evil Sects or Evil Factions. Wherever people lived, there would be those who were evil, and such people formed in the dark corners of the cities.

But these people were different from the Evil Sects that Hae Bang had encountered. Those who fled to the North of the river to escape the Four Precepts Alliance were as rough as demons and didn’t show even the slightest respect for Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects.

“What will you do?”

It was polite but rough. It wasn’t just due to the fatigue from the situation but also because of the irritation and hostility Hae Bang felt towards them.

“Hehe. You seem to know anger despite being a monk. They say thick-skinned people don’t get angry easily.”

“Thick-skinned?”

“Right, I am thick-skinned! You little moron! The reason we are making a mess here is because of you Shaolin! If you hadn’t let go of the reins of the Four Precepts Alliance, we wouldn’t have had to come here escaping those bastards!”

“....”

“If we have come to an unknown land, isn’t it normal to find a way to make a living? Are you telling me to do nothing but starve ourselves to death? Don’t speak nonsense to me! If that were the case, I wouldn’t have risked my life to run here. I would have rather fought and died there than suck my thumb and die!”

It made sense, but it wasn’t worth considering.

It wasn’t like stealing to avoid starvation wasn’t a crime. No, a decent person wouldn’t think of taking other people’s things to live.

Hae Bang, who had been strictly disciplined by Shaolin, couldn’t understand that excuse.

“So, you won’t follow me?”

“Heheh. Didn’t the Shaolin guys have swords stuck in their bodies?”

Hae Bang’s expression turned cold.

“Gong Mu.”

“Yes, sasuk!”

“Subdue them and send them off!”

“Yes!”

As soon as he finished speaking, the Shaolin disciples rushed towards the Evil Sect members, who were wielding weapons. Their faces were fierce, which seemed odd for monks who were supposed to preach mercy.

“These damned monks!”

“I will peel their skins off!”

“Subdue them at once!”

Hae Bang watched the fight break out and cried out in frustration.

“Amitabha...”

He and the Shaolin disciples were running around, sweating, but the chaos around them was only getting worse.

At first, it seemed like the northern sects were running wild, but now not only the Evil Sects in Hanam but even the justice faction sects were becoming extremely sensitive and going against the wishes of Shaolin to cause trouble.

It felt like pouring water into a bottomless pit. He was doing his best to stabilize the land, but the situation was only getting worse.

Hae Bang, who had been feeling concerned, shook his head.

‘Do not think of all that.’

He forced himself to shake off the thoughts that perhaps all of this was happening because the reputation of Shaolin wasn’t what it used to be. Then he sighed and walked away.

No matter how difficult it was, it would eventually end. So until then, there was no other choice but to try and try again.

Clack!

The door opened loudly.

“Big bro!”

The person who opened the door came in frowning. The stench of alcohol made him lose consciousness for a moment.

“Bro! Get up now, big bro!”

The top floor of the most luxurious building in the land, Flower House.

The man who had taken the most expensive room was lying on the large luxurious bed.

“Ah, big bro! I said to get up!”

The man who had been sleeping as if he were dead slowly opened his eyes.

“... ugh, fuck.”

He grabbed his head as if it hurt and asked in a hoarse voice.

“What is it? Right from the morning?”

“Big bro, the Red Horse Clan, who had gone to clean up the Righteous Blood Clan, have been taken away by the Shaolin!”

“...”

“What do we do?”

“Sigh...”

The man reached out and grabbed the bottle of alcohol on the bed. But no matter how much he shook it, nothing came out.

Feeling annoyed, he threw the bottle down and looked at the person who came to report.

“Yaso.”

“Uh?”

“Do you have any money?”

“Why would I have any money? Didn’t big bro take all the remaining emergency funds to come here?”

“... right.”

“If the Red Horse Clan had done their work right, they would have been given a good amount of money by now, but they didn’t. There is no place to make money now, no matter how hard we search! This is why I told you not to enter Hanam!”

“Tsk.”

The man clicked his tongue.

“Stop speaking nonsense, moron. Where is there a good place left in the Northern lands? Should we head to Hubei, where all those people gathered? Or enter the Southern Lands? Or maybe to Sichuan?”

“....”

“It’s the same everywhere.”

The one who came to report sighed at those words and asked.

“... then what do we do? This is why we cannot settle here.”

“Again, with the nonsense.”

The man chuckled.

“This is better. If it seems like we won’t settle down, we can just give up.”

“Uh?”

“Now that we’ve figured it out, let us hit it big.”

“Hit it big?”

The eyes of the man shone.

“It is time to step back now. My back hurts from being hit by Jang Ilso every time...”

“So you plan to steal now?”

“Yes. Steal. Gather everyone you can.”

“How many?”

“Everyone!”

The one who heard it frowned.

“... No. If you say big, those people will gather like clouds. Where can you even lead them? They won’t just follow and end up raiding every place.”

“A big place. We are going to a big place too.”

The man’s eyes turned to the west.

“Wouldn’t we be able to earn enough money to live off it if we raid one of the top ten merchant unions?”

“Top ten merchant unions?”

“Yes. I have a plan. Gather everyone interested in working for me.”

“I-I understand. Big bro!”

Gathering people without a plan wouldn’t be easy. Yet, the person who heard this nodded without hesitation and dashed out.

It was expected.

The one who spoke was none other than the infamous Ghost Blade, Gal Cheon-rip, known for slaying the Seven Killers of the West.

If Gal Cheon-rip, renowned for sparring with Jang Ilso, gave the word, packs of hungry wolves would come clutching their stomachs.

The capital, Luoyang.

Dark clouds were gathering over the sky of Luoyang.

Chapter 864: Even If I Don't Know Where You Are, I Know Where You Belong (4)

Luoyang’s Pleasure Street.

It was now an ancient capital that had lost its former glory, but Luoyang was always Luoyang. The brightly lit night streets attracted countless moths tired of life.

“Ugh... I am drunk.”

“Hehe. You’ve gotten so weak! How much did you even drink?”

“Huh. Still, so far. I can drink until daybreak.”

“Tsk tsk. Such a bluffer with empty pockets... euk! What!”

The drunk man, who had been staggering, fell to the ground with a thud. His shoulder bumped into something passing by.

“This man must be crazy...!”

The man who was about to shout in reflex closed his mouth.

“....”

The person he had bumped into glanced down at him. His face wasn’t particularly fierce. He wasn’t even big.

But the man who fell couldn’t even breathe and could only tremble.

It wasn’t because he was sensitive. Anyone who saw a dozen or so knife marks on the face would react the same way.

“Uh... uh...”

His friend, who had just sobered up, quickly ran over and bowed his head.

“I am sorry! This friend is so drunk...”

“....”

“I-I apologize on his behalf, sir!”

The moment the person staring at the two with cold eyes tried to move his shoulder slightly, the person standing next to him put his hand on his shoulder.

“Don’t waste time here. Let’s move.”

“....”

Then, the man with sword marks on his face licked his lips.

He stared at them, making it impossible to know what he was thinking, and then turned around without a word.

Then, the person who had stopped the man spoke to the drunk people with a chuckle.

“There seems to be a gambling hall in front, so stop by. I think you people are really lucky today.”

“....”

“N-no. Have you used up all your luck in your life? Maybe so. You can survive even after meeting the Bloody Blade Killer.”

The man who had been mumbling to himself soon caught up with the one who was ahead and hurried on.

“...”

The drunk, who had sobered up in an instant, quickly wiped the cold sweat running down his forehead.

“Well... the person’s face...”

“Did you see those eyes?”

“H-he must be a warrior.”

It wasn’t as common as one might think for ordinary people to meet warriors.

“W-why is a warrior in here... uh?”

The person who had been mumbling absentmindedly flinched and shrugged his shoulders. The person who had passed right next to him had a blade hanging from his waist that was so large it made his spine hurt just looking at it.

“...”

It wasn’t just a blade.

The person walking in front had a sharp spear sticking out of his back, and the man with a huge physique, who looked strong at a glance, walked briskly.

“W-what is happening?”

Everyone who passed by had an eerie air about them.

The others sensed it, too, holding their breath and stepping to the side.

“Is there a war happening?”

“Why would all those warriors come here?”

As these men with fierce expressions began to appear around, the common people glanced with curiosity and fear.

But there was someone who was watching this scene with a completely different thought.

‘... Bloody Blade Killer, Iron Ghost, Twirling Spear Ghost, Nine Ghost Swordsmen? What is happening?’

At the sight of the fierce Evil Sect people rushing in, the face of Luoyang Beggars Union branch head, Gwak Byung, turned pale.

If he hadn’t seen this scene with his own eyes, he would have never believed that so many people gathered in one place. Because the Evil Sect’s people were basically more wary of the people’s hate than the Justice Faction.

‘W-we need support now.’

But if he said it the other way around, it meant that something huge enough to gather all those people was happening.

Gwak Byung, who was about to turn in shock, saw something and froze.

“Uh... ah...?”

He could clearly see the face of the man walking.

“So... Soul... Soul Breaking Killer...”

Gwak Byung’s face turned pale.

If the people he had seen so far were the famous figures around the river, then Soul Breaking Killer was the ‘big one’ who couldn’t be compared to them.

The middle-aged man, who looked dangerously thin, walked slowly and slightly turned his gaze to stare at Kwak Byung.

“....”

The moment their eyes met, Gwak Byung’s entire body went cold, as if he had been thrown into an ice cave.

‘H-he noticed...’

But that was all.

Even though he must have recognized that Gwak Byung was from the Beggars Union, Soul Breaking Killer didn’t show any interest and just passed by.

“....”

Gwak Byung’s legs gave out, and he ended up collapsing on the spot.

“B-branch leader!”

“Are you alright?”

The beggars behind him quickly ran over and helped him up.

“.... Just what is happening?”

Instead of answering, Gwak Byung followed the backs of the Evil Sect people with his eyes.

In front of them, at the end of the street, the watchtower of the Flower House towered high.

The top floor of the house was where only the wealthy were accepted, and it wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that one’s status depended on how the floor was decorated.

The top floor of the Flower House was also beautifully decorated with extravagant ornaments, befitting its reputation as the best house in Luoyang.

But.

Usually, the top floor of the place would be bustling with gold and the laughter of women, but now it was filled with dead silence.

“....”

Those sitting on the chairs, which looked expensive at first glance, stared in silence.

It was interesting.

There were at least 20 people seated on the top floor of the Flower House. But no one spoke first, nor did they make eye contact with one another.

They knew.
