Although they were called by the name of Evil Sect, they were not on good terms with one another. How could such people, who swung their swords at one another and wouldn’t hesitate to stab each other in the back to take over this place, be on good terms?

It was even more shocking that there were no fights despite people gathering there.

They were gathered there for a reason, and they were trying their best not to provoke one another to avoid conflict.

But... the silence continued for so long that eventually, someone could no longer endure it.

“Damn it! How long are we waiting for?”

The Green Snake Blade, Jin Ryang, shouted. It had been an hour since they arrived, but the person who called for them was nowhere to be found.

Of course, he became angry about it.

But it seemed that some didn’t think of it like that.

“Don’t make a fuss, kid.”

“... What?”

“I said to shut up before I put this blade in your mouth.”

The veins in the eyes of Jin Ryang began to show.

“Do you really have the ability to do that, Red Monster?”

The one who was called Red Monster snickered.

“It isn’t easy for me to deal with the Green Snake Blade alone.”

“You know it well.”

“But there must be around 10 people here who want to kill you. So know your place and talk.”

“This fucking dog...”

Green Snake Blade was about to lose it.

“Shut up.”

“...”

Green Snake Blade closed his mouth.

His gaze fell on the person sitting at the back, Soul Breaking Killer. Seeing his expressionless face that couldn’t guess his intentions, Green Snake Blade sat back down without saying a word, red-faced.

Even though he was notorious enough to make kids stop crying just by his name, he couldn’t dare to go against the feelings of this man.

The strong were respected in Kangho.

And that respect for the strong was even more observed in the Evil Sects.

“But....”

At that moment, Soul Breaking Killer opened his mouth in annoyance.

“It is true that this is boring. That person doesn’t treat his guests well.”

“If we were guests, he would have treated us well.”

At that moment, the door behind them opened wide, and seven men walked in. Everyone’s eyes were drawn to them.

‘Those people...’

“...as expected.”

The world was wide and vast.

Not everyone famous knew each other. Even if they knew of their titles and their fame.

And today, those who saw the Seven Killers of Jiangxi nodded their heads in realization.

Iron Ghost’s lips twisted.

“The rumors were no exaggeration.”

It was so exciting that his fingers tingled.

The Seven Killers of Jiangxi, the seven sworn brothers, were the messengers of Jiangxi.

Each and every one of them was capable of making a name for themselves.

This was why they had no choice but to face each other. Since two tigers could not live on one mountain, if seven tigers lived on one mountain, it was natural that hell would unfold.

But instead of fighting and killing each other, the Seven Killers of Jiangxi became sworn brothers.

Although there were just seven of them, it was a moment when seven strong individuals, untouchable by any power in the world, were born.

Especially...

“Is this the killer? The ruthless Gal Cheon-rip?”

The moment they saw the person walking in the lead with a lazy look, they felt their entire body’s muscles tense up. Their bodies instinctively knew that this man was dangerous and warned them.

Step. Step.

Gal Cheon-rip, who had been swaying ahead, turned and grinned at everyone.

“Welcome, trash. No... should I call you the bastards who ran without speaking out?”

“This...”

Everyone’s faces hardened at the sudden insult.

“I didn’t know that spitting at others while resting is your hobby.”

When Iron Ghost spoke, Gal Cheon-rip laughed out loud.

“Right. Let us greet you now. I am Gal Cheon-rip, the first of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi.”

Those who had linked arms nodded.

Everyone already guessed who he was without him speaking. First, his presence seemed different.

“Let us get to the point.”

Gal Cheon-rip opened his mouth with no intention of being polite.

“The fact that those people who live off their pride have come all this way with heavy steps means they are starving. You ran to the North to avoid the wolves, but this is where the tigers live.”

Everyone here moved because they didn’t want to live under Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso offered death or surrender as the only options.

But there was no paradise where they moved to.

It wasn’t as bloody as the river, but the North was an even harder place to breathe in. Every time they tried to do something, the damn Shaolin, Tang family, and Beggars Union would come running.

No one spoke, but this was a disgusting reality for them.

That was why they responded to his call. If the situation hadn’t been like this, these people would have never gathered in one place.

They came here because they hated doing so in the first place.

“Stop with the nonsense and speak. Why did you call us?”

Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes lit up at the man’s words.

“If the Soul Breaking Killer of the world asks, I should answer. The reason I called you is simple. I am out of money.”

At those words, laughter flowed around.

“So? Are you asking me to do you a favor now?”

“I am a beggar, but you are all beggars too. I cannot accept favors from beggars. In that case....”

Qi shone in the eyes of Gal Cheon-rip.

“It would be better to kill and take it.”

At that cold voice, even the air in the room began to turn cold.

Chapter 865: Even If I Don't Know Where You Are, I Know Where You Belong (5)

“Kill and rob.”

It wasn’t a particularly great phase.

That was obvious to those who called themselves Evil.

It was a phrase used by those who rejected the rites and laws that bound people.

If there was something they needed, they’d get it, even if it meant stealing. If there was something they wanted, they would take it, even if it meant killing. Those who found that uncomfortable probably didn’t belong to Evil.

But no one took these words from Gal Cheon-rip’s mouth lightly.

“Are you speaking about here, in the north?”

“Yes.”

“...easier said than done!”

Negative reactions erupted around the room.

The reason why these reactions came from those who had no aversion to killing and taking was simple.

The Northern lands they were treading on were ruled by the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.

If it had been just a few years ago, they would have been less concerned. Even if the force of the two factions was that great if they started a fight and the problem got bigger, they would just cross the Yangtze River and head south.

The world was vast.

That was why even if they were chased by the Nine Great Sects, they could not chase them to the end of the land.

But now, they were in a state where they could not set foot back into their land. The moment they set foot there, they would die by the blade of Jang Ilso.

“We are not doing this because we are happy. The moment we act as we want to, those bastards will come running to us with anger in their eyes. How are you going to kill them and take their things away?”

“You are not saying you want to fight the Nine Sects, right?”

“Don’t forget this is the north.”

“...we came here for nothing. We didn’t come here to listen to such nonsense.”

Despite the voices of criticism from around, Gal Cheon-rip snickered. The soft laughter drove everyone back into silence.

“...what is so funny?”

The First Killer stopped laughing and opened his mouth at the question from Soul Breaking Killer.

“It is funny. Of course it is.”

He shrugged his shoulders and twisted his lips into a smile.

“How could it not be funny to see those starving to death drooling while a feast is placed right in front of them?”

In an instant, a serious killing intent surged through the quiet room.

Everyone in the room glared at Gal Cheon-rip with eyes that seemed like they wanted to devour him. Then, the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, guarding Gal Cheon-rip, bared their teeth and stepped forward.

“Not even knowing your...”

At that moment, Gal Cheon-rip lifted his hand and stopped them.

“Quiet.”

“... yes, big bro.”

Then he narrowed his eyes and asked.

“Did I say something wrong?”

“....”

“You must have held your breath because you couldn’t do anything right away. Those who saw blood on their land are more vicious than those who had their money stolen. So you must have thought to wait and see. But... did anything get better?”

No one spoke.

It had been 2 years.

They had been sucking their thumbs for 2 years now without doing anything. But everyone felt that even that had reached its limit now.

“In the end, it is obvious what fate you will face: either hold on and hold on until you die of hunger. Or... in the end, you cannot hold on and do something yourself or get caught by someone and get stabbed in the back.”

Severing the muscles in their limbs and cutting off their pulse.

A punishment that destroyed a warrior’s life by having their limbs cut. If the dantian was destroyed, one could not use their internal qi, but they could still use their martial arts learned through the body. But those who had been hit on their blood points could not use their hands and feet properly, so they could not even live normal lives.

The Nine Sects imposed that punishment on the Evil Sect who committed terrible crimes.

The faces of those who heard this were filled with displeasure. However, the reason no one objected greatly was because they also intuitively felt that the obvious future awaited them.

“So what are you going to do? Stop whining and tell.”

“I told you.”

Gal Cheon-rip sneered.

“Even if we gather in small groups, we will only be robbing small ones. If we are going to rob, we should choose a good place. A place where everyone can live without concern for the rest of their lives.”

“What about the catch?”

“If you have money, there is nothing to worry about. And even if it was in the past, they cannot chase you for long now. Just go to Xinjiang or Cheonghae, and when things calm down, we move back with fake identities.”

“Hmmm.”

Different thoughts unfolded in the minds of those who heard that.

‘They won’t choose us anyway.’

‘We’re moving to Xinjiang.’

‘We cannot live up to the reputation of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi.’

Those who briefly joined hands tended to disperse when the work was done. No matter how fast the pursuers chased, they wouldn’t be able to catch everyone who participated in the crime.

In that case, they had no choice but to give priority to those with great reputations. And the most famous people there were the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, no matter what anyone else thought.

“Not bad.”

“Definitely. I am sick of just sucking on my thumb like this.”

“Damn it. It isn’t the money! Those Nine Sects are openly looking down on us. How long should I keep my mouth shut?”

“... it’s like this now, and if the Wudang and Southern Edge open the gates....”

There was a reason why the Evil Sects gathered in the Southern land in the first place. It was because it was too difficult to do business in the north, where many of the Nine Sects were located.

The sects guarding the center of the north were Shaolin, Wudang, Beggars Union, Southern Edge, and Namgung.

Even though two of the five sects had shut their gates, the pressure made it difficult to breathe. If that was the case, wouldn’t it be obvious what would happen if those two sects lifted their Closed Gates?

“So?”

Gal Cheon-rip raised his head.

The Soul Breaking Killer was staring at him coldly with arms crossed.

“Where are you planning to raid with all these people gathered here? It shouldn’t be an ordinary place.”

“Of course not. I am going to raid one of the ten sects of the Central Plains.”

“Ten sects?”

“Are you even in your right mind?”

When the words “ten sects” came out, everyone fell silent.

“This is nonsense.”

The Soul Breaking Killer also shook his head.

“If it is a place like that, it has to be related to the Nine Great Sects or Five Great Families. The Nine Sects who robbed us of our lives will come chasing after us if we touch anything.”

“You don’t have to worry about that. The place I am going to rob does not have much chance of being tracked.”

“Hmm?”

“There is no guard for them, and there is just one place where the warehouse is thicker than anywhere else.”

“... Where is that?”

“Eunha Merchant Union.”

The people gathered frowned and whispered at Gal Cheon-rip’s words.

“Eunha Merchant Union?”

“... Such a place exists?”

“Ah. If it is the Eunha Express Delivery, then is it easy to understand?”

“... You are going to rob that?”

“In recent times, it has risen to the top ten in merchant unions in the Central Plains. But that doesn’t mean it is at the bottom, either. It is a place with money swelling up.”

“How could there not be an allied sect with such a place?”

“Of course there is. But not now. The ally is Mount Hua.”

“...”

Those who finally understood the situation glared.

“... Mount Hua, who was the flame, is their ally.”

“An interesting story.”

“And...”

At that moment, Iron Ghost, who had been listening silently, spoke.

“Now, the Shanxi sects are under Closed Gates.”

“... and the place is close to Cheonghae. If we leave Shanxi, we will be safe.”

Those who had guessed the situation roughly laughed, unable to hide their joy.

“There might be escorts and guards, but even they will be a bunch of idiots.”

“A small shop with no owner is no different from a treasure trove with the door wide open.”

“There is no owner?”

But the Soul Breaking Killer still seemed unconvinced.

“You seem to be mistaken. Do you think being in Closed Gate means being locked in? Even if they live in seclusion, the moment a fire starts in the front yard, they will come to put it out.”

“Ah. I know. Of course, they will open the gates.”

Gal Cheon-rip nodded calmly.

“Hmm?”

“But... isn’t that the same even if they do it? Surely...”

He looked at everyone in the room.

“You are not trying to say that these many people cannot handle Mount Hua, are you?”

Everyone who heard that laughed softly.

Not all of the people gathered there.

Of course, there were some who acted alone, but about half of them were leading small troops. If they could gather all of it, dealing with a decent sect was no problem.

“It would be burdensome if it was of the Nine Great Sects level... but Mount Hua, who doesn’t even belong there, should be manageable.”

“Don’t look down on them; Mount Hua leads the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“He is the leader in name. Is there even one moron who thinks Mount Hua is stronger than the Sichuan Tang family?”

Mount Hua was a prestigious sect in the past, and a few years ago, it was at the center of a world-shaking incident and gained fame.

But, objectively speaking, Mount Hua was nothing more than a small-scale sect that was nowhere near the level of the Nine Great Sects or Five Great Families. Fame and skill weren’t always the same.

Mount Hua’s Swordsmen, whose name faded long ago, gained fame by fighting Jang Ilso, the leader of the Four Precepts Alliance. To be blunt, that was only Mount Hua Sword Saint’s achievement and not Mount Hua’s.

“Even so, it is clear that they are members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, right?”

“There is no reason to worry about the Heavenly Friends Alliance. The main force of the alliance, the Tang family, is tied to the Yangtze River, and there is no need to care about their pursuits. The rest of the northern bastards are far from the Central Plains.”

Those who had roughly finished calculating looked at each other with meaningful glances.

“So, if we can just handle Mount Hua, we don’t have to worry about anything else.”

Gal Cheon-rip nodded vigorously.

“It seems like everyone understands now.”

“Is it really alright?”

“If you are scared, get out now.”

“....”

Those gathered looked at one another. It was definitely a tempting story. But there was something on their minds.

One of those watching raised his hand.

“I am out.”

Gal Cheon-rip looked at the person who raised his hand with a smile.

“I have never heard of the Shadowless Mad Killer being a coward.”

“I am not saying this out of fear. Even if I escape here, I will still have to return to the Central Plains. I don’t want to live my entire life being chased.”

“Right. It isn’t a bad choice. But...”

At that moment, a sword was pulled out from Gal Cheon-rip’s waist like a ray of light.

Crack!

At the same time, Shadowless Mad Killer’s arm, which had been raised, was cleanly cut off and shot into the air.

’ACKKKKK!“

A desperate scream came from his mouth as he grabbed the severed arm and fell from the chair to the ground.

Step. Step.

Gal Cheon-rip approached him with a calm look and trampled on his face.

“K-Kuak...”

“This is what you call a coward. And I hate cowards.”

“E-enough...”

Slash!

Shadowless Mad Killer’s body, whose head had been severed in an instant, fell limply to the ground.

“....”

Everyone remained silent as if bound by an unspoken agreement.

If it was the Shadowless Mad Killer, most of the people there were strong enough to not be able to guarantee victory. Such a person died like a fly in this fight without even fighting back.

‘Ghost Blade.’

This scene proved why he was the first among the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, and the title of Ghost Blade Killer made the world tremble.

“Tsk.”

Gal Cheon-rip, who had wiped the blood from his body, looked around at the others.

“Anyone else wants out?”

No answer came back.

Even if Gal Cheon-rip hadn’t done anything, the people there would have said something. And since they had witnessed this now, they wouldn’t speak back.

“There is no one wanting out.”

The Soul Breaking Killer stared at Gal Cheon-rip with arms crossed.

“But you should be prepared for one thing. If you gather this many people and move, one shouldn’t expect control. Hell will unfold now.”

“Nice. Very nice.”

Gal Cheon-rip smiled, showing his teeth.

“In the name of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, I permit it. Go wild as you please. Anyway, I will be the one handling the aftermath.”

“If you say so.”

The moment the Soul Breaking Killer nodded, everything was decided.

“The nine Sects must have heard through the Beggars that we are here. We don’t have the resources to stop them, but there is no point in wasting time.”

“Then when are you going to move?”

“Tomorrow.”

“... in one day?”

“Why? Is there any reason not to?”

The Bloody Blade Killer slowly licked his lips after hearing those words.

“Of course. I was already bored to death now.”

“Not just you. Bring everyone who is interested. I will do it in a flashy way.”

The wolves, who had been enduring hunger for a long time, smiled bitterly at the smell of blood wafting from them.

Chapter 866: This Is The Land Of Mount Hua (1)

“Will it be alright, big bro?”

“What will?”

After the gathered people left, Dam Hae, the youngest of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, spoke.

“It seems like things are turning into a big scale.”

“Youngest one.”

“Yes, big bro.”

“If things are small, it would be a problem.”

Gal Cheon-rip smiled brightly.

“People cannot stand small troubles. When a house catches on fire, the entire neighborhood will rush out. Even if you don’t like the owner of the house, it is their habit to run to the well first.”

“Yes.”

“But when there is a fire spreading to the mountain and starts to burn it all, the same humans will sit back and watch it burn. When you see something you cannot help, you cannot even think of taking a bucket of water to pour on it.”

Dam Hae nodded as if it made sense.

“But I am still worried. Aren’t they on the same side? Shaanxi and Hunan aren’t far, and if any of the Nine Sects come running...”

“That won’t happen.”

“Uh?”

Gal Cheon-rip laughed bitterly.

“The reason the Nine Sects are acting so violently is because they are really losing it. Since their face is covered with filth, they have no choice but to hide from others and pretend like nothing happened.”

“Yes, big bro.”

“But that doesn’t mean that their shame will vanish. Those who have been shamed want one of two things. Either to find a chance to do something to wash off the shame or...”

The second of the seven, Go Song, snickered like he knew what it was.

“Watch the others suffer the same fate.”

“Right.”

Gal Cheon-rip slowly nodded.

“Do you think they will try to stop Mount Hua from being damaged? They will look sorry on the outside, but on the inside, they will be happy.”

“... but they are called the Justice faction.”

“Tsk tsk.”

Gal Cheon-rip clicked his tongue at those words.

“No matter what you say about those Justice factions, they are still people. It’s not like they’re born reciting chants and prayers. Their stomachs feel hunger, and they are no different from us.”

At his words, Dam Hae silently nodded.

Actually, he didn’t completely understand, but since Gal Cheon-rip was the scariest man among them, there was no good in making them angry.

Despite being sworn brothers, Gal Cheon-rip was definitely the scariest among them.

“But big bro,”

At that moment, Go Song spoke up, sounding displeased.

“It’s good to make a big deal out of it, but are you really going to make them go there?”

“Of course.”

“... Are you serious?”

“Am I not? It is better to have a solid plan. But...!”

Yeom Wang-bu looked at Gal Cheon-rip with eyes of anticipation.

“If they are the ones who say they will follow us even after all this is over.”

Gal Cheon-rip smiled at those words, while Go Song looked doubtful.

“Follow? Are you really thinking of handling them in the future?”

“Yes.”

“Haven’t we been living well without too many subordinates? But why now...?”

“Living well?”

At that moment, Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes turned red.

“Are we living so well that we had to come to the north to waste our time now?”

“....”

As a deep killing intent rose from his body, Go Song and Dam Hae turned pale.

“While that damned Jang Ilso became the lord of the southern land, I ended up as scum in the north. What am I lacking compared to him that I had to end up like this?”

“... Brother.”

Gal Cheon-rip gritted his teeth.

“This was all due to the difference in strength. I can match Jang Ilso, but we cannot fight the Ten Thousand People Clan, not to mention the Four Precepts Alliance.”

Go Song nodded.

“That is definitely true. If we had strength, we wouldn’t be persecuted by the other Nine Sects either.”

“We wouldn’t have had to run away from our land either.”

It wasn’t that the Evil Sects lacked pride. No, compared to the Justice Sects, which could bend their pride for a cause, the Evil Sects didn’t need to compromise their pride.

“I thought if we had power, we would live freely. I didn’t think it was necessary to bother managing subordinates and growing my strength. But... I was wrong. He was right. I am going to bring them all under my wing.”

“But those people...”

Just as Go Song was about to say something inappropriate, Dam Hae intervened.

“You are thinking of making things worse.”

“Hmmm?”

Gal Cheon-rip looked at Dam Hae with amusement.

“Why do you think so?”

“If we just steal a little money and run, they will disperse and avoid being tracked. But if we do something big that must be resolved, we will need a lot to evade the pursuers who will chase us to the ends of the earth.”

“Hahaha. As expected of the youngest. Right.”

Gal Cheon-rip burst into laughter.

“First, I will raid Eunha for money. And instead of running away, I will turn Shaanxi into a wasteland.”

“Shaanxi...”

“Then Southern Edge will eventually open their gates. Southern Edge is persistent, so they will try to find everyone and take them down. Then we will block their attempts.”

“If we were scattered, we would lose the pursuit, but even if we go against Southern Edge, we wouldn’t be able to bring all our forces down to Qinghai. The Four Precepts Alliance made sure of that.”

“Right. That damned Jang Ilso is stopping it.”

Gal Cheon-rip’s face contorted. Just mentioning the name Jang Ilso was frustrating to him.

“Well, we just have to subjugate those who have no way to return to the Central Plains. And....”

He paused for a moment and spoke with his teeth bared.

“We will wait for the Justice faction and Four Precepts Alliance to fight one another before we return to the Southern lands again. This is what they mean by returning home in glory.”

Go Song looked at Gal Cheon-rip with admiration. He had thought that his leader was only interested in gold and money, but it seemed he had a well-thought-out plan for the future.

“But they must have some idea...”

“They will be on guard. And they will also move according to plan. But...”

Gal Cheon-rip chuckled.

“Even so, it will only last for a moment. How long can a dog wait when the food is in front of its nose? There is no need to force anything. If we step ahead and start cutting down a couple of them, they will get even more excited and start running wild. By the time they come to their senses, everything will have long passed.”

“Well...”

“That is the kind of people the Evil Sects are made of.”

Dam Hae frowned.

“But big bro... will those who didn’t even listen to Jang Ilso’s words listen to us? It’s not like we are below the Ten Thousand People clan, but these people don’t listen to anyone at all.”

“You can leave that to me. I am without mercy.”

“Yes, brother.”

And now the tuning was done.

“How many years has it been since we were called the Seven Killers of Jiangxi?”

“At least 15 years now.”

“Yes. Right. It is time for that name to change; it is boring. Instead of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, we need to be called the Seven Killers of Qinghai so that it looks good when we return home.”

Gal Cheon-rip got up from his place and walked to the window.

He looked down at the peaceful streets of Luoyang and smiled with twisted lips.

“If the world is burning, we should throw in at least one spark. Let us make it clear that the world isn’t just for the Four Sects alliance and the other Evil Faction.”

The next morning.

Outside the city gates of Luoyang, the Iron Ghost appeared in the deserted lands.

He looked around quietly and frowned.

There wasn’t a single person around besides him. He tried to see if anyone was hiding, but it was the same.

Iron Ghost, who kept looking anxiously, soon gritted his teeth.

“... damn it, was it all just words then?”

Even though they came out a little early, he hadn’t expected to see this sight.

Iron Ghost, who had momentarily pondered whether he had come to the wrong place, shook his head.

“Damn it. Seven Killers of Jiangxi, my ass.”

Just as he was about to turn around, thinking he had wasted his time.

“Hmmm?”

He noticed a few people walking from afar.

“... maybe they aren’t all cowards?”

But at this level, it would be difficult to even rob a small merchant, let alone Eunha...

But Iron Ghost, who was cursing, slowly became shocked.

One. And one more.

Against the rising sun as the backdrop, countless people began to appear.

At first, there weren’t many, but as time passed, more and more gathered. The killing intent they exuded was terrifying to him.

‘Isn’t this more than I expected?’

He thought there would be, at best, around 50 people who would gather...

“Phew...”

At that moment, someone whistled next to Iron Ghost. Iron Ghost, startled, turned to see Gal Cheon-rip, who was smiling.

“A lot more than you expected had gathered?”

“... I didn’t think there would be so many, big bro.”

“Right?”

Not only that, but the Seven Killers had also gathered and approached the people who were coming.

“Fortunately, the name doesn’t seem too shabby now, right, Iron Ghost?”

“...”

Cold sweat formed on Iron Ghost’s forehead.

Who didn’t know how fearsome this man was? Just being close enough made his spine shiver.

Fortunately, however, Gal Cheon-rip didn’t seem interested in him any longer and only cared for the approaching people.

It seemed like he had underestimated the Evil Sect people. Judging by the number of those who gathered wanting to spill blood, it was clear.

“Sigh, crazy people.”

In other words, this meant that there were many Evil Sects who were suffering under the pressure of the Nine Great Sects.

Dam Hae shrugged his shoulders.

“Since so many people have gathered from the start, I think there are still some who haven’t arrived yet.”

“It doesn’t matter. They will join us soon.”

The direction had already been set, so there was no need to wait for everyone. If they wanted to achieve it, they would move right away without letting the momentum die down.

The gathered people glared at Gal Cheon-rip.

Gal Cheon-rip nodded, appreciating the ominous atmosphere.

“Welcome, trash.”

He paused, considering if there was anything more to say. Now was the time to show with action rather than a hundred words.

“Let us go right away.”

A sinister voice escaped his lips.

“Let us head over to Shaanxi and turn it into hell. We will give you whatever you want, be it blood or money!”

At those words, everyone smiled, baring their teeth. They seemed unable to contain their excitement for what was about to happen.

“Let us go.”

A pack of hungry wolves began to run towards Shaanxi in unison.

Towards Shaanxi, to the west of Luoyang, where Mount Hua was.

Chapter 867: This Is The Land Of Mount Hua. (2)

“Ugh. We don’t even have time to rest.”

Hong Dae-Kwang let out a deep exhale.

He thought life would be a little easier with Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, who caused trouble everywhere, being sealed behind the Closed Gate, but that was a huge misunderstanding.

As the huge storm raging in the center vanished, small gusts of wind kept coming around. Each one was not a great threat, but it seemed like even ten people wouldn’t be enough to handle them.

Hong Dae-Kwang glanced around.

‘No one?’

Of course, there was no one else there now. The place was falling apart, but after all, this was his branch.

Nevertheless, he kept looking to the left and right as if something bothered him. He took out a bottle from under the desk, which looked like it would collapse.

Puk.

He uncorked the bottle and sniffed it.

“Ugh... I am dying.”

Because he was so busy, he didn’t even have time to leisurely have a drink.

‘When was the last time I even smelled alcohol?’

It must have been at least a month now. Since all the beggars below him were wide-eyed, he could tell they were taking time to drink. But they couldn’t openly say they wanted a drink.

So even at times like this...

Just as Hong Dae-Kwang was drooling and about to sip from the bottle.

Kwang!

“ACKKK!”

Startled by the sound of the door opening, Hong Dae-Kwang threw the alcohol over his head.

“Branch leader!”

The beggar who rushed in without much attention gaped at the alcohol flowing down Hong Dae-Kwang’s head.

“NO, what...”

“...T-that...”

The beggar, who had been staring at Hong Dae-Kwang’s appearance with blurred eyes, flinched and quickly approached him.

“It isn’t important now.”

“Are you angry?”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who thought they would be angry at him for fooling around during work, was shocked.

“We have secret information from the sect!”

“What? From the main sect?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s petty concerns were blown away in an instant.

A secret message was an emergency call from the main sect, delivered by a hawk. In recent years, despite numerous incidents, there hadn’t been a case where the main sect needed to send secret information.

“Now? Did it arrive now?!”

“Yes, branch leader! Just now!”

Sensing that something significant had happened, Hong Dae-Kwang leaped off the desk and snatched the red letter from the beggar’s hand.

He quickly broke the seal and read it.

“...this... this is?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes widened in shock, and his mouth stuttered.

“Iron Ghost? The Ghost of Mount He? Nine Swordsmen? Red Killing Clan? Nine... Nine Spirit Clan? Wait? Soul Breaking Killer? T-this damned! Him too?”

And that wasn’t all.

“S-s-s... Seven Killers of Jiangxi?!“

All the blood drained from Hong Dae-Kwang’s face.

Each name was associated with powerful and terrifying figures. But all these people gathered in one place and moving together?

“Why? Just where?”

The direction of their movement wasn’t in the letter.

Just as Hong Dae-Kwang was lost in thought and about to shout, another beggar rushed in.

“B-branch leader! Another emergency letter!”

“Bring it here, right now!”

Hong Dae-Kwang snatched the newly arrived letter and opened it.

“Ugh...”

The moment he saw the concise words inside, he forgot to breathe.

-To Shaanxi.

“...S-Shaanxi?”

“T-they are coming to Shaanxi?”

“Why?”

The beggars’ faces also began to turn pale.

“T... this is insane...”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s fingers, holding the red letter, trembled.

He had heard each and every name. It was impossible for anyone handling information not to know they had moved north to escape Jang Ilso.

But the moment he saw all the names on one paper, Hong Dae-Kwang felt fear rise in his bones, realizing how careless he had been until then.

If they were scattered around, they wouldn’t be worth worrying over. No matter how strong each one was individually, it would be difficult for them to make much difference.

But if they all came together in one place?

‘They could easily take down any single sect.’

Considering the violent nature of the Evil Sects, the damage they could do would be greater.

“W-why are these fucking morons stepping into Shaanxi to make trouble?”

Hong Dae-Kwang shouted as if he was jumping up from his place in anger.

Their intentions weren’t clear yet, but given the situation, even a 3-year-old could figure out their purpose.

“Reinforcement? What happened to the reinforcement? Request reinforcement from the main sect right now!”

“B-branch leader! The main sect has...”

“What the fuck is it! Does this seem like a normal situation? Shouldn’t we put aside everything and come here to help! Request the reinforcement right now! Right now!”

“Yes!”

When Hong Dae-Kwang shouted, a beggar who had been lost in thought ran out.

Even at that moment, Hong Dae-Kwang’s head spun.

‘Where? Where are they aiming? It cannot be the entirety of Shaanxi?’

Hong Dae-Kwang’s blunt fingers moved across the desk, not knowing where to point.

“What the hell can it be...”

The beggar who had run out then came back in with a paler face.

“B-branch leader! More information came.”

“What?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes trembled. He had never heard of emergency letters coming up in this count over his time.

“B-bring it!”

Hong Dae-Kwang hurriedly opened the letter he received.

“...”

This time, it wasn’t like a letter.

A map.

A clear red line was drawn on the small map that briefly depicted the appearance of Central Plains.

The ominous line, which started from Luoyang, spread to Shanxi. As he followed it, the line suddenly broke in the middle, but Hong Dae-Kwang’s gaze moved to the line moving ahead and then left.

The moment he finally saw the name of the city, he groaned helplessly.

“... Xi’an.”

Cold sweat ran down his forehead.

Perhaps this news was natural.

What they were after was so obvious. Would a group of so many morons come to Shaanxi for any other reason? Whether their goal was wealth or chaos, their target would be Xi’an, the largest city in Shaanxi.

“N-No.”

If both Southern Edge and Mount Hua were moving openly, those Evil Sects people would never dare to do this. Xi’an was a city to the south and east of Southern Edge and Mount Hua.

But both of those sects were now under Closed Gates. In other words, the current Shaanxi was a no man’s land without a sect looking after it.

What if they invaded Xi’an and committed a wrong during this time?

“Why! Why these crazy bastards! Just why!”

Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t hold back his curses.

“B-branch leader! What do we do?”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang clenched his fists, stared into the air, gritted his teeth, and said.

“Request support from the main sect! And request support from Shaolin right now! Right now!”

“Without going through the main sect’s permission?”

“There is no time for that! Send a message to Shaolin right away requesting support from the main sect!”

“Yes!”

“Aso!”

“Yes!”

“Run to Southern Edge right now! Tell those guys to release their Closed Gates and come out! Right now!”

“But the Closed Gate...!”

“Now isn’t the time to be discussing this! Go and knock on the damn gate!”

“Yes!”

“Oh, Ming!”

“Yes!”

“Go to the Hwa-young clan in Xi’an right now! Tell them the situation and unite the many sects in Xi’an! These people are different from the Ten Thousand People clan who invaded us before! Not a single one of them should be setting foot in Xi’an!”

“Understood!”

“You, send a letter to the sects around Xi’an right now! Tell them all to gather here! We need the support of all the sects and clans in Xi’an!”

“Yes!”

“Move! Right now!”

“Yes!”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit down on his lip until it bled.

He wanted to run out and start moving immediately, but he had to contact the nearby sects first. Without Mount Hua and Southern Edge, the sects in Xi’an wouldn’t be able to stop those bastards, so the support of Shaolin and the Beggars Union was essential.

‘Please, please!’

Actually, it was all too late.

The distance from Luoyang to there was just 500 li, a distance that even most non-warriors could cover in half a day. Even if Shaolin moved, they wouldn’t reach Xi’an before them.

That meant they would have to hold them off with their current strength and buy time until Shaolin arrived.

‘Please hurry up!’

Hong Dae-Kwang got up and ran out.

He opened the door and went out, tilting his head up. Mount Hua came into his view, the peak covered by the clouds.

After watching the sight for a while, he bit his lip and quickly turned around again.

A blood-streaked flame was engulfing everything around.

It wasn’t a common sight to see villages burning down, even if the people didn’t care about right or wrong.

The sight of black smoke rising from the red flames into the sky was a scene of hell unfolding on land.

Black smoke, red flames.

And...

The ground soaked with blood that was thicker than the flames.

“Ughh...”

The person lying in the pool of blood convulsed, forcefully raising their trembling head.

“Y... you will receive... divine punishment...”

“Tsk tsk.”

Gal Cheon-rip snickered as he looked at the old man glaring at him with eyes full of resentment.

“We have already received the punishment, old man. That is why we are in such a place.”

“...M-Mount Hua will not...”

Puak!

Gal Cheon-rip’s sword pierced the old man’s body and sank into the ground.

The old man, who was trembling, fell to his death. Gal Cheon-rip, who pulled out the sword, mumbled with a displeased face.

“These people in this town are always talking about Mount Hua. They all say, Mount Hua, Mount Hua.”

The strange thing was that the name they spoke of was Mount Hua and not Southern Edge.

‘Odd.’

No matter how much Mount Hua’s fame had grown, Shaanxi was still a land ruled by Southern Edge. Why did these people say Mount Hua and not Southern Edge, then?

“Well, anything is fine.”

Gal Cheon-rip raised his head and looked at the burning village.

He also clearly saw the Evil Sect’s people standing in front of him, exuding a deep killing intent.

The moment they saw the blood soaking their hands, a helpless smile appeared brightly on Gal Cheon-rip’s lips.

‘It is slowly getting dangerous.’

He slowly licked his lips with his tongue. The deep killing intent everyone was exuding made him shiver.

Their vicious nature, which had been long suppressed, was gradually bursting out.

Just as a drinker who had been held back from alcohol for a long time drank until he became unconscious the next day, these people, who had refrained from killing for a while, had tasted blood, so no one could stop them.

“Big bro.”

“I know. Let’s move.”

Gal Cheon-rip’s gaze turned to the west.

“We clean up a few more villages before we reach Shaanxi.”

By then, a true frenzied army would be formed.

An army that would drench that great city in blood.

Chapter 868: This Is The Land Of Mount Hua. (3)

“You damned bastards!”

A thunderous roar erupted.

The leader of the small and medium clans, Rampant Tiger Clan, located in Sanmenxia, Jo Myung-San, looked at the scene before him with a horribly distorted expression.

He had come running without looking back when he heard that the Evil bastards had come to Xifan village and were slaughtering civilians. The moment he saw the burning village and the civilians lying miserably, a fire like a terrifying flame ignited in his heart.

“How...”

The helpless civilians, regardless of age or gender, were dying miserably. Hot tears flowed down Jo Myung-San’s face.

“How could they do such a thing while being humans! Even if I tear them apart, it won’t be enough!”

The Rampant Tiger Clan was a small clan in the region and had never harmed normal people. They believed the only reason they learned martial arts was to protect and care for those weaker than themselves.

To their eyes, the sight of innocent people dying in such a brutal manner was a shock beyond comparison.

“This...”

“Worse than animals!”

As the roar rang out, those who were leisurely leaving the village turned and looked at the disciples of the clan with eyes like wolves.

The sight of their clothes covered in blood made the Rampant Tiger Clan more furious.

“... what is that now?”

“Tsk tsk. What a great person.”

They were all laughing, but their eyes weren’t smiling; it was just madness in their eyes.

The word ‘creepy’ must have been made for people like them.

“I just cannot understand the thoughts of the Justice faction bastards.”

The Spear Ghost of Mount He, holding his long spear, snickered and approached the Rampant Tiger Clan disciples.

“If you had just pretended not to see and run, you could have saved your lives. What on earth are you thinking, coming all the way here and making a mess now? Do you think you won’t have a knife stuck in your throat?”

“That must be the Justice faction for us.”

“Morons.”

The others also slowly began to turn back.

“Well... since you are our guests, we need to treat you well.”

Even though the Seven Killers of Jiangxi didn’t set the mood, the Evil Faction members stepped forward. They began to tighten their grip on the disciples who wanted to fight.

“C-clan leader.”

“This...”

Looking at the approaching evil bastards coming with killing intent, the clan leader bit his lips until they bled.

“Gwan Hong!”

“Yes!”

“Lead the third-class disciples and go to Xi’an right now! We need to inform Xi’an of this situation!”

“Yes!”

“The remaining first-class and second-class disciples will join me in stopping them!”

“... I understand.”

The disciples had a gut feeling that with their strength, they would absolutely not be able to stop them.

The force emanating from their bodies was already making the disciples’ fingers tingle. How could a small clan like theirs handle such monsters?

Even so, they couldn’t back down now.

The reason was simple.

If everyone ran, everyone would die. Someone had to stay and become the shield for the disciples who were running.

And the more important reason...

“There may still be people alive inside! If we run away, leaving the innocent people who need help to survive, wouldn’t it be shameful to call ourselves the justice faction people who have been shouting about righteousness all our lives?”

“Yes!”

“A warrior proves himself by how he dies! Everyone, grit your teeth now!”

The fear in the eyes of the disciples vanished at the shout of their clan leader.

“Prove yourself by how you die?”

Kukuku.

A bitter smile appeared on his lips as he scraped the floor with the tip of his spear.

“Then you will end up as dogs. You will all die here!”

He ran towards the Rampant Tiger Clan like he didn’t want to give up such delicious food he had seen for the first time in a long time.

“Block him!”

“Defeat those evil ones!”

Starting with the Spear Ghost of Mount Hoe, the other members of the Evil Sects rushed in, laughing.

The clan leader began to gather as much internal qi as he could.

‘Even for a moment!’

He didn’t aim to win this fight.

What he wanted was to buy some time. He wanted to delay these evil people from reaching the next village, even for a second, to give his disciples a chance to escape.

‘The Nine Sects will surely come to help! Surely!’

With determination, he stepped forward and swung his fist at the airborne Spear Ghost.

The unique martial art of the Raging Tiger’s 18 Punches unfolded perfectly, like never before in his life. His two fists, enveloped with internal qi in red, swung like the forelegs of a tiger.

But...

“Slow down, old man.”

Kwak!

The spear extended like a flash and pierced the clan leader’s right fist with ease.

Kwak! Crack! Crack!

The spear made numerous holes the size of a child’s fist in his forearm, elbow, and shoulder in succession, before lodging itself in the chest of the clan leader.

“Euk...”

Just one attack.

The Spear Ghost of Mount He let out a mocking laugh at the clan leader, who was defeated in an instant, making everything seem meaningless.

“Oh my, if you had trained instead of playing around, you could have endured longer.”

It was the moment when the Spear Ghost of Mount He was about to pull the spear from the chest.

“Cough...”

Jo Myung-San grabbed the spear stuck in his chest with trembling hands.

“UM?”

That wasn’t a move to counterattack.

Jo Myung-San pushed the spear further into his chest, tightening his chest muscles and holding the spear with both hands.

As if he wouldn’t let it go.

As if he would delay the spear’s withdrawal and prevent it from being swung again, even if just for a moment.

“... this old man?”

Jo Myung-San smiled, blood gushing from his mouth, even though his face was contorted and unable to form a proper smile.

“D-do not... lay a hand... on civilians...”

Paat!

At that moment, the spear swung in a single breath, splitting Jo Myung-San’s body in half.

Thud.

“Such a foolish old man. You don’t even know your place.”

He turned his head, wiping off the blood that had fallen from his fingertips. Before he realized it, those who had been engaging the disciples were slaughtering them unilaterally.

Spear Ghost, looking for his share, clicked his tongue in annoyance. He couldn’t even swing his spear properly against these people.

“So lukewarm.”

He felt like his blood wasn’t boiling.

‘... there must be more worthy opponents in Xi’an.’

While he suppressed his killing intent, Dam Hae, who was watching the situation from behind, asked Gal Cheon-rip.

“Isn’t this funny, big bro?”

“What is?”

“I thought all the Justice faction people were hypocrites. Didn’t those Nine Great Sects beg for their lives from the Four Precepts Alliance?”

“Yes.”

“But didn’t those people, who couldn’t even reach the Nine Great Sects, die while keeping their mouths shut? It’s funny that these people are called small and medium clans.”

“That is the opposite.”

“Uh?”

Gal Cheon-rip chuckled.

“That is why they are small and medium clans. If they don’t pay attention to profitable things and avoid things that could be beneficial, how can a clan grow?”

“... now that I hear you, it is true.”

“A growing clan has to be sensitive to profits. A sect that truly believes in agreements and such is bound to end up like that.”

“But isn’t it still great?”

“It is great. They certainly deserve respect. At least they don’t just talk the talk like those moronic hypocrites, you know. But...”

Gal Cheon-rip’s gaze turned to the Rampant Tiger Clan disciples, who were screaming and dying miserably.

Contrary to his words, his gaze didn’t show even a shred of respect.

“They are such fools. They throw their lives away for meaningless things. Even animals avoid and run when they feel threatened... isn’t this worse than animals?”

“Hehehe. Yes, big bro.”

Gal Cheon-rip took his eyes off the scene of the massacre that was slowly coming to an end.

The Rampant Tiger Clan wasn’t even a thing to pass the time for them. It was like pouring a drop of water into the throat of someone who was dying of thirst.

‘If I make it worse, they might rush to bite their leader.’

Gal Cheon-rip gestured to the west.

“We have delayed too much. Everyone seems to be getting sick of it, so I think we need to head to the west now.”

“Yes, big bro. I will do that.”

A smile formed on Gal Cheon-rip’s lips.

‘Perfect.’

The preparations were now done.

“Branch leader.”

The beggar who entered the office bit his lips.

“What is it?”

“A village is on fire.”

“... what?”

“The Evil bastards killed all the people in the village and set it on fire.”

Crack.

The brush in Hong Dae-Kwang’s hand broke.

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lips until they bled and took a deep breath with red eyes.

“...Sanmenxia?”

“Yes.”

“Is that all?”

“... I only confirmed that they annihilated the village and moved further. They say they headed to the next village and were blocked by the Rampant Tiger Clan...”

“... Rampant Tiger?”

Hong Dae-Kwang closed his eyes tightly with a pained look.

It was foolish. There was no way they could have stopped them. The difference in their power was so great that it would be fortunate if even one person could survive the moment they met.

But...

How could they call those who risked their lives and ran for the people after hearing their screams foolish?

“Remember that name.”

“Yes!”

“Reinforcements? What happened to that request?”

“We sent it with an eagle, so it is about time for us to get a reply.”

“Damn it, it is late!”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lip tightly.

“They should have been contacted before we got this information and even moved first.”

If everything went as planned, everyone would be running towards Shaanxi like madmen. They should be here before any damage happens...

“Branch leader! Branch leader, the reply has come!”

“Where is it?”

“Here it is!”

“Bring it here, fast!”

Hong Dae-Kwang snatched the envelope the beggar had brought and opened it in one swift motion.

Soon, his eyes began to tremble.

“This...”

“Branch leader! A response came from Shaolin!”

“... open it.”

“Uh? But my power isn’t...”

“I get it, so open and read it, you moron!”

The beggar, startled by the killing intent from Hong Dae-Kwang, opened the envelope on the spot.

The beggar opened the letter, flinched at the words written inside, and looked at Hong Dae-Kwang.

“... read it.”

“That...”

“Read it!”

The beggar bit his lips tightly. His eyes, filled with bewilderment, shook.

“.... Reinforcements... not possible.”

“Hahah...”

Hong Dae-Kwang snickered and dropped the letter onto the desk as if it was slipping from his grasp.

The three words “Reinforcements not possible” were clearly written on the paper that slipped out of his hand.

“They cannot send support?”

Kwaang!

Hong Dae-Kwang slammed the desk, shattering it into pieces.

“What are these bastards talking about! Dozens, hundreds of people are dying right now, and support is difficult? You... this... ugh, morons!”

Unbearable curses burst out of Hong Dae-Kwang’s mouth.

Their target was the main sect and Shaolin. It wasn’t something that could come from a single person’s mouth, but no one could blame Hong Dae-Kwang for reacting like that.

“Branch leader... what do we do now?”

‘... What do we do?’

“The main sect is telling us to withdraw...”

At that moment, Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes shone.

Bam!

He kicked the inkstone that had fallen to the floor and hurled it at the beggar, who opened his mouth before gritting his teeth.

“You moron. Even though we are crippled beggars who cannot even beg properly, we can live without starving because the people of this town trust us and help us. But you want us to be the first to leave because it’s dangerous? Are you a beggar from the sect of no use?”

“B-But... this is the order of the main sect...”

“Ignore what these old, senile, insane beggars say! Damn it!”

Hong Dae-Kwang blushed and barely managed to suppress his anger.

“You morons, run to the villages along those morons’ paths and evacuate everyone.”

“I-it isn’t possible...”

“Do whatever you can, by any means! Tell the people in the city to evacuate right now!”

“... understood.”

“Move!”

“Yes!”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lips as the beggars ran away. Red blood flowed down his torn lips.

“And even after this...”

Hong Dae-Kwang clenched his fists until the blood drained from his body, gritting his teeth and running outside.

His bloodshot eyes turned to Mount Hua, hidden in the clouds.

Finally, he made up his mind and ran in one breath to the hiking trail leading to Mount Hua.

Chapter 869: This Is The Land Of Mount Hua. (4)

“... what did you just say?”

“The Evil Sect is coming. Get here right now!”

It was a statement that would make anyone pause and wonder about its meaning at least once. But, looking at the members of the Beggars Union, whose bodies were soaked in sweat as if they had run with all their might, no words came out.

“We must unite the sects in Xi’an and prepare for it now! The others are also communicating with the other sects in Xi’an, so reinforcements will arrive.”

“Around Xi’an...”

The question of whether any sects could help rose to the tip of the tongue, but the Hwayoung clan leader forced himself to suppress those words.

It wouldn’t be polite to the Beggars Union, who were trying to help them.

“Let me ask you something. What is the... strength of the enemy?”

“There is no time to explain in detail. But... they are strong.”

“How do they compare with the Ten Thousand People clan who invaded Xi’an before?”

“If it is the entire Ten Thousand People clan, then they aren’t comparable to it.”

“... right,”

Wei Lishan nodded with a heavy face.

In the past, the sects of Xi’an had been so afraid of a single sect that they fled from there.

Although things had changed since then... the fact that they alone couldn’t handle it was no different from the past. No, it was several times more hopeless.

“Reinforcements will come from Shaolin and the Beggars Union. Just endure until then!”

“Understood.”

Wei Lishan rose to his feet without hesitation.

The Beggars Union warrior looked at him with curious eyes. Usually, when someone hears something like this, their face turns pale, and they rush to find out more information. That way, they can decide if they want to fight or run.

But Wei Lishan didn’t ask any more questions, as if he already knew everything he needed to know.

The man found this odd and asked.

“Are you alright?”

“About what?”

“Is this enough for you...?”

It was a vague question that even the one asking was unsure of. But Wei Lishan nodded lightly as if he understood it.

“It doesn’t matter who the other people are.”

“...”

“When a sect settles in a region, it means to share the joys and sorrows of the place. Unless all the people in Xi’an have fled, there is just one thing we can do now.”

The Beggars Union man’s eyes trembled.

“A disciple of Mount Hua will never back down. I will unite the sects of Xi’an and do my best to block them, so please hurry up and bring support for us.”

“... I will definitely do so.”

“Then.”

As soon as Wei Lishan took a step forward, he shouted.

“So-Heng.”

“Yes, father!”

Wei So-heng, who had been waiting outside, quickly answered and opened the door. The young child had grown quite a lot in the meantime.

“Send the disciples to gather the clan leaders in Xi’an. It is urgent!”

“Yes!”

“And send the remaining disciples to gather all the disciples in Xi’an too.”

“Yes!”

“Hurry up!”

Wei So-heng ran out without answering.

The Beggars Union man watched and exclaimed in admiration.

‘Wei Lishan, the head of the Hwayoung clan.’

As far as he knew, Wei Lishan was a lucky man who received full support because he was the only one who held onto Mount Hua when everyone else abandoned it.

Thanks to that, the Hwayoung clan was now the representative sect in Xi’an. Still, the beggars’ opinion was that Wei Lishan, the clan leader, was nothing more than an ordinary warrior.

But...

‘Does a position make a person? Or is it the Beggars Union that didn’t understand him properly?’

They said that a person’s true value was revealed in a crisis, and the way Wei Lishan showed up now was enough to make all their previous comparisons go down.

“Now, Gul Gae.”

“... Uh? Ah... yes, clan leader!”

“Perhaps...”

Wei Lishan, who had been speaking freely without hesitation, hesitated a bit and cautiously said.

“Did... any news come from Mount Hua?”

The beggar hesitated and opened his mouth.

“No matter what the situation is, visiting a Closed Gate sect is against the rules of martial arts....”

Then, he said with a confident look.

“The situation is so perilous, so the branch leader must have gone to deliver the message.”

Wei Lishan nodded.

“I understand. Then I guess we have to endure a bit.”

“We might get turned down.”

“That will never happen.”

Wei Lishan smiled brightly with a face that had no doubt.

“They will definitely come. It is Mount Hua.”

They were such people.

“Evil Sect...”

Hwang Jongi’s face was filled with deep concern.

He let out a soft sigh after thinking about many things in a short moment.

“They are coming here?”

“Yes. In our opinion, the first place they will aim for when they come to Xi’an is...”

“This place, I guess.”

Hwang Jongi sighed.

The current Eunha was incomparable to the past. The world had become chaotic recently, so their business became difficult. Still, even so, the wealth they had accumulated in recent years will not go anywhere. They have long established themselves as the best merchants in Xi’an and one of the top ten merchant unions in the world.

There was no way that the Evil Sect would leave such a delicious place alone.

“You should get out of here first.”

“...”

When Hwang Jongi remained silent, the beggar bit his lips and urged him.

“Those people are coming, and they are dangerous. I am not saying this because they are of the Evil Sect. They are truly insane beasts.”

“...”

“Merchant lord, you know that the Ten Thousand People clan had invaded Xi’an in the past, right?”

“Yes.”

“It is different now. Back then, the forces didn’t touch the civilians. But they have already annihilated two villages now. If you stay here, you will not live. So please...”

“That cannot be done, Gul Gae.”

But Hwang Jongi shook his head.

“Uh? Y-yes, but...”

“I know. But I cannot run away. A merchant doesn’t abandon his place.”

“...merchant lord. This isn’t a situation where you can be so complacent. Right now...”

“I am not saying this because of that.”

“...”

Hwang Jongi shook his head.

“There are many goods in the warehouse right now. If they are taken, this place would naturally collapse.”

“Why don’t you run away with them?”

“Since most of the members of the Eunha are out on expeditions, there is no one to move the goods. And... would the evil ones who came in after the treasure just go back empty-handed because the warehouse is empty? They will chase you to the ends of hell and try to steal it. They would even kill the workers here.”

“...you have money with you already, right? If you just move the valuables first...”

Hwang Jongi smiled.

“A merchant union that piles up its earned money in a safe is doomed to fail. There are no valuables here that can be easily taken away.”

“...”

“And.”

A heavy voice flowed from Hwang Jongi’s mouth.

“A merchant isn’t in a place where you can make money alone. There are many places that trust us and do business with us. If the goods in their warehouse are taken, we go bankrupt.”

“... isn’t your life more precious?”

“There is something my late father used to say. To a merchant, trust is more important than life. My father always kept his word. And if I run now because I value my life and betray his trust, how can I see my father when I die?”

Hwang Jongi shook his head.

“And most of the goods here are not my property. They belong to close friends who trusted me.”

“...”

“If it were them, I would tell them to just take away the money and leave the people, but... haha, since they are not around, I cannot do as I please with their money, right?”

“Merchant lord...”

Hwang Jongi smiled broadly.

If the person in front of him hadn’t been so open-minded and detached from wealth, he would have explained in detail how difficult it was to entrust all of the wealth to someone else.

And how much trust was required for that?

Mount Hua entrusted all of their wealth to Hwang Jongi and entered Closed Gate. But if he then lost all of his wealth and welcomed those who had come out of the Closed Gate?

Even though he was a merchant who couldn’t live without shame, his face wasn’t that thick-skinned.

“I will evacuate the people around. But I will not be leaving this place. They will not touch a single grain of rice without cutting through me first. That is my responsibility as the lord of the merchant union. So do not speak more of it.”

The beggar bit his lip.

He didn’t think this was reasonable. But what more could he say to someone who was so strong-willed?

“... the Hwayoung clan leader will unite the sects in Xi’an and help us.”

“The clan leader will?”

“Yes. When he heard of the evil bastards coming, he said he would fight without another thought.”

Hwang Jongi smiled at those words.

“What is it?”

The beggar asked in confusion, and Hwang Jongi shook his head.

“No, it is nothing. It is just... I thought of something.”

“What...”

“They are so consistent. I guess it is what they have seen and learned.”

The beggar tilted his head as if he couldn’t understand it, but Hwang Jongi didn’t bother explaining it either.

‘That is why they are close to Mount Hua.’

It seemed like he had been watching the ones who said to get out of danger but run to the front whenever there was danger for others. Even those around the sect didn’t seem to have the slightest intention of running.

Hwang Jongi stood up.

His job was clear. He had to evacuate the innocent people of the merchant union so that they wouldn’t die and guard the place with escorts.

“They seem to be looking down on this place, so we should show them how fierce the people of Shaanxi are.”

“...Merchant lord, why are you doing this...”

“I understand.”

“...Uh?”

Hwang Jongi smiled strangely.

“I’ve thought that way many times too. There were many times when I couldn’t understand why smart people acted foolishly, and it was frustrating.”

“...”

“But now that I think of it, I understand. If I were a young leader, I would have told my father to run without looking back. But now I am the leader. Those who are responsible for others shouldn’t care about profit.”

Just like his father and Mount Hua did.

So now, it was time to prove how foolish he could act.

“Do not worry too much. I am not thinking about messing around. They will surely come.”

“...Mount Hua?”

“Yes.”

Hwang Jongi nodded.

“But Mount Hua is in...”

“If they get word, they will come running. We just need to endure until then.”

“...I understand.”

But Wei Lishan, Hwang Jongi, and even the beggars’ unions were so busy evacuating the people that no one expected it.

Hwang Dae-Kwang, who climbed Mount Hua with all his might, would face an unimaginable situation.

Chapter 870: This Is The Land Of Mount Hua. (5)

“Euk! Huk! Huk!”

Heavy breaths escaped from Hong Dae-Kwang’s mouth.

Even though he was a branch leader of the Beggars Union, he couldn’t help but gasp after exerting himself to climb the steep cliffs of Mount Hua with all his might.

His lungs felt like they would explode and burn, and his dantian felt like it was being stabbed by needles, but he couldn’t stop moving.

“Kuak!”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes, having jumped up the last cliff in one breath, saw the firmly shut gates of Mount Hua.

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had rushed immediately, began to pound on the gate.

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon! Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon! You fucking moron!”

Kwang! Thud!

The gate shook wildly as if it would break at any moment. But no answer came from inside.

“Mount Hua clan! Anyone! Damn it! Come out for a moment! We are in huge trouble!”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s voice, filled with internal qi, resonated loudly. But there was still no answer from inside.

“This...”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lip tightly.

It was already improper to shout outside the Closed Gate of a sect, but right now, he didn’t have the time to consider such things.

His eyes turned to the low walls of Mount Hua.

‘Nothing more can be done.’

Invading a Closed Gate was an act of showing no respect to the sect. Even if Mount Hua regarded him as an enemy and publicly declared it after, no one would be surprised.

However, Hong Dae-Kwang didn’t care.

‘There is no way Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon cares about trivial manners.’

If he didn’t deliver this news, the only thing left would be to be beaten to death at the hands of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.

With his resolve firm, he took a deep breath and jumped over Mount Hua’s wall in one breath.

“Mount Hua sect! Now...”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had been shouting while jumping over, closed his mouth before he could even land.

“W-what?”

The sight that came into his eyes was different from what he expected.

His eyes fluttered open wide.

Everything was shattered.

The guesthouse, once neatly constructed with stone, was now broken and dug up as if stones had fallen from the sky. The surrounding pavilions were half-collapsed.

“This is...”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face turned pale.

Didn’t this seem like someone attacked this place?

Hong Dae-Kwang gulped and came to his senses.

‘T-this isn’t the time.’

No matter what happened to the pavilion, he must inform the disciples of Mount Hua about what was happening outside right now. If they didn’t help him immediately, the people of Xi’an would be in trouble.

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

Hong Dae-Kwang ran ahead, shouting loudly, and opened the gate of the pavilion that was still intact.

“Outside now...”

But once again, Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t finish his words.

There was no one inside the pavilion he opened. What shocked Hong Dae-Kwang the most wasn’t the emptiness, but the white dust that had piled up inside.

It was dust that suggested no one had set foot there for at least several months.

Hong Dae-Kwang turned around with trembling eyes and opened the doors of another pavilion.

Kwang!

... nothing.

His face, as he opened all the doors around the pavilion, looked like someone who had lost his mind.

And until the moment the last pavilion was opened, not a single ant could be seen in Mount Hua.

“N-no...”

Hong Dae-Kwang mumbled hoarsely and collapsed to the ground.

All sorts of thoughts swirled in his mind.

“Why...”

What on earth happened here?

‘Did the Ten Thousand People clan invade?’

That couldn’t be the case.

Even if it wasn’t the Ten Thousand People clan but the Four Precepts alliance, it would be impossible to come all the way to Xi’an and eliminate Mount Hua without anyone noticing.

Then.

Then what happened here?

Hong Dae-Kwang covered his mouth with trembling hands.

“If that happens... if this happened then Xi’an....”

His eyes were filled with despair. No matter what happened here, the only thing certain at this moment was that Mount Hua, the protector of Xi’an, did not exist.

“Damn it...”

Hong Dae-Kwang cursed as if he were in pain, and soon his bloodshot eyes turned red.

“Damn it! If this is the case, the people in Xi’an will die! Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon! MOUNT HUA BASTARDDDD!”

Hong Dae-Kwang screamed as if his lungs would burst out of his throat and slammed his fist into the ground.

Kwang!

He then bit his lips until they bled again.

‘... I need to go.’

If Mount Hua wasn’t there, he had to go. He had to go and help. He jumped up from the mountain and looked back, feeling foolish.

He clenched his fists and took in the scenery of Mount Hua with both eyes red and teeth gritted.

“Damn it!”

His loud voice echoed harshly in the quiet Mount Hua.

A heavy silence slowly descended on Mount Hua after Hong Dae-Kwang left.

“... Thank you for coming, Clan Leader.”

Hwang Jongi grabbed Wei Lishan’s hand tightly. Then Wei Lishan shook his head.

“It isn’t something to be grateful for. This is an obvious thing.”

“Clan Leader...”

“It is so obvious that the Xi’an sect protected the people in Xi’an. And it is also obvious that the Mount Hua clan protects the merchant union.”

“It is easy to speak, but...”

How many people would risk their lives to do that?

Hwang Jongi’s heart was filled with emotions, but he didn’t say them out loud. He thought that Wei Lishan could also guess the gratitude without it being spoken.

“The gratitude should be given to them and not me.”

Hwang Jongi turned his gaze to the back of Wei Lishan.

“Everyone...”

The clan leaders of small and medium clans of Xi’an and their disciples. Among them, there were those who had close ties with Mount Hua or Hwayoung Clan, but there were also those of Southern Edge’s sub-clans who opposed them.

“... Thank you.”

“Well...”

The head of the Dawning Moon Clan, Heart Moon Sword Nam Ja-Myung, scratched his head with a slightly embarrassed look.

The Dawning Moon Clan was the representative of Xi’an’s Southern Edge sub-clans. No matter what, they couldn’t maintain good ties with the Hwayoung clan, a sub-clan of Mount Hua, or the Eunha, which was close to Mount Hua.

They weren’t at the level of conflict as before, but there was still this subtle uneasiness lingering.

But.

“As the Hwayoung clan leader said, this is a natural thing, so you don’t have to be thankful. I cannot leave the responsibility of protecting Xi’an to Mount Hua alone.”

“Clan leader...”

“Ahem.”

Nam Ja-Myung coughed loudly.

During the past incident, he also realized something significant.

‘The important thing isn’t about which sect is stronger.’

That might be important now. But, in the end, the future of a sect never depended solely on strength. The sects and clans that fled with their tails between their legs during their last fights had to endure unspeakable humiliation for years.

Not only was the influence of Southern Edge in Xi’an shattered, but the Hwayoung clan took away his position as the representative of Xi’an. As a result, he had to watch, eyes wide open, as many sects left Southern Edge and came under Mount Hua’s command.

But he didn’t really feel any resentment. That was the price they paid for their mistakes.

‘A man who doesn’t protect the powerless commoners is no different from a thug with a sword.’

The men under Nam Ja-Myung and the Xi’an sects learned their lessons through experience.

It wasn’t that they weren’t afraid. Who didn’t know the fierce reputation of the Evil sect people rushing toward them now? No one was stupid enough to not know that clashing with such people was no different from inviting death.

But.

‘There are times when we have to face the oncoming storm head-on.’

They learned this by observing Mount Hua, which was initially ignored and then became famous worldwide.

People who weren’t from Shaanxi might think that Mount Hua was renowned for its strength, but at least the people in Shaanxi knew the truth. The reason Mount Hua gained fame was that they never backed down.

“Don’t worry too much. I will fight with my life on the line, to the very last of the disciples.”

“Thank you, clan leader.”

At that moment, Dong Bang-hwi, the clan leader of the Righteous Sword Clan, asked with a worried expression.

“But clan leader Nam, why are we setting up camp at the Eunha merchant union?”

“Hm?”

“No. If those people are interested in money, they will come here... wouldn’t it be better to shut the gates and block it with a wall?”

In response, Wei Lishan replied.

“That is a bad idea.”

“A bad idea?”

Wei Lishan nodded.

“Not many people can climb that high wall, and for those skilled in climbing, that wall would be no obstacle. If they jump over the wall, can we stop them?”

“... Hmm, right.”

“And it is impossible for us to guard the entire wall by ourselves. It would be better to do it here, where we have the advantage of numbers.”

The Eunha merchant union had the largest manor in Xi’an. If they stayed together there, it might actually be easier to deal with the enemy.

“I am worried that the enemies might not come here right away and attack the civilians first...”

“Then we should go and fight. However... the probability of that happening isn’t so high. They will want to check if the Eunha merchants have escaped or not.”

“Hmm. Could be so.”

The clan leaders nodded, indicating they understood Wei Lishan’s reasoning.

At the same time, tension began to spread across everyone’s faces. After hearing Wei Lishan’s explanation, they truly felt that they had to confront the evil people with just their numbers.

“... those damn evil people. Why Xi’an of all places?”

“Isn’t it because our benefactors are gone? Damn it! If Southern Edge hadn’t closed the gates, they wouldn’t have even come close.”

“Even if Southern Edge did so if only we had Mount Hua...”

The fact that the last words came not from the Hwayoung Clan but from Dong Bang-hwi of Southern Edge’s sub-clan proved that the status of Mount Hua had changed.

It was during that movement that everyone felt subtle glances of unease upon them.

“Clan leader!”

A Beggars Union member came running in from outside.

“The Evil Sect have arrived outside the border!”

Wei Lishan’s face hardened.

‘Already?’

It was much faster than expected. That meant their skills were beyond what was known.

‘I guess there will be more trouble than expected today.’

He steeled his resolve and gave a warning to the wavering clan leaders.

“They are after none other than our Xi’an.”

“...”

“So, of course, we should be protecting it, right? It doesn’t matter how strong the enemy is. What matters is the civilians of Xi’an who trust us.”

Nam Ja-Myung opened his eyes.

“You are saying something so obvious to us all. We know better than anyone else. We were born and raised in Xi’an!”

It meant that they would fulfill their role, and no one had to remind them. Wei Lishan smiled at those words.

“Sounds dependable.”

“Hmp.”

Nam Ja-Myung shouted with a determined look.

“Let us tell those evil bastards that Xi’an is full of vicious people!”

“Yes!”

“Defeat the Evil Sect!”

It was a shout to shake off the fear, but it was definitely effective. When those who had raised their morale formed their formation, Hwang Jongi quietly approached Wei Lishan and asked.

“Will this be alright, clan leader?”

Wei Lishan quietly responded with a chuckle.

“Merchant lord. I don’t know much, but I do know one thing.”

“That is...”

“If we receive a favor, we should know how to repay it.”

“...”

“I was finally given the chance to repay the favor I received from Mount Hua, so I am happy; what more is there to be afraid of?”

Hwang Jongi, who saw him smiling, finally nodded quietly.

“I feel the same way.”

And at that moment.

Finally, the group of people with wild killing intent arrived in front of Xi’an city gate.

Chapter 871: This Is The Land Of Mount Hua. (6)

“Hmm.”

Gal Cheon-rip’s lips twitched as he saw the wide-open gates of Xi’an.

“They don’t seem so stupid.”

“Aren’t they really stupid? They opened the gates for us to raid them.”

“Maybe so.”

They had different thoughts, but there was no need to argue. Right now, managing the force is more important.

“Don’t they know that we are here?”

“There is no way we could avoid the eyes of beggars around the world. Of course, they know. It is obvious when we don’t see a single civilian walking in this big city.”

Dam Hae nodded at those words. It was definitely odd that no pedestrians were moving around the city when the sun hadn’t even set yet. The fact that there were no guards was also strange.

“What do we do? It could be a trap?”

“Such nonsense.”

Gal Cheon-rip slowly licked his lips with his tongue.

“Whether it is a trap or something, once we have come this far, there is no turning back.”

Gal Cheon-rip glanced back at those exuding killing intent behind him. If they even mentioned stopping, their killing intent would fall on him.

“Hehe.”

Gal Cheon-rip smiled and looked down.

Even if they all rushed at him, there was no reason for him and his sworn brothers to be afraid, but there was no reason to stop a festival midway.

“Let us see what you have prepared.”

Led by Gal Cheon-rip, the Evil Sect people began to head inside through the gates of the city with fierce intent.

“Eik...!”

“T-they are coming.”

They could see.

The people of the Evil Sect looked no different from normal people in appearance. But the killing intent and their fierce aura were enough to make the hearts of those who saw them tremble.

Those who couldn’t run, those who couldn’t bear to leave their homes, gulped and watched them walk down.

The sight of those who didn’t even think of wiping off the blood on them, walking with weapons that looked terrifying, struck them with fear.

Gal Cheon-rip bared his teeth as he watched those who couldn’t even hide and froze.

“Big bro.”

“First... let us check the Eunha merchants.”

The group of people and carriages running could be seen. Then, it was natural to first check out their prey.

As soon as he finished saying that, Dam Hae flew like the wind, grabbed the nape of one of the people in front of him, and returned to Gal Cheon-rip.

“Ugh... uh...”

Gal Cheon-rip asked softly to the one frozen, unable to even speak.

“Do you know where the Eunha Merchants are?”

The man nodded with a blank look. Gal Cheon-rip smiled.

“Guide us. Then I will send you off safely.”

“... what did you say?”

Wei Lishan asked back, looking at Hong Dae-Kwang, who was covered in sweat.

Hong Dae-Kwang wanted to ignore the shocked look on the face, but he couldn’t help but tell the truth.

“... there were no disciples in the Mount Hua sect.”

When he said it again, Wei Lishan, who had a stiff face, was silent for a moment, turned his head, and looked at the distant sky. Then he nodded.

“I see.”

“Clan leader. Think about this again. If it is like this, it would be better...”

“No more.”

Wei Lishan shook his head.

“Nothing will change. We will protect this place until every single civilian in Xi’an escapes.”

“That is too much!”

“I know. But... that is too familiar to the disciples of Mount Hua.”

“....”

“Thank you for your hard work, branch leader. You should also start getting out of here quickly.”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lips tightly.

“Damn it. If Xi’an falls, all the beggars here will starve to death. Where do you want us to go? Isn’t that right, beggars?”

The beggars of the Beggars Union, who had gathered before Hong Dae-Kwang arrived, shouted.

“YES!”

“Damn it, it is the same whether we starve to death or fight to death. If there are no people, we will starve to death anyway.”

“Kekeke. We need to show them how foolish it is to invade the beggars’ land.”

“Begging isn’t shameful. What is shameful is not being grateful for what we have received. Shouldn’t we pay for the food we have eaten?”

Upon hearing that, Wei Lishan smiled.

“Look at that. Why are you telling us to run away when you won’t run?”

“... the beggars of Shaanxi are all out of their minds.”

“It isn’t the beggars of Shaanxi who are like that. It is the people of Shaanxi who are like that.”

Wei Lishan said with a smile.

“These are the people who represent Shaanxi, so what can we do?”

“... damn Mount Hua.”

Hong Dae-Kwang looked at everyone with an annoyed expression. But, on the other hand, he could see a hint of pride that he couldn’t hide.

Even the Shaolin monks and Wudang wouldn’t hesitate to bow their heads to their enemies to save their lives. Still, these people with no fame or possessions would not hesitate to throw their lives away against the evil ones.
