“Ah, right. Since we will only live once anyway, let us not waste our lives! You beggars! Hang on and bite at least one more! Only then can the innocent people live.”

“Yes!”

Just as Hong Dae-Kwang was about to say something more, someone spoke.

“Come.”

That short word created a tense atmosphere among everyone.

A group of warriors was approaching through the wide-open gate and the street.

Hong Dae-Kwang gritted his teeth.

They looked strong even from their gait. Those who didn’t even consider their opponents as enemies were coming to catch the rats they had pushed into the trap.

‘Damn these evil bastards.’

His breathing became rapid, his heart pounded, and blood rushed to his face.

It wasn’t because his pride was hurt. Just looking at the sight made him realize how big the gap between them was.

‘Their number... 200? No, maybe 300?’

Among all of those people, there was no one who looked weaker than him. Their numbers were clearly superior, but their quality was incomparable.

‘Seven Killers of Jiangxi.’

Hong Dae-Kwang clenched his fists tightly after confirming the identity of those walking in front with his own eyes.

“That is it.”

Gal Cheon-rip smiled brightly as he reached the end.

There was no need to confirm. It was clear just by looking at the people holding the line inside the place.

“T-this is it. I guided you right, so please spare me...”

“Ah, don’t worry.”

At that moment, Dam Hae’s small sword, which had been held at the man’s neck, dug into the man’s back.

“Kuak...”

The man’s eyes widened. His body, which had been filled with life, began to tremble.

“Wh... why...”

Gal Cheon-rip shrugged his shoulders as he saw the resentment in the man’s eyes.

“You kept your promise. I saved your life. But my younger brother doesn’t seem to think the same. Even though I am an older brother, it isn’t right to tell a grown man what to do, right?”

“...”

“And don’t be so angry at us; it is a blessing to die in peace.”

The man’s body fell to the ground. His body, which had been twitching and twitching, soon fell limp.

Hong Dae-Kwang shouted with bloodshot eyes.

“You! You bastards! Why are you killing innocent civilians! Why!”

“Why?”

Gal Cheon-rip asked as if he was truly puzzled, watching him shout in a fit of rage.

“Is there any reason why people kill others?”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang stared at Gal Cheon-rip without even closing his mouth.

“If you have no strength, you must die. Isn’t it simple? If you don’t like me like this, you can come kill me too. Of course, that is if you have the ability.”

His extremely calm tone gave him goosebumps. Did he think that killing a perfectly healthy person was no different from cutting rice with a sickle?

“This...”

At that moment, Wei Lishan stepped in front of Hong Dae-Kwang.

Wei Lishan, who had been staring at Gal Cheon-rip silently for a moment, opened his mouth.

“We won’t be able to talk anyway, but know one thing.”

“Hmm?”

Wei Lishan glanced at the Eunha merchant gate for a moment. It was as if there was this invisible wall there. Then he turned to Gal Cheon-rip again.

“This is the land of Mount Hua.”

“....”

“The disciples of Mount Hua have not learned to show mercy to evil people who invade the land of Mount Hua. You, too, will pay the price.”

“Ha... hahah.”

Gal Cheon-rip laughed as if it was ridiculous.

“This fucking bug...”

At the same time, an eerie killing intent rose from his body, as if all his calmness until then had vanished like a lie.

“You dare run your mouth in front of me? I will kill you without mercy!”

He could no longer hold back the bastards behind him. Those who saw the enemy in front of them were as dangerous as wild beasts who saw fresh meat.

Gal Cheon-rip opened his mouth softly with killing intent.

“Do whatever you want. But don’t kill the merchant lord.”

As soon as he said those words, the people behind him charged ahead with an explosive burst of breathtaking momentum.

There were no loud cheers, and that was what was more terrifying. Without giving anyone a chance to raise their spirit, the Evil Sect people rushed in and stabbed the ones ahead in an instant.

“ACKKK!”

“AHHHH!”

Terrible screams rang out.

Seeing his disciples dying without doing anything, Nam Ja-Myung was shocked.

“You worthless bastards!”

At that moment, the Spear Ghost pierced through the swords blocking him and struck one. The Bloody Blade Killer also cut the flying swords like reeds and attacked.

Blood splashed around, and severed heads soared into the sky without even closing their eyes.

It was a one-sided massacre. In an instant, the battlefield was engulfed in fear.

“Damn it! Fight back, you morons!”

“Yes!”

“Hwayoung Clan, do not back down!”

“Yes!”

The Beggars Union and the Hwayoung Clan rallied their forces to support those who were shocked and frozen, but regaining momentum once lost was no easy task.

“ACKKK!”

“AHKKK!”

No, even before that, they were no match for them.

They were certainly not lacking in hope. Weren’t they the ones who risked their lives by staying back? Even if their opponent was the Four Sects Alliance, they wouldn’t have backed down.

But it was absolutely impossible to overcome this difference in skill with just hope.

“Eu-eup!”

The Hwayoung Clan’s disciples were wide-eyed as they saw the swords flying toward them.

‘I-I might die...’

At that moment, when he sensed unavoidable death, a sword suddenly appeared from behind and deflected the attack.

“Are you alright?”

“C-clan leader?”

Wei Lishan bit his lips tightly.

His wrist, which had been blocking the sword attacks, felt like it would break down. He had only exchanged a couple of blows but couldn’t help but feel the level of their strength. Even the nameless Evil Sect people were stronger than him.

However, Wei Lishan didn’t hesitate and stepped forward.

“Don’t try to knock them down!”

A loud roar burst from his throat as he blocked another knife.

“Hold on tight! Survive at any cost! We need to buy some time.”

Looking at their fierce eyes, he couldn’t help but understand. Their weapons wouldn’t calm down with just their blood.

‘I will save at least one more person.’

Hwang Jongi was behind him.

He couldn’t be standing there without thought. He could have guessed now. If the Eunha Merchant Union had run, who would their angry swords have been directed at?

Even the merchants risked their lives for civilians, so how could he, the Lord of Eunha, run to save himself?

Of course, his life was more precious now. And the lives of his disciples who believed in him and followed him were even more precious.

But there is something that cannot be exchanged for anything to a warrior.

“We die here today! Prove that the warriors of Shaanxi do not spare lives for the sake of negotiations!”

With shouts filled with anger, the battlefield began to heat up even more.

Chapter 872: How Do You Want To Die? (1)

“AHHHH!”

A long spear pierced his chest.

The Spear Ghost of Mount He, who had driven the spear deeper into the chest of the swordsman blocking his way, smiled and said,

“Fucking moron.”

“Kuak... uhh...”

Paat!

As he pulled out the spear in one breath, blood gushed from the swordsman’s chest like a fountain. Hot blood spurted out, staining the area under his eyes red.

The Spear Ghost, who had kicked the one who hadn’t yet fallen, knocked him down and wiped the blood off his face with one hand.

The strong smell of blood filled the air.

The disgusting heat.

“Kukuku.”

A soft laugh escaped.

Although the opponent wasn’t one he could truly savor, isn’t resistance important?

Stabbing a spear into the back of someone running away was a special pleasure, but deflecting the sword of someone resisting like this and stabbing a spear into their chest was an incomparable thrill.

‘It has been too long.’

A sinister light shone in his eyes.

The humiliation of having to flee from their land and the suffocation of being held in the hands of the Nine Great Sects were liberated at that moment.

“It isn’t enough.”

With eyes filled with killing intent, he turned his gaze to find the next prey.

“AHHH!”

“ACK!”

Screams erupted from all directions. A wolf baring its teeth, rushing around, searching for gaps.

Paat!

One person’s head flew into the air.

Eyes filled with pain and fear briefly stared at the sky before sinking to the ground.

“Uh...”

The Bloody Blade Killer glared forward, eyes shining, drunk on the smell of blood.

The faces of those who saw one person flying up turned pale and hard.

‘This is why the Justice faction people are so much fun.’

The Evil Sect never dared to fight the strong. They knew better than anyone that doing so would cost them their lives.

But these people were different. They knew how hard survival would be, yet they did not back down easily.

It was quite amusing to see those who were unable to do anything with their heads, not wanting to back down, and their bodies struggling to run away at any moment.

But.

The Bloody Blade Killer rushed ahead, swiftly cutting down the one who hesitated and severing the neck of the person who hadn’t even blinked yet.

“Even so, it is just urging the order to be implemented.”

He smiled softly and shook off the warm blood that had splashed on his hand. A vile pleasure and thick killing intent swept through his eyes like a storm.

Those who were inspired by their killing intent attacked the Xi’an warriors with even greater ferocity.

“They are fucking insane.”

“Hmm.”

Gal Cheon-rip looked ahead with an odd smile.

Those who were excited to the tips of their heads were swinging their swords without thinking about what would happen.

‘It is going to be a long night.’

They probably wouldn’t be able to quench their thirst even after killing everyone here. They would only come to their senses after seeing more blood.

Not all of the Evil Sect people were insane about blood.

It was just that the more violent ones and those who were put under pressure came under him. All Gal Cheon-rip did was create a situation for them to run wild and unwind.

‘At this point, they won’t be able to hide their identities and come back again.’

Those who turned the merchant union and those who annihilated the people and massacred the civilians cannot be the same.

Perhaps the Shaolin and Beggars Union, who had hoped Shaanxi would be only moderately damaged, would be shocked after seeing this sight.

All Gal Cheon-rip had to do was gather all those who had nowhere to go and build a strong force.

‘Then...’

Crack.

A bright killing intent bloomed in Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes.

‘I guess I can hit that damned guy.’

The image of Jang Ilso smiling arrogantly came to his mind. Just 20 years ago, the difference between him and Jang Ilso wasn’t that significant. In fact, Gal Cheon-rip’s reputation was higher than Jang Ilso’s, and his skills were superior too.

However, the current situations of Gal Cheon-rip and Jang Ilso were as different as heaven and earth.

‘If I had built my strength earlier, I wouldn’t have fallen so far behind.’

Although he felt regret for what had happened, he was glad that an opportunity had opened up for him.

‘In a way, it was a chance he managed to create.’

If the Four Precepts Alliance hadn’t tried to subjugate the Evil Sects in the south, it would have been impossible for the Seven Killers of Jiangxi to gather such a force. It would have taken him decades at most, and the Four Sects Alliance would have noticed it before then.

But in times of chaos, opportunities are bound to arise.

If he could just make good use of this chance, he could build a powerful force in an instant. Of course, it would take some time to make them his loyal followers, but isn’t that something that is bound to happen?

So...

Gal Cheon-rip gestured ahead with his chin.

“There might be some who realize what they are doing, so go and make them run wild a little more.”

“Yes, big bro.”

The younger ones who had been guarding him ran out in an instant, their killing intent palpable.

Gal Cheon-rip licked his lips with his tongue. An unseen desire rose in his eyes.

“Damn it!”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes were red.

The sects of Xi’an were falling, coughing, and spilling blood everywhere.

“ACKK!”

Blood rushed to Hong Dae-Kwang’s face as he saw another person die.

‘Damn it!’

They were all no match for him.

Those gathered there must have practiced their best, but a clan was just a clan. They couldn’t possibly match the Evil Sect, especially those who had been infamous since the past.

“Stop the front line from collapsing!”

“Yes!”

The Beggars Union members who heard the voice gritted their teeth and rushed forward. They were not the ones typically called warriors in the Beggars Union, but they were still part of it. They were incomparably stronger than the disciples of the weak and incompetent small and medium clans.

‘But even so, it is too much!’

Even the best of the Beggars Union could face them and try to win.

What was more terrifying was that the opponent’s morale was higher. The Evil Sect, who had seen blood for the first time in a while, were running around like beasts who had been starving for a month. Those who were overwhelmed by their force were falling without even being able to demonstrate their lacking skills.

Hong Dae-Kwang clenched his fists with an anxious expression.

At this rate, they would soon be annihilated. Their strength exceeded their fame. Even if they endured as long as they could, they wouldn’t be able to hold out much longer.

“When are those damn Southern Edge people coming?”

Now, the only hope was the Southern Edge sect. Someone who received his orders asked Southern Edge for help. Those who declared a Closed Gate might ignore it, but Hong Dae-Kwang erased that thought from his mind.

If Southern Edge didn’t come to help, everyone here would die, and this entire city would be covered in blood. So why bother thinking about that?

Clench!

Hong Dae-Kwang’s body was shaking as he clutched the bat until it bled.

Common sense told him that he had to be there. The only person who could grasp the situation and give the right orders was Hong Dae-Kwang. If he survived until the end, he could endure a little longer.

But...

“AHHH!”

“KUAKKK!”

Even the Beggars Union members who rushed forward were dying while screaming in despair.

“Ughh...”

He knew it in his head but couldn’t stand it.

“You fucking bastards!”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who couldn’t endure it any longer, lunged forward, screaming until his throat turned hoarse.

“Uh?”

His club, filled with fierce killing intent, fell on the head of Spear Ghost, who was pulling his spear from the chest of a beggar.

Spear Ghost, his lips twisted into a smile, blocked the club swinging down at him.

Kuung!

The club and spear collided in front of Spear Ghost’s head. Hong Dae-Kwang’s arms trembled as he tried to push down the spear with his club.

“This...”

“A beggar who only does one thing?”

Thud!

The Spear Ghost, who lightly pushed Hong Dae-Kwang away, looked at him with confident eyes. Judging from the heavy feeling left on his fingertips holding the spear, this guy seemed to be quite different from the other morons.

“If you are a beggar, there is no way you wouldn’t know who I am. You have courage, I tell you.”

“You are a moronic bastard, so you lack understanding. Which is why you have courage.”

“Hmmm?”

Hong Dae-Kwang gritted his teeth and raised the club.

“Since you did this, you won’t have a graceful death. Every single one of you will go to hell.”

“Hahaha, does the Beggars Union have the power to do that? Even Shaolin and Wudang don’t have the strength to chase us.”

“Moron, you know nothing.”

“...what?”

“This is Shaanxi. You attacked the clans of Mount Hua in the land of Mount Hua.”

Hong Dae-Kwang gritted his teeth and said.

“You don’t know what that means. You touched those who should never be touched.”

“Have you gone insane from begging for so long? We don’t fear the Shaolin or Wudang, so why would we fear a mere Mount Hua Sect?”

“That is why I called you a moron.”

“...”

The face of Spear Ghost of Mount He hardened. Even though it wasn’t a serious provocation, his tone was annoying.

“Do you think those who fight here are fearless and enduring? As if! It isn’t like we don’t care about our lives and are throwing them away.”

“...”

“I believe that revenge will definitely be achieved without a doubt. I firmly believe they will chase you to the depths of hell and make you pay, allowing me to stand here. That is why it is a sect called Mount Hua.”

The Spear Ghost glared at Hong Dae-Kwang with a shocked expression.

“Huh...”

The eyes of those who heard Hong Dae-Kwang’s words changed dramatically. The eyes that had just moments ago looked like frightened chicks now resembled snakes.

“Right.”

“Heheh. These people don’t seem to know what it means to provoke Mount Hua.”

“If they knew, they wouldn’t do this. If only they knew.”

The Spear Ghost frowned at the people speaking out.

‘Mount Hua?’

Not Shaolin or Southern Edge, but... Mount Hua?

What on earth was Mount Hua that made them react like this?

At that moment, Hong Dae-Kwang growled.

“There is no way for you to survive. The moment Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon... No, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint learns of what happened here, you will face unavoidable hell.”

The Spear Ghost of Mount He looked at Hong Dae-Kwang with a dumbfounded expression.

“... what can that kid from Mount Hua even do?”

“Kid?”

Hong Dae-Kwang chuckled.

“You have to be at least like Jang Ilso to survive him. But you won’t be any good once you wake up dead, right?”

“... you...”

The face of Spear Ghost hardened at the creeping humiliation, and he let out a cold killing intent.

“Let’s see if you can still talk after your body is full of holes.”

“Go ahead and try it, you moron!”

As soon as he said this, Hong Dae-Kwang rushed at him.

Spear Ghost clenched his teeth, realizing this beggar didn’t care about his life.

“You damn beggar!”

His spear, full of killing intent, shot towards Hong Dae-Kwang’s neck.

Chapter 873: How Do You Want To Die? (2)

‘How strange.’

Gal Cheon-rip thought with eyes slightly open.

Conventionally, a battle was a fight where one side would be exterminated, but in reality, it wasn’t like that. If one side unilaterally died, their camp collapsed, and one-sided massacres and pursuits followed.

That was why a crushing defeat in murim was when one side lost more than 30% of its troops. This was because a side that lost 30% of its troops could no longer maintain its morale.

But...

“Don’t make me laugh, you bastards of the Evil Sect!”

“I am still alive!”

What were these people?

Although countless people had already fallen as corpses, their fighting spirit didn’t fade from their eyes. They really seemed determined to fight to the death until the last person died.

A man with his legs cut off, and his stomach stabbed still swung his sword as he fell to the ground, and a man with his throat half-cut still desperately grabbed the enemies with failing arms, blood splashing around.

‘Are all the Justice Factions like this?’

How could that be?

If all the Justice Faction fought like this, the name of the Evil Faction wouldn’t even exist in the world. No, one wouldn’t even need to go that deep. The situation where the Four Precepts Alliance emerged in the Yangtze River probably wouldn’t have happened.

Even the famed sects negotiated with the Evil Sects because they were afraid of losing their lives, so why on earth would they risk their lives fighting here?

“How odd.”

But that wouldn’t make a difference. They weren’t causing damage here. At best, it was just a waste of time.

“There must be quite a few who could have survived if they ran away while we were wasting time. So stupid.”

Gal Cheon-rip looked at the brutal battlefield with an obvious sneer.

Kaka!

Iron Ghost’s face contorted.

The moment he was about to smash the head of the guy in front of him, five swords flew at him at the same time.

“Kuak!”

Iron Ghost took a step back and swatted the swords flying towards him. Since he had learned physical skills, he could handle swords with his body without being wounded, and indeed, he didn’t get hurt.

‘So persistent.’

The dynamics changed after that beggar jumped in a little while back.

No, to be exact, it was after the name Mount Hua came out of the beggar’s mouth.

From then on, these people didn’t even blink an eye, even when someone was screaming and dying next to them. They kept biting, hanging on, and rushing at him.

The battle was truly odd. If one side rushed in without regard for their own life, it seemed like the outcome would be quick, but it didn’t actually happen that way. No one wanted to give up an arm in exchange for cutting off the head of a lesser-skilled opponent.

“DIEEEEE!”

At that moment, a sword strike from a disciple of that damn Hwayoung Clan flew towards his face. Among these insane people, the ones wearing the robes were the most insane.

“Insane...”

Iron Ghost mumbled in between, swatting away the sword flying towards him with the back of his hand, then stabbed his eagle-like fingers into the chest of the Hwayoung Clan member.

Crack!

“Kuak...”

The moment he clenched his hand and tried to tear off the flesh, something flew towards his neck.

Iron Ghost pulled his hand out like lightning and struck the flying sword.

Crack!

Red blood gushed out like a fountain. But even though his insides were touched, he didn’t back down at all and swung his sword.

Kuak!

Iron Ghost’s sword split slightly, revealing red flesh.

“...”

Iron Ghost’s eyes shook a bit, and he stepped back, looking down at his shoulder.

Hwayoung Clan leader Wei Lishan, who staggered and took a stance, forcibly calmed his trembling legs and aimed his sword.

Iron Ghost let out a hollow laugh. Looking at Wei Lishan’s appearance, with blood spreading from all the cuts on his body, he didn’t even feel angry.

“... a man who is dying.”

“But I am still alive.”

“Clan leader!”

“Clan leader! Step back! We will deal with this man!”

The Hwayoung clan disciples screamed, but Wei Lishan didn’t move as if he couldn’t hear them.

“...if you want to kill my disciples, you will have to go through me first. That is the law of Mount Hua.”

“You are good. If your sword had as much strength as your mouth, that would have been better.”

“I have the strength to bite on and endure.”

“...it seems like Mount Hua teaches you how to work your mouth first.”

At the sarcastic remark, Wei Lishan smiled.

“Well, it isn’t so wrong.”

“What?”

“Come at me, you bastard. If you cannot even handle my sword, you will not be able to handle one of Mount Hua’s third-class disciples.”

“Ah, really?”

Iron Ghost rushed in and struck Wei Lishan. At that moment, Wei Lishan swung his sword sharply and struck the shoulder, but the sword didn’t even penetrate an inch.

Instead.

Woong!

The moment Iron Ghost’s shoulder, like an angry bull, struck Wei Lishan’s chest, his chest sank. Wei Lishan fell down, coughing blood.

Thud!

“CLAN LEADER!!”

“T-this fucking bastard to our clan leader!”

“Get back!”

A harsh rebuke was poured out towards Hwayoung, who was unable to contain their anger and was about to rush in. The disciples looked at the fallen Wei Lishan with trembling eyes.

Wei Lishan pushed himself off the ground with a trembling arm and struggled to get up. Black blood flowed from his mouth.

“...I am still alive...”

He raised his sword and aimed it at Iron Ghost again.

“I will have to... get over this. You bastard.”

“Hahahah! Nice! Very nice!”

Iron Ghost burst into laughter and charged at Wei Lishan.

Wheeing!

The sound of the spear tip cutting through the air was like a ghost’s howl.

With this creepy sound of wind, the sharp spear struck the end of the club and dug into the chest of Hong Dae-Kwang.

Puak!

The blade pierced more than half an inch deep. Then, the spear was quickly drawn back and, this time, plunged into the other side of Hong Dae-Kwang.

Crack!

His side was split so deeply that the bone was visible.

“Kuaak!”

Despite being so severely wounded, Hong Dae-Kwang showed no sign of retreating.

He cut his side, grabbed the spear being pulled back, and swung his club at the man.

“Uh?”

Spear Ghost, with a shocked expression, pulled the spear hard. The spear blade cut Hong Dae-Kwang’s hand and was pulled back, causing the club to bounce off.

Tat!

Although he had inflicted a one-sided wound, it was Spear Ghost who had to retreat. He glared at Hong Dae-Kwang with a very displeased look.

‘Did I say I wanted to see if he could still talk with holes in his body?’

The results were clear. Hong Dae-Kwang was proving he could still talk despite the holes in his body.

Shoulders, chest, stomach, and legs.

Even with nearly ten holes the size of a child’s fist in his body, Hong Dae-Kwang didn’t fall. Instead, he laughed.

Even though his rags were turning red, not to mention the blackness around.

“Running away... are you too scared?”

“... insane bastard.”

“Huh... you don’t seem to know a beggar well... a person who has nothing has nothing to fear from death.”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes shone.

And Spear Ghost clicked his tongue watching that.

It was hard to finish this man properly when a weakling rushed to die. As the beggar was the one who took the lead to tie him down by the ankles, the others began to gain motivation to fight.

Surely that motivation didn’t turn the tide. But something was noticeably awkward compared to before.

“In other words...”

Spear Ghost smiled oddly.

“If I kill you brutally, their morale will all drop. Right, try to endure it till then. Ten holes you can handle, but twenty? What about thirty?”

“...”

“Let us see if their expressions will be the same when you die so miserably without a single drop of blood left in your body.”

His spear, moving toward Hong Dae-Kwang, shone brightly.

Kaang!

The spear he swung had hit a heavy club and bounced off. The sword that deflected his spear flew in without losing momentum, scraping his neck.

He felt a sharp pain in his neck, but he didn’t have time to check how deep the cut was. The moment he lost consciousness, there would be no need to check how much his neck was cut.

‘I am helpless now.’

A line of blood flowed down from his clenched lips.

‘If only I were a bit stronger...’

Even as he retreated from the spears and swords of those whose names he didn’t even know, another life was being lost.

If only he had been stronger, couldn’t he have stopped them? Couldn’t he have saved people? Why didn’t he devote himself to training more? Why wasn’t he as strong as they were?

Even if it meant stepping into Mount Hua, he had to be as strong as them. Wei So-Heng understood at that moment those without strength could not protect anything.

“ACKKK!”

‘Helpless...’

Another life, as clean as silver, was lost. The ground they stepped on was already bright red.

Who could be the owner of all that blood?

It belonged to those who risked their lives to protect this place. Their dazzling determination and bravery were all powerless against these evil people.

If he had formally stepped into Mount Hua and learned their techniques, would things have been any different?

Clank!

His sword, which had blocked the flying sword, bounced up. Then, he was kicked in the chest by a foot.

Kuung!

With a shock that felt like the world turned upside down, Wei So-Heng was thrown back, coughing blood.

“Young leader!”

“Damn it! Young leader!”

He fell headfirst to the ground and clenched the dirt. The terrible pain in his chest and head was more agonizing than the smell of dirt soaked with blood.

Wei So-Heng, who was squirming, flipped over again and struggled to raise his upper body.

“ACK!”

He was dying.

“Ahh! ACKK! ACKKK! You fucking bastards!”

Those who had been struggling to hold on were also dying, leaking blood. The wide-open eyes of the fallen ones, the sadness in their eyes that he couldn’t even feel in death, seemed to tear down the heart of Wei So-Heng.

“AHH....”

Hot tears began to drip from Wei So-Heng’s eyes.

“Young leader.”

Hwang Jongi, who had come running to him, grabbed Wei So-Heng by the shoulder and supported him.

“Are you alright, young leader?”

But Wei So-Heng didn’t hear a single word from Hwang Jongi’s mouth.

’AHH...“

Everyone would die. Everyone.

Those who laughed together, those who fought and got angry together, those who competed with each other. Those who were like family and sometimes like enemies will all die here.

Suppressing the anger and sadness welling up in his chest, Wei So-Heng clenched his teeth and got up.

He knew.

That he would die here.

But...

‘If it was Taoist Chung Myung, he wouldn’t have cried until the moment he actually died.’

Wei So-Heng, covered in tears, gripped the sword tightly. Now, he seemed to understand it—how much he had carried. How much fear he had fought in.

Wei So-Heng’s eyes, holding the sword, saw someone’s blood spilling into the sky. The red blood seen through his blurred vision looked like plum blossoms.

He staggered as if he would collapse at any moment, then struggled to straighten himself up. He grabbed the sword with trembling hands and bit down on his lip until it bled.

‘I...’

He saw Wei Lishan being stabbed by the enemy. Wei Lishan’s contorted face and blood dripping down his chest filled his eyes.

Crack.

With an eerie sound, a spear blade pierced the shoulder of Hong Dae-Kwang. The sight of Hong Dae-Kwang holding onto the spear and staggering pierced Wei So-Heng’s eyes.

‘I...’

“ACKKK!”

The scream of another came.

Even if he couldn’t stop it, he shouldn’t at least watch them die from behind.

“I am the young leader of the Hwayoung clan! You morons!”

Wei So-Heng once again raised his sword and charged ahead.

“Young leader!”

“YOUNG LEADER!”

Someone noticed and tried to stop him, but Wei So-Heng’s gaze was fixed on the opponent who was stabbing a disciple of their clan in the stomach right in front of him.

“AHHHHH!”

He rushed forward, feeling a burning sensation in his chest, and swung his sword with all his might.

A swift and splendid sword movement.

The most powerful move Wei So-Heng had ever executed in his entire life collided with the opponent’s careless swing and was distorted.

Clang!

The sword tore through his hand, spun around, and soared into the sky.

‘Ah...’

The world slowed down for a moment.

The sword, which had been moving quickly, appeared too slow to his eyes.

And then.

He could clearly see the rough sword flying towards his chest, which was exposed due to his arms being spread wide.

And someone’s scream.

Sweat ran down his face.

Trembles he felt from his scarred hands.

And even more clearly, the smell of death.

‘I... I was no coward.’

Wei So-Heng closed his eyes, accepting the pain in his chest and the death that would soon follow.

‘Taoist...’

Was it because he wanted revenge that the Taoist’s smiling face came to his mind at the last moment? Or was it because he missed seeing him?

When he met that man in the afterlife, would he be able to say he wasn’t a coward till the end?

The road to death was a long one.

He still couldn’t tell if the sword had cut through his chest or if his thoughts were lingering despite death approaching him.

It was then.

Tak.

The feeling of something lightly touching his head instantly pulled Wei So-Heng back to reality.

A slightly large, warm hand.

“You did good, kid.”

Wei So-Heng opened his eyes.

What was in front of him wasn’t the sword that pierced his chest or the person holding it.

It was the huge shoulders of someone in black robes.

“Uh...”

Wei So-Heong’s eyes grew wide, and soon he spoke.

“C... Chung...”

How could he not know?

Those shoulders. That person.

Wei So-Heong bit his lips tightly. He felt like he would burst into tears if he said one more word.

Even so, the words he couldn’t hold back finally escaped his lips.

“... Taoist.”

The back of the person he had wanted to see so badly.

Chapter 874: How Do You Want To Die? (3)

Wheeing!

The spear of the Spear Ghost of Mount He, flying in with a whistling sound like the wind, pierced his upper chest. Blood poured from the wound.

Hong Dae-Kwang struck the spear with the club in his hand, but the force he exerted was weaker than that of a child. The light in his eyes was almost gone. It was the sight of someone pushed beyond their limit.

“Kuekuk.”

The Spear Ghost snorted softly and withdrew the spear.

“You stupid beggar!”

It was praiseworthy that this fat man was standing on his own legs despite so many wounds. It was worthy of applause, and this man was swinging his club, too, saying he would fight even if he was close to death.

But even so, it was a meaningless struggle.

“Haah!”

The Spear Ghost’s spear created a dozen or so holes and tore through Hong Dae-Kwang’s chest as if it were a joke. Hong Dae-Kwang flinched back, dancing, unable to resist properly with his body drained of qi.

“It is the end.”

Tired of playing around, the Spear Ghost struck a final blow. His spear, filled with fierce spear qi, aimed at Hong Dae-Kwang’s neck in one swift motion.

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had reached his limit, didn’t react at all, even as he watched the spear flying in to cut him down. The Spear Ghost, not doubting the outcome, glanced around for his next prey.

‘Next will be...’

At that moment.

Kwaang!

An excruciating pain surged through the hand holding the spear, and his body was thrown back.

‘What?’

Thud!

The Spear Ghost, who barely managed to keep his balance by slamming the spear into the ground, looked ahead.

A man had suddenly appeared, blocking the gap between him and Hong Dae-Kwang.

‘Just when?’

He hadn’t even sensed any presence.

The moment he turned his gaze, convinced of his opponent’s death, this man had jumped in and blocked the spear?

This young man?

The Spear Ghost looked at his opponent with cold eyes. His survival instinct, developed through countless battles, was trying to understand the opponent’s capabilities first.

Those black robes.

A thin sword in one hand.

A face befitting the word young.

But what caught his eyes before anything else were the numerous scars on the arms, visible through the slight reveal from the sleeve.

Those scars, drawn horizontally and vertically as if intentionally carved, covered his arms like spider webs.

At that moment, he felt goosebumps rise.

‘That is...’

He had seen that a few times before.

Wanderers who roamed the world looking for fights. Even among them, those who were worthy of the term, simply missed, there were those who survived for an uncanny amount of time, and their hands ended up like that.

Those hands resembled those of people who had been through countless battles and crossed countless lines of death.

The man who had intervened then slowly spoke, taking his eyes off.

“Are you alright?”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had barely come back from the brink of death, looked at the person standing in front of him as if he had forgotten everything that had happened to him. His expression was like he was in a dream.

“... Gul.”

His dry mouth wouldn’t speak right, so he tried to speak numerous times.

A familiar yet unfamiliar solid back. Hong Dae-Kwang thought of one person he knew.

“... Taoist Jo Gul?”

Jo Gul turned his head slightly and looked at Hong Dae-Kwang with a slight smile.

“It has been a while.”

“Ah...”

Hong Dae-Kwang blinked his eyes several times as if he couldn’t believe it, and finally, his strength gave out, and he collapsed there.

Thud.

His face had collapsed and changed to numerous expressions. He bit his lips in anger and then sighed...

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had been expressing such complicated emotions, finally let out his anger.

“Damn it... should have... come sooner.”

“I am sorry. I came running the moment I heard the news.”

“No... it is good enough that you came now. Even now. Oh my...”

The face of Spear Ghost changed and distorted slowly.

‘These people?’

They were ignoring him and talking normally. How could he endure such humiliation?

“You people are ignoring me now?”

Just as he was about to speak more, Jo Gul turned to him.

Flinch.

The moment he saw those eyes without emotion, the Spear Ghost closed his mouth against his will.

In front of those eerie eyes, his instincts warned him that he shouldn’t speak any longer.

‘Did I just get scared by a kid?’

It was at that moment when he was confused by the sudden and incomprehensible situation.

“This bastard!”

He turned his gaze without realizing it at this animal-like howl coming from the side.

“Are you alright?”

“... Y-Yoon Jong, Taoist!”

Someone was supporting the Hwayoung clan leader, who had been fighting with Iron Ghost just a moment ago. And what happened? Iron Ghost, who was growling, didn’t dash in to fight?

‘Just what...’

Only then did Spear Ghost realize the strange flow happening through the battlefield.

The atmosphere had changed. Strange as it might seem, the entire feel of the battlefield had clearly shifted from a moment ago.

The only unusual thing was that two people, no, at most three people, had joined.

But the fierce advantage they had was stopped, and those who had been clawing and biting the surroundings without mercy were now growing distant and growling?

It seemed as if the wolves fighting over the land had discovered that a tiger had approached.

On the other hand, the faces of Xi’an warriors who confirmed the arrival of these three people were filled with overwhelming joy and relief. Some were even sobbing.

The Spear Ghost was dumbfounded by this.

There were just three people who joined them. How was he supposed to accept that the entire battlefield, which was boiling like a furnace, had stopped because of them?

“Who the hell are these people?”

“Euh.”

At that moment, the sword of the one standing in front of him moved lightly.

“What?”

By the time he realized it, the sword was already lodged in his shoulder.

“Kuak!”

The Spear Ghost jerked back involuntarily due to the intense pain in his shoulder. He immediately retreated more than a foot, and cold sweat began to flow down his forehead.

When he glanced at his shoulder, blood was gushing from the torn clothes. What he had experienced a moment ago was no illusion.

“...I couldn’t see anything.”

How could a sword swung by a human be so fast?

The Spear Ghost shuddered at the speed of the sword movement he had never seen before. Goosebumps rose from the tips to the crown of his head.

He was surprised that the one who had shown such speed with a sword technique was a young man who hadn’t even lived half as long as he had. He felt an unknown pressure as he faced the young man.

Step. Step.

The man called Yoon Jong, who had saved Wei Lishan, and Jo Gul, who had put a hole through his shoulder, stepped forward.

Under this suffocating force, the Spear Ghost desperately tried to stop himself from stepping back.

The Evil Sect people, who seemed unstoppable and against whom the defenders of Xi’an risked their lives to fight back, all stopped and blankly stared at those who stepped forward.

“These people...”

At that moment, something caught his eye, and his eyes opened in shock.

He belatedly discovered that the person approaching him had a plum blossom pattern engraved on his chest.

“M-Mount Hua...?”

It was the Mount Hua that these people continued to talk about. The Spear Ghost, whose bones went cold for a moment, spat out as if growling.

“Mount Hua Sect?”

The black robe and the red plum blossom pattern engraved on the chest were the symbols of Mount Hua.

But the reason he couldn’t immediately think of the name Mount Hua when he saw them was because the force they held felt too unusual.

Their aura was so different and odd for a Justice faction sect. And what about this suffocating killing intent?

This was why he couldn’t possibly think of them as Mount Hua when he saw them. He couldn’t imagine that a Taoist could exude a killing intent greater than someone from an Evil Sect.

‘Mount Hua? These people are...?’

That was when it happened.

“Stay back. You will get hurt.”

“T-Taoist, I...”

“Let us talk a bit later.”

A single voice resonated calmly and comfortably through the battlefield.

It was a slow voice that didn’t fit the battlefield of blood and death.

Step.

The owner of the voice slowly walked ahead.

Hair that was roughly tied up.

A slightly small body.

A black robe that fluttered with each step.

It wasn’t a particularly impressive sight.

Compared to Jo Gul, who exuded an aura as sharp as a blade, or Yoon Jong, who exuded an intimidating qi that suppressed his own, he seemed too normal.

But.

From the moment he heard the voice, the Spear Ghost couldn’t take his eyes off this one man.

A warning from his instinct. Those who had lived their entire lives in a world where the strong prey on the weak knew who the most dangerous person on the battlefield was.

And now his instinct was telling him:

This man was the most dangerous person there.

Step. Step.

He walked out slowly and then stopped.

Swish.

He slowly took in the dead and fallen people. His gaze didn’t move for a while, as if he was engraving it into his eyes.

None of those watching dared to look away from him.

Swish.

The man’s gaze shifted again. This time, it was to the injured and groaning people.

The time that passed while he silently moved his gaze wasn’t long, but it felt like the entire world had stopped. Like an eon had passed.

This was what they meant when they said it was a gaze that didn’t allow you to breathe.

Gulp.

The small sound of someone gulping was as loud as thunder. A frightened silence weighed down the entire place.

And finally.

The man’s gaze slowly returned.

Nothing had changed. His expression remained the same as before, and his eyes hadn’t altered either.

Yet, the sense of oppression felt by the Evil Sect members under his gaze was unlike anything they had experienced before.

The one who had witnessed the massacre, death, and suffering that had unfolded here, simply with his eyes, slowly opened his mouth.

“Pretty...”

Everyone held their breath, waiting for him to continue. It was as if it had been predetermined that it must be this way.

“...pretty good job you’ve done here.”

The man’s lips curled into a smile.

At that eerie smile, the hearts of the Evil Sect people sank.

“So now...”

He looked at all the Evil Sect members standing before him, scanning from left to right as if he were memorizing each of their faces.

“How do you want to die?”

As soon as he finished speaking, the battlefield turned ice-cold, as if the previous heat had been nothing but an illusion.

Chapter 875: How Do You Want To Die? (4)

“Father.”

Wei So-Heng supported Wei Lishan, who was staggering as if he might collapse at any moment.

He would have liked to lay him down on the ground to rest, but this was a battlefield. For the sake of the Hwayoung clan disciples, who were desperately holding on with trembling legs, Wei Lishan couldn’t collapse.

“...So-Heng.”

“Father. He is here... Taoist...”

“Right.”

Even in Wei Lishan’s voice, there was undeniable relief.

The situation hadn’t changed much.

The fangs of the wolves targeting them were sharp, and those who hadn’t yet fully filled their stomachs would once again come to bite them.

But even so, Wei Lishan was no longer worried.

That was the only way.

Anyone who knew the name of the person standing in front of him, who understood what kind of person he was, would feel the same way as Wei Lishan.

Standing on the edge of a cliff, Xi’an warriors who had been fighting with their lives at stake bit their lips when they saw this light standing in front of them.

“... Taoist Chung Myung.”

“Taoist...”

“Mount Hua’s...”

It was then that someone’s muttering made them realize it again.

“... Mount Hua has come.”

The echo of those words swept over those who endured the fight until then.

Just a few years ago, the name of Mount Hua had no meaning in Xi’an.

But now, it was entirely different.

Beyond Xi’an, in Shaanxi, the meaning of Mount Hua had become so great that nothing could replace it.

Those who were terrified of death, those who were panting because they didn’t have the strength to lift their swords, and those who desperately dragged their legs to escape—all began to burn with fighting spirit again the moment they heard the name, Mount Hua.

With just three people appearing for them.

‘This isn’t good.’

Gal Cheon-rip, who was watching this, clicked his tongue.

The situation had changed so much. The fact that the warriors of Xi’an were so tenaciously clinging to them could have been dismissed as nothing more than tantrums.

But it wasn’t like that. The atmosphere had changed because of the three people.

The bigger problem was that those who were running around like beasts were now holding their breath.

If this force waned any further, his plan might go up in smoke.

Gal Cheon-rip looked at the person standing in front of him with an uncomfortable look.

A man in a black robe with a plum blossom engraved on it was looking at them with his hands hanging down. The moment he faced the coldness in the eyes, a name came to his mind naturally.

A name so famous, but whose luster had now faded with the passage of time.

“Right.”

Gal Cheon-rip opened his mouth, looking straight at the man.

“Are you Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

The moment that name was mentioned, the entire Evil Sect reacted strongly.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“The... Yangtze River...”

The faces of the Evil Sect people turned pale.

They couldn’t even finish their words, but it was clear what they were talking about.

He had fought on equal terms with Jang Ilso.

Everyone here fled to avoid that man. There was no need to explain how much weight Jang Ilso, the leader of the alliance, held over them.

The leader of the Four Precepts Alliance, the undisputed hegemon.

The warrior who turned the South of the river into a land of blood and finally achieved his unification.

At one time, the title of one of the Shenzhou Five Sects was attached to his name. Still, now, three years after the Yangtze River incident, no one in the world called Jang Ilso with such a simple title.

So naturally, the name of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint couldn’t help but shine. In recent years, the only one who tarnished the name of Jang Ilso was Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

‘That man...’

‘The rumor?’

A tense atmosphere filled the room, but the man simply stared at Gal Cheon-rip, who had opened his mouth without saying a word.

“I guess I was right.”

When Chung Myung didn’t respond, Gal Cheon-rip laughed softly.

“This kid has become quite arrogant after gaining fame. Bastard. Would anyone believe that you are a warrior on par with Jang Ilso? I didn’t believe it before, but seeing you with my own eyes makes me more certain of it.”

At those words, the members of the Evil Sect stared at Chung Myung with wary eyes.

“Why? Do you have nothing to say?”

At that moment, Chung Myung, who had been silent, snickered and spoke.

“Ridiculous.”

“What is ridiculous? You are supposed to be as good as Jang Ilso...”

Gal Cheon-rip complained, but Chung Myung cut him off.

“A fucking moron from the Evil Sect is shaking his butt in front of me.”

“...”

The smile vanished from Gal Cheon-rip’s face.

“You...”

“Right.”

Chung Myung tilted his neck to the left and right as if he had no more intention of listening to the man.

“If the Evil Sect is going to show up in front of me, then okay. One has to be at least on the level of that moron Jang Ilso like you said. I can at least tolerate what that idiot has to say. But...”

A sneer appeared on Chung Myung’s face.

“Not you. You little bastard.”

“...”

“So shut your mouth. I have no interest in talking to those who will die.”

Gal Cheon-rip’s face contorted.

“Little bastard...?”

When in his life had he ever been called such?

What made him even angrier was that this little brat was treating him as lower than Jang Ilso. This was a sore spot for Gal Cheon-rip.

He clenched his fist and suppressed his anger, so furious that his hair was standing up. To show anger in front of all these people would be the same as admitting he was caught in the words of this kid.

He gritted his teeth and then spoke, trying his best to appear calm.

“That son of a bitch has a sharp tongue, I see. But you should keep your boasting in check. Even if you had the rumored skills, three people are nothing...”

“I said your fucking mouth stinks, so shut it.”

But this time, a cold voice cut him off again. Gal Cheon-rip’s face hardened to the point where he couldn’t remain calm.

“This little brat is messing around...”

“You seem to be mistaken.”

Chung Myung bared his teeth.

“I am holding back from wanting to tear you to pieces right now. So do not open your mouth. My patience has a limit.”

“This bastard!”

The eyes of Dam Hae, the youngest, were red with anger.

However, Gal Cheon-rip felt more shock than anger at Chung Myung’s words.

“You are holding back?”

“...”

“Why? What are you holding back for?”

“Simple.”

Chung Myung said with a sneer.

“I am not the one who is going to catch and kill you.”

“... what now?”

“And let me correct another misunderstanding.”

Chung Myung snorted and asked.

“Who said we were just three?”

And at that moment.

“It surely isn’t just three.”

Gal Cheon-rip hurriedly turned around at the clear voice behind him.

A handsome young man in a black robe was slowly walking past the gate of the merchant union.

The moment he saw him, Gal Cheon-rip’s body was unconsciously filled with tension.

The steps were neither too much nor too little. His breathing was neither fast nor slow. All of it indicated that this man had reached a level of controlling his body.

His angry gaze lingered on Gal Cheon-rip’s face for a moment.

“It isn’t that there are just three around.”

As soon as he said so, a group of warriors appeared behind him.

‘W-when? I couldn’t feel any movement?’

But that wasn’t all.

One by one, people in black robes climbed up the extremely tall walls around them.

The eyes of the Evil Sect people who saw this trembled as if they had experienced something unknown.

Were they surrounded?

Or was it because an unexpected enemy appeared?

No, the reason they were panicking was the sharp force of those who surrounded them.

‘Just where did these people come from...?’

It wasn’t the heavy and gentle pressure of the Justice faction. It felt as if they would cut themselves if they touched anything... no, just meeting them felt like blades piercing their hearts, pressuring them.

In particular, what caught their attention was the woman standing in the center of those on the wall.

Excessive beauty.

And a cold, cold killing intent that covered even that beauty.

Those who faced such gazes, which held no trace of emotion, stepped back.

‘Sword Ghost...’

The killing intent they exuded was like a hundred sword ghosts standing around them.

The more skilled one was, the greater the pressure. Only those with experience dealing with such killing intent could understand how formidable they were.

‘This is...’

Cold sweat ran down Dam Hae’s forehead.

There were hundreds of those people...

‘Damn it. What is happening?’

Just looking at the plum blossom pattern engraved on their chests, it was clear.

Mount Hua Sect.

A famous sword sect from Shaanxi that once aimed for the title of the Best Sword Sect in the world.

But the current Mount Hua was by no means a famous sect. Didn’t everyone in the world know that the fame gained by Mount Hua wasn’t its own, but rather by the works of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?

But when Mount Hua actually encountered them, they shattered such thoughts from Dam Hae.

If he could, he would find all the people who said that Mount Hua itself was nothing special and slit their mouths.

But regret always came too late, and when he realized how wrong he was, it was after everything was done.

The vast expanse of Eunha’s mansion began to feel narrow now.

At that moment, those who had entered through the main gate of the manor and occupied the area behind them split to the left and right. Three old men walked between them.

“S-sect leader!”

“Sect leader!”

A scream, like a shout, burst from the mouths of the Hwayoung disciples.

The main sect leader of Mount Hua, Hyun Jong, walked in with a cold, hard look on his face as he surveyed the yard.

A rare anger welled up in the eyes of Hyun Jong as he saw the miserable corpses cooling down and the survivors who were still breathing, covered in blood.

“How dare...”

He clenched his fists and gritted his teeth as he shouted.

“Un Geom. Baek Cheon!”

“Yes! Sect leader!”

The two people whose names were called out immediately appeared.

The force that Hyun Jong exuded was tremendous. It wasn’t as intense as the killing intent of his disciples, but it was surely a mountain of wrath.

Even without the famous title of the sect leader of Mount Hua, no one would be able to ignore Hyun Jong now if they saw him.

“You dare invade Shaanxi.”

The moment the heavy voice of Hyun Jong fell, groans of pain escaped from the mouths of the Evil Sect people.

“Attack close friends of Mount Hua!”

Upon hearing those words, the disciples of the Hwayoung Sect and the Xi’an sects clenched their fists.

“IN this land of MOUNT HUA....”

After catching his breath, Hyun Jong firmly ordered.

“Make those who dare commit such heinous acts pay the price for what they did! Show them clearly what the wrath of Mount Hua is!”

“We accept the order!”

Clang! Clang!

As soon as the words fell, the disciples of Mount Hua drew their swords at once.

The sight of a hundred swordsmen, seemingly devoid of any emotion, drawing their swords at once, brought shivers down the people.

To those who had been waiting for them, it was an indescribable thrill and overwhelming feeling.

To those who had to handle them, it was suffocating pressure and fear.

“In the name of Mount Hua!”

A majestic voice burst from the mouth of Baek Cheon.

“Punish the evildoers!”

The disciples of Mount Hua, with their swords extended downwards, rushed at the Evil Sect people simultaneously. It was like a black wave rippling in the middle of Xi’an.

The movement declared to the world that Mount Hua had finally returned.

Chapter 876: Die Like A Bug (1)

Swords surged along a black wave. The sight of a hundred black-robed swordsmen rushing towards them as if they were one body, one mind, could only be described as pure intimidation.

The Evil Sect members, sensing something was wrong, shifted their eyes away in haste. When the opponent felt burdensome, the unique instincts of the Evil Sect people were displayed.

But there was no way to run. Mount Hua had entirely surrounded them and was closing in on them as if they wouldn’t spare a single ant.

“Uh...”

“T-this?”

In battle, momentum was half the battle.

With the appearance of Chung Myung, the Evil Sect members, who had lost the initiative, began to waver, not knowing what to do. It didn’t matter that their reputation in Murim was suddenly in tatters.

However, not everyone was like that.

“Look ahead, you morons! They are still kids!”

“Do not be afraid of little flesh and blood running wild!”

Those who flinched at those words checked the faces of the Mount Hua sect disciples rushing towards them.

Surely.

The black robes and thick killing intent obscured their thoughts, but upon closer inspection, the Mount Hua disciples were all kids who hadn’t even reached the age of 30.

No matter how talented Mount Hua was at doing such things, they wouldn’t be able to make these young swordsmen into experts.

“Destroy them!”

Someone’s shout lifted their fallen morale.

‘Damn it, it wasn’t unexpected, right?’

‘Right. We can step on Mount Hua at any time!’

Considering the fame and skills of those gathered, it was wrong to be afraid of Mount Hua appearing there. They gritted their teeth and clenched their weapons, recalling that fact once again.

They tried to ignore the obvious. In order to survive in the harsh world of martial arts, one should value their own intuition over the evaluations of the world.

If one could run, they had to fight. They raised their hopes and spirits and began to face the disciples of Mount Hua rushing towards them.

“They are still kids at the end of the day.”

Bong Pyung gripped the blade tightly.

Even if Mount Hua had regained their skills from a distant past that few could remember, it would be impossible for kids to transform into master swordsmen.

If such a thing were possible, it would be the Mount Hua sect, not Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, that would gain a reputation in the Yangtze River massacre.

“I will tell you your place, you little bastards!”

If they were scared to this extent, it would be a shame to be called the Lion’s Blood Blade, notorious in the Zhejiang region.

Bong Pyung displayed the fierce aura befitting his title. The best way to handle the inexperienced little bastards was to clench his hands with killing intent and speed.

“AHHHH!”

His blade began to emit blade qi. Blue, almost black, blade qi surged out like a storm.

‘First, kill the momentum of those kids rushing in!’

If only that momentum wasn’t there, these kids would be nothing. Anyone with a sense of thought would have no choice but to block the blade qi rushing towards them.

...but that was Bong Pyung’s biggest mistake.

The disciples of Mount Hua who rushed at him did so without hesitation, despite seeing the blade qi.

‘What?’

Had they lost their minds?

Bong Pyung was quite flustered. His eyes met with the Mount Hua Sect’s disciples through the swirling blade qi.

Creep.

The moment he saw that cold gaze, showing no anxiety or excitement, his heart froze.

And.

Paat!

A ray of sword qi flew through the storm of blade qi like a ray of light.

Kakaka!

There was no time to be surprised.

The burning pain came first. The tip of his hand holding the sword felt hot as if it were on fire, and then he clearly saw something appear in his vision.

Something slightly curved and long.

The moment he realized this unfamiliar yet familiar object was his own finger holding the blade, Bong Pyung’s eyes opened wide.

“Uh...”

But that wasn’t the end. The black snake-like qi had swept away his little finger and ring finger and climbed up his forearm.

Pat! Pat! Pat!

The ligaments in his wrist were cleanly cut. A long gash appeared on his forearm, and the tip of the sword was stuck in his elbow.

The whole process happened in the blink of an eye.

‘AUKKKK!’

A scream burst out from Bong Pyung’s mouth.

He wanted to let go of the blade because of the terrible pain in his arm, but that would be suicide. He suppressed the sharp pain and swung his blade at the disciples of Mount Hua who were around; he wanted to kill the kid.

But before his sword could even swing halfway, the disciple retrieved the sword he had stabbed and accurately blocked the attack with the tip of the handle.

Kaang!

And he perfectly utilized the recoil and stabbed the sword again.

Puaak!

“Kuaak...”

A pain as if he were being burned by a hot iron spread through his lower stomach. But before he could even groan in pain, the sword that had been retrieved cut Bong Pyung’s elbow, which had been caught by the sword that had been thrown back.

Crack!

He couldn’t even properly understand what was happening. The opponent’s sword was too fast for that.

All he could see was that this fluffy little brat was just one-sidedly stabbing him. And he did it without a change in his expression.

‘AHHHH!’

Bong Pyung screamed and tried to stab his blade. That scream didn’t even threaten the opponent.

Fear instantly consumed his mind. It was a scream to shake off the fear this young savage gave him without even changing his expression.

Pat!

The blade, extended with all his might, flew towards the face of the Mount Hua disciple with incredible speed.

The Mount Hua disciple’s head turned to the side just by an inch. Bong Pyung’s sword literally brushed past the face closely.

And at that moment, Bong Pyung noticed it.

Even though the blade was flying close to his face, that kid’s expression didn’t show a hint of emotion. He just stared at him, his cold eyes looking deep into his own.

Paat!

The red sword qi, which bloomed in an instant, cut through Bong Pyung’s extended arm.

Crack!

It was a fantastic sight.

To some, it might be nothing special. But it couldn’t be anything but significant for the person concerned to see his arm, which had been attached to his shoulder his entire life, detached and spinning around like a wheel.

“Uh...”

Slash!

Before he could even process his shock, both thighs were cut, with blood gushing out.

Slash.

He didn’t even allow Bong Pyung, who had his knees cut, to stand any longer.

But the man couldn’t fall. Before his bent knees could even touch the ground, a dozen sword strikes moved to his stomach and chest.

Slash. Slash! Slash! Slash!

The sword pierced one by one. Before he could even feel the pain of the first strike ripping through his flesh, the second one tore through his body, and then the third cut his bones.

All Bong Pyung could do was gape wide-mouthed, feeling the sensation of his chest being torn apart.

“Ugh...”

His body finally fell.

His half-severed knees touched the floor, and his torn stomach made him curl down awkwardly. He couldn’t even stop his head from hitting the ground because his arms were cut.

Bong Pyung knelt down as if apologizing and laid face down with his forehead on the ground. The cold metal touched his neck, and in an instant, an eerie feeling spread through his entire body as if it were shrinking.

’N-No...“

A scream burst from Bong Pyung’s mouth, who couldn’t help but know what that sensation was.

Slash.

But the sword that touched his neck cut it without a shred of hesitation. The cold metal cleanly sliced through his veins.

Red blood gushed from his cut neck. Having lost his arm, he couldn’t even hold his neck to stop the blood from flowing out.

Drrr...

Blood flowed back into his neck, and the metallic taste spread throughout his mouth. A terrible, trembling pain enveloped his entire body.

But Bong Pyung did nothing. With all his muscles torn, all he could do was writhe in agony.

The unprecedented pain dragged Bong Pyung to the gates of hell, yet his tenacious life wouldn’t grant him a clean death.

‘Kill... just kill me...’

This was too cruel. Even the Evil Sect didn’t kill so brutally. The Justice faction shouldn’t be doing this.

It was then.

A cold voice pierced Bong Pyung’s ears as he struggled in pain, begging for mercy.

“Die like a bug.”

The voice was so cold it made his body shiver.

“There is a death that is right for trash.”

Kwak Ho, who simply glanced at Bong Pyung, shook the dirty blood off his sword and charged ahead to his next opponent.

His white teeth, revealed between his lips, shone like the fangs of a hungry beast.

“E-eik!”

Ma Ryang, the Wolf’s Fang Axe, was shocked.

‘W-who the hell are these people?’

They were just young kids who were taunted easily. No matter how famous, they could not be experienced. Considering their age, it was normal for them not to even manage to deal with the Evil Sect.

But the reality Ma Ryang faced shattered his common sense.

Paat!

The flying sword twisted as it dug into his shoulder.

Ma Ryang desperately suppressed the scream that burst from his throat. The pain of the sword digging into his shoulder was nothing special. The real agony came when the sword twisted and tore through all his muscles.

It was a pain that made his body tremble instantly.

“T-this fucking dog!”

Ma Ryang’s axe descended upon the head of the Mount Hua disciple with fierce force. The disciple slightly raised his sword, seemingly intending to block the axe.

‘Moron!’

Ma Ryang secretly cheered.

The inexperienced young brat must have thought it was a normal axe, given how he tried to block it with a thin sword.

His axe alone weighed fifty pounds and was a heavy weapon. Even if that sword was made of the finest metal, it wouldn’t last long against his axe.

“DIEEE!”

His axe, infused with internal qi, fell with explosive force towards the raised sword. He intended to cut through both the sword and the disciple’s body at once.

But, at the very moment that the axe made contact, the sword twisted diagonally.

Ma Ryang was shocked.

His axe, flowing along the sword, ran down the slope. In the slowed world, he could clearly see the sword rippling and the axe slipping away unintentionally.

Tung.

When the axe met the tip of the sword, the sword was lightly thrown off the axe.

At that moment, Ma Ryang saw.

The tip of the sword.

The tip of the sword aimed at him was rapidly growing bigger and bigger.

‘N-No...’

Ma Ryang desperately twisted his head, but the tip of the sword still filled his world.

“NO...”

Puah!

The sword pierced Ma Ryang’s eye.

’K-Kuaak...“

Ma Ryang’s body twitched in pain as if he were having a seizure.

Takaa.

He heard something twisting and breaking. The sound coming from within his body, which shouldn’t have been making such noises, was more terrifying than the pain of his eye being pierced.

“Baek Sang.”

Before he could even question what those words meant.

Pat!

Ma Ryang’s head was cut off in an instant and soared into the air.

Ma Ryang’s head, spinning in the air, contained the fear and despair he had felt before his death.

Swish!

Baek Sang, who had thrown away his sword, coldly rebuked Ma Ryang’s body as it collapsed.

“You should at least know the name of the person who killed you before you die. It would be too much for trash like you, though.”

It was a voice that could no longer be heard by someone who was already dead.

Chapter 877: Die Like A Bug (2)

“Eik!”

So Jang-Gye’s face contorted horribly.

‘What the hell is with these people!’

The young swordsmen in black robes rushed in. Of course, they were not flustered by such situations. Everyone gathered here was experienced. This level of battle was something that happened often in the Southern lands.

What really flustered him was that the swords these people were swinging were sharp beyond normal sense.

On top of that,

Clash slash!

The clashing swords flowed down the blade of the sword and stabbed the back of his hand.

“Eik!”

So Jang-Gye groaned as if in pain and pulled his arm back in a hurry. However, the moment his sword was pulled back, his opponent’s sword, which had been aiming for the back of his hand, changed direction in an instant. Then, it created dozens of sword images and aimed at his upper body.

So Jang-Gye waved his blade and retreated again.

‘Nonsense!’

It was understandable that they were strong. Even though Mount Hua wasn’t a sect that belonged to the Nine Great Sects, it was now widely known that they once fought for the title of the Best Sword Sect in the world.

But this wasn’t simply a matter of discussing strength.

‘They are skilled!’

Their swords were shockingly skilled as if they had been swinging them for decades. When one thought they were going for the arm, the sword would move for their neck, and when they tried to block it, their knees would be cut.

It seemed like he was going to strike hard, but then he backed away and quickly stabbed, and the sword that had been flying to the face like a flash of lightning changed direction and moved to the chest.

“Kuak!”

In the end, blood gushed out from his chest after being cut long. So Jang-Gye, who screamed in pain, gritted his teeth.

“T-this... this fucking!”

He couldn’t understand it.

The sword technique itself was shocking. It felt like being toyed with from the start. What they demonstrated was closer to the illusion of the sword of the Evil Faction than the sword of the Justice Faction.

But what was even more shocking was the look in the eyes of the men wielding the sword.

It was as if they belonged to a monster that devoured people on the battlefield.

No matter how calm one is, it is normal not to maintain one’s usual composure on a battlefield where blood splashes before the eyes and people die incessantly. Naturally, the arms became stronger, and the sword technique focused more on striking.

But these people didn’t change their gazes at all, like veterans who had been through a hundred battles. Far from being excited, they held their swords perfectly.

‘What kind of training did they undergo to achieve something like this...’

But he didn’t have time to think any longer. The opponent’s sword flew toward his eyes.

“Kuak!”

So Jang-Gye’s face turned bright red as he barely avoided the sword by twisting his head.

“This fucking kid!”

Woong!

His blade emitted a reddish blade qi. No matter how incomprehensible the opponent was, he was still young. So he decided it was better to suppress him with force than to fight him clumsily.

As expected from someone who had been through a lot on the battlefield, his judgment in that split moment was right.

Kakang!

The sword and blade clashed with sharp sounds in the air.

‘This bastard!’

So Jang-Gye gritted his teeth and pressed down on the blade. A look of victory was close in his eyes.

No matter how cleverly the sword was swung, no matter how miraculous his sword technique was, it was still a technique of a young man. He couldn’t possibly beat him in terms of experience, so if he crushed the kid with force, the kid was bound to crumble.

“I will cut you into two...”

And at that moment, a faint smile appeared on the swordsman’s face.

‘What?’

So Jang-Gye’s eyes widened slightly.

Was he laughing at this situation now?

“This fool!”

So Jang-Gye felt goosebumps for a moment and pressed down with all his might. Even if he couldn’t split the blocking sword in two, he intended to crush the opponent’s body with the sword that was blocking the blade.

But...

Kakakang! Kakakang!

The sound of the sword and the blade rubbing against each other pierced the air.

Kakakak!

And then So Jang-Gye knew something was wrong. He couldn’t help but notice.

‘Why?’

That damned sword wouldn’t get pushed back.

He pressed down with all his might, with all his strength, but that thin sword blocking the path didn’t move back even by an inch. It stood firm in place, as if it were a stone statue, holding the path firmly.

The sword didn’t even move an inch. And the emotionless eyes beneath it.

It was surely he who was pressing down, but it was also he who felt burdened.

“Eu-euuuuuu!”

So Jang-Gye pressed down on the sword with all his might.

Kakakang!

The sword and blade continued to grind against one another, sparks flying. But that was all. There was a slight feeling of being pushed back, but the sword was still blocking his path.

And at that moment.

The lips of the damned Mount Hua disciple curled up, and at the same time, the sword facing the blade was pulled back with a bang.

The moment their tense body balance was broken, So Jang-Gye, who was pressing down on the sword with all his might, staggered forward as if being sucked in.

Kwaang!

And then, the Mount Hua swordsman, who had pulled the sword back, struck So Jang-Gye’s blade with a force incomparable to that of before.

“Kuak!”

So Jang-Gye, who had been thrusting forward, was pushed back without much thought.

The qi transmitted through the blade twisted not just his wrist but his shoulder, too. It felt as if he had been struck by lightning, and his entire arm went numb.

‘F-fuck!’

Was he being pushed? By strength?

No, it was more technique than strength. But in any case, the fact that he got pushed remained unchanged.

The incomprehensible situation made his head spin. Furthermore, the eeriness he felt from that guy made his angry blood run cold.

At that moment, a very small sound passed by So Jang-Gye’s ear as he absentmindedly retreated.

The faint ticking sound, like snow falling, pierced his ears clearly, even amidst the chaotic battlefield.

Swish!

So Jang-Gye opened his eyes wide.

The sword of the Mount Hua disciple seemed to shake lightly in the air, and at its tip, flowers like an illusion began to bloom.

‘N-no...’

He instinctively felt danger, but he wasn’t given the chance to rush forward as he stepped back to relieve the shock from the blade.

Swish!

The flowers that had bloomed in the air swayed as if hit by a wind and soon began to scatter petals in all directions.

‘T-this is...’

He had heard of it.

The sword that bloomed flowers.

A sword that once commanded the world but was now forgotten and a legend. Yet, at some point, the poisoned sword of Mount Hua began to make a name for itself again.

“P-plum blossom sword....”

Swish!

The blooming flower petals suddenly began to fly toward him.

“... fuck.”

Feeling the sensation of the thin plum blossoms digging into his body, So Jang-Gye let out a helpless sigh.

“Come together! Damn it, these guys are no joke!”

“Do not fight alone! Gather together!”

Becoming known within the Evil Sect was not as easy as it sounded. The more notorious you became, the more frequent the fights and the more work you had to do to maintain your reputation.

Those here had survived a dozen battles without ever having fought a battle of such a scale. They might have the eyes to see the grand battlefield, but their survival instincts were the best.

Those who saw the front people collapse made an instant judgment. Those who ignored the enemies as kids and fought alone were all bleeding.

Considering the fame they had, it was ridiculously fast and brutal.

‘One-on-one is a sure defeat now!’

It was absurd to have to make such a judgment, but they could not deny what they saw with their own eyes; at any given time, survival was important, not pride.

“Fight in groups of two or three!”

So, their conclusion was simple. Their opponents were definitely strong. But they had never experienced a melee like this. They thought that if they gathered together and pressured the opponents, the battlefield would become entangled. At this point, they would be at a greater advantage since they could swing blades behind their backs.

It was a great conclusion for them to come to in such a short time.

The only mistake they made was that they didn’t know who they were dealing with.

Kaang! Kaang!

Sword and sword. Sword and blade. Sword and spear.

After confirming that their opponents were formidable, the Evil Sect warriors tried to focus on blocking the sword of their opponent. They had already confirmed that if they made a fool of themselves against those swords, which dazzled people’s eyes, their necks would get cut off.

‘How is this!’

But at that moment, things that had never been imagined before began to happen.

Kakang!

The moment the sword and the blade met, this incredible force gushed out from the sword and twisted their weapons.

It wasn’t some simple strike. The sword, which had been glued to the blade as if stuck, instantly pulled their weapons to the side.

‘How?’

Just as they were about to wonder how this was being done.

Paaat!

Right under the swordsman’s arm, which twisted the blade, a tremendous amount of sword qi flew in.

“Kuak!”

The frightened ones tried to pull their bodies back, but the sword attached to the blade refused to let them withdraw their weapons.

“Ack!”

“ACkk!”

The color drained from the face of Sima Ho.

“T-this is insane... bastards...”

It was an absurd act that no one could have imagined.

The fact that the sword qi flew from behind as soon as the guys in front twisted their arms meant that the ones who stabbed their blades from behind had moved before those who twisted the sword.

The guys in the back thought that the ones ahead would naturally handle the attacks and swung their weapons.

If they had been even a little off, the sword qi wouldn’t have cut their arms but their comrades’ arms!

‘This is insane.’

The people who swung their swords without hesitation or the ones who encouraged others to swing the sword qi at themselves without hesitation.

These kids were out of their minds.

But the effect was incredible. First, those who tried to endure and change the situation fell back, leaking blood.

At that moment, the swordsmen behind them jumped over the heads of those in front and scattered sword qi.

“S-stop!”

“Damn it! What the fuck is happening!”

Those who were wounded here and there swung their weapons in a hurry without even thinking about tending to their wounds. Wasn’t it obvious what would happen if they couldn’t block the sword qi?

But the connection didn’t end there.

“Ackkk!”

“My feet!”

Those in front began to drop low and hold their legs.

A blow above the head and another low to the ground.

It was definitely not easy to block sword qi flying from two places at the same time. A combination of high and low, rather than left and right, and this was deadly with their sword qi.

‘H-how?’

They didn’t even look at one another. Not a single talk between the kids, not a single signal either.

But those insane brats were perfectly coordinated as if they had already done this before and had been performing together for years. As if they were one.

“T-this makes no sense! You damned bastard!”

It was at that moment that Sima Ho, who let out this explosive roar, saw something strange.

The ones who had been chasing them suddenly changed direction and moved to the left and right.

‘What?’

This absurd act managed to snatch away the victory the guy had secured.

Sima Ho just stared at the sight.

Swish!

And then he saw it.

At the tip of the swordsman’s sword, flowers bloomed beautifully, just like the pattern on his chest.

Those who had no choice but to retreat due to the momentum simply stared blankly at the plum blossoms blooming again. Now, there was nothing they could do. Even though they knew full well how terrifying the blooming flowers could be.

“... Mount Hua.”

The vivid flower petals were flying everywhere they could see. Sima Ho realized that each and every leaf and petal was made of sword qi and let out a hollow laugh.

‘You can deal with Mount Hua at any time? That insane bastard Gal Cheon-Rip...’

If there were a hundred people who used such an insane sword technique, that place would be no different from hell beyond the underworld. Their fates were determined from the moment they stepped into this hell.

The blooming flower petals flew in at once.

‘How beautiful.’

The Plum Blossom Rain, which filled the sky, began to pour down mercilessly, digging into the bodies of the wounded men.

Chapter 878: Die Like A Bug (3)

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes widened.

Pain throbbed from the wounds all over his body, but at that moment, he didn’t even feel it.

“... what the hell.”

Mount Hua’s disciples were one-sidedly attacking the Evil Sect members?

They were striking the enemies with their sword qi like lightning, using perfect coordination to overwhelm them. Mount Hua’s special qi, the Plum Blossom Sword qi, was blooming in all directions like beautiful, gorgeous flowers.

“Oh my...”

Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t help but be shocked as he watched the Evil Sect members helplessly fall, dripping blood.

He was prepared to die, pinning all his hopes on Mount Hua. But wasn’t it usually difficult to turn an expectation into reality?

In truth, what he really expected was for Mount Hua to arrive early and join hands with them to fight on equal terms. He had never imagined that Mount Hua would attack them on their own and single-handedly dominate like this.

For Hong Dae-Kwang, who knew every detail about who and what kind of people those Evil Sect members were, even having expectations felt absurd.

But right there, right before his eyes, something he had never expected had turned into reality.

‘What on earth have they been doing for the past 3 years, these crazy bastards...’
