The faces of those who had been young and innocent had now transformed into the faces of seasoned swordsmen.

Flutter!

They pushed away their opponents with incredibly quick swords and deflected their opponents’ counterattacks with skillful, soft sword movements. And when the distance was just right, the Mount Hua Sect’s illusion bloomed splendidly.

“This is Mount Hua...”

Mount Hua, who once pushed away even the Wudang and took the title of the best sword sect in the world, was now roaring at the world from there.

Clench.

Hong Dae-Kwang clenched his fists. His face was hot, and something surged through his chest.

Unable to hold back the overwhelming emotions, he shouted loudly.

“You are doing good! Beat them! Those damned bastards!”

“...”

Blood drained from the face of Gal Cheon-rip due to this unexpected situation.

“Big bro!”

“....”

“Big bro! Shouldn’t we do something?”

“...”

“Big bro!”

Gal Cheon-rip flinched for a moment and looked around. Dam Hae was watching him, waiting. Not just him, but his other younger brothers who surrounded and protected him looked anxious since Mount Hua arrived.

“If we leave, we will fall apart!”

Gal Cheon-rip bit his lip.

‘Damn it.’

With red eyes, he scanned the battlefield. That kid... no, now the Mount Hua swordsmen, who couldn’t even be called kids anymore, were one-sidedly slaughtering the best warriors he had brought.

The Evil Sect, overwhelmed by the momentum and unable to hold their ground, got pushed back further and further, retreating.

It was a sight that couldn’t be understood.

‘How could this happen...’

The situation should have been the opposite.

No matter how much they were the disciples of a known sect, those here were not ones to be crushed by such fame. Rather, those young kids who had never experienced a battle properly should have been crushed by the weight of the battlefield, where people’s heads were blown off, and arms were cut off right in front of them.

If one couldn’t withstand the weight, wasn’t a battlefield a place you wouldn’t be able to even display half your abilities?

But...

“Eik!”

“G-get back! Those damn petals are moving again.”

“ACK!”

The ones who were lost in thought and unable to display their abilities were the Evil Sect.

No matter how much they thought about it, this made no sense.

Those who gained fame and skill through countless battles in the world of murim for decades were being defeated so miserably by young people who hadn’t even lived half their lives.

On top of that, those evil-looking Mount Hua people didn’t let their guard down at all while watching the scene; they continued to release their strange sword qi.

‘Is this a prestigious sect?’

No, it couldn’t be.

If all the Justice faction sects were capable of this, there would have been no such fierce division between the Justice and Evil factions. This wasn’t the power of a Justice faction but the power of Mount Hua.

‘We lost too much momentum at first. Those damned bastards!’

At the first moment of the clash, several people were beheaded before they could even raise a hand.

It was this cruel hand that went beyond boldness.

Even though it was a temporary alliance, those who saw their comrades being brutally treated right in front of them were naturally terrified...

Gal Cheon-rip, who had been anxiously lost in thought, suddenly was wide-eyed.

‘Isn’t this what we were going to do?’

“This..!”

He bit his lips tightly to suppress the anger.

“Big bro!”

Another anxious voice came.

Gal Cheon-rip gathered his mind. This wasn’t the time to analyze things. Once they lose the momentum, things would become really uncomfortable if they didn’t recover.

He shouted loudly, using his experience to help.

“Iron Ghost! Blood Blade Killer! Mount He Spear Ghost! Soul Breaking Killer! How long will you be watching this!”

There was only one way to revive this broken momentum: to crush them with more force. The force of the battlefield always changed with the actions of a few skilled warriors.

“You people join in! Before this gets out of hand, let those little bastards know how high the sky is!”

“Yes! Big bro!”

Kang Seo-Chil, who was guarding him, quickly rushed ahead.

“Damn it...”

Sweat dripped down Gal Cheon-rip’s palms.

This wasn’t going to happen. All he had to do was clean up Xi’an, empty the vaults of Eunha merchants, and head to Qinghai.

Then why did things get so messed up?

‘No! Not yet!’

But it’s not that bad yet. If he just took care of those damned people, everything would go according to plan.

‘Absolutely!’

The sight of those flying in front of him was clear in the eyes of Gal Cheon-rip.

Paat!

The tip of a sword dug into a shoulder like lightning.

‘Ack!’

A terrible scream burst from the mouth of the person whose shoulder was pierced in an instant. At that moment, Mount Hua’s swordsmen twisted and pulled out the sword that was stabbed in the shoulder, then stabbed him again without a moment’s hesitation, slamming it into the opponent’s dantian.

“Kuaak...”

The person clutching his lower stomach had despair in his eyes. If his dantian was destroyed, he could never live as a warrior again. This was a punishment worse than death for him.

Thud.

The one who was kneeling fell forward.

The foot of Jin Tae, the third-class disciple of Mount Hua who subdued the opponent, dug into the opponent’s chest.

Kwang!

He kicked the man backward and caught his breath.

The smell of blood rushed to his nose. In addition, the heat from the bodies struggling to live, the screams, the cracking sounds of weapons—he felt everything vividly with his body.

But his eyes only grew calmer with each moment.

Swish!

At that moment, a blue-green qi of a blade flew toward his head. Jin Tae, who confirmed the blade qi rushing, looked at the ground.

‘Nope.’

This was all he had to handle. His back was supported by the sahyungs.

He grasped the situation clearly and instead jumped ahead. The blade was originally a weapon that crushed the opponent with strength.

But,

Kaang!

The tip of the plum blossom sword that Jin Tae swung hit right near the handle of the blade, causing it to fly. The moment the blade bounced back, the opponent’s face was filled with shock.

So easy.

There was no reason to engage with someone relying solely on brute strength. If you targeted the point where their strength was misapplied, both the blade and the person could be deflected even with a slender sword.

As soon as a weapon lost its strength, a series of incredible stabs followed, utilizing qi.

‘This isn’t it!’

A battlefield where blood splashed and flesh was torn?

This was hardly worth noticing.

The training they had undergone was a hundred times more intense and brutal than this.

Killing intent?

For someone who had faced that bastard’s killing intent head-on, this was barely a tickle to their instincts. He didn’t even sense any hostility from those they considered opponents.

So why be afraid?

He was a survivor who lived even after dealing with that damned bastard. To him, their momentum was so gentle it made him yawn, and their attacks were easily identifiable.

Jin Tae tried to calmly sever the opponent’s tendons, but then.

At that moment.

Paat!

A sword flew towards his face.

“Euk!”

Jin Tae’s waist snapped back.

‘When?’

He confirmed there were no other enemies around. The sahyungs wouldn’t have missed their opponent, so where on earth did this sword come from?

The sword, which passed right in front of his face, spun in the air and then descended towards his neck like a poisonous snake pursuing its prey.

A desperate situation.

But Jin Tae didn’t panic. He swung his sword upwards, even though he was off balance. The tremendous impact of the sword meeting the sword made his shoulders tremble.

“Kuak!”

Even though he had used only half his strength, it wasn’t easy to handle the impact. So he had to acknowledge it. This was different from those he faced so far. Jin Tae quickly spun his body and tried to escape the opponent’s range.

But.

Swish!

Once the opponent’s sword gained momentum, it wouldn’t release him so easily. It persistently pursued him, trying to prevent him from adjusting his form.

“This...!”

Jin Tae gritted his teeth.

He instinctively felt that this could be dangerous if he moved clumsily, so he prepared to sacrifice one side and steadied his sword.

It was then.

Kwang!

Something flashed before his eyes, and the sword pursuing him was knocked back. Jin Tae glanced at the person who stood in front of him. For a moment, relief spread across his face.

“I will handle this guy, sahyung.”

Jin Tae nodded without a word.

In Mount Hua, what was important wasn’t the ranks, but skill. It was natural that the stronger one fought the stronger opponent.

And the one standing in front of him was definitely stronger, even though they joined later.

“Hmm.”

The evil sect person flicked the sword as if his wrist was tingling and looked at the Mount Hua swordsman in front of him with a slightly tense expression.

The force of the sword and a stable form without disturbance.

She was the epitome of a swordsman.

“As someone who uses the same sword as a weapon, let us at least exchange names. I am the Nine Hold Swordsman, Hwang Seong. What is your name?”

“I don’t have a name to tell you.”

“...”

At that moment, a bright light shone in Hwang Seong’s eyes.

“This damned woman...”

The Mount Hua swordsman raised her sword and aimed it at his neck. Hwang Seong clenched his teeth at the cold and sharp force.

“Someone who uses the same sword?”

A cold voice, like the wind of the North Sea, escaped her lips.

“That is an insult to my sword. The sword you use is not the same as the sword I use.”

“....”

“Know this and die. This is the sword of Mount Hua.”

A proud swordsman of Mount Hua, with the spirit of Mount Hua raging from the tip of her sword.

Tang Soso, who had grown stronger over the three years, charged at the swordsman with her sharp gaze.

Chapter 879: Die Like A Bug (4)

Kakakang!

Hwang Seong’s face twisted in pain.

Paat!

“Kuak!”

The sword qi, like lightning, swiftly swept past his face. The skin on his chin split open, creating a long, red gash from chin to cheek.

‘Damn it!’

This was not a sword technique learned half-heartedly. It was a technique mastered by a swordsman who had swung the sword so often that they couldn’t tell if the sword was in their hand or an extension of it.

How could he accept that a woman, who appeared much younger than him, could wield sword qi that made his own sword seem insignificant?

“This..!”

As more wounds appeared on his body, Hwang Seong’s face showed signs of exhaustion.

Whoever this damned woman was, the fact remained that she was cornering him and inflicting slashes.

He let out a roar and swept his sword horizontally. The fiercely rising sword qi surged forward like a torrent of water pouring from a broken embankment.

“Take this...”

Prrr!

But then his eyes widened.

Suddenly, red flower petals bloomed before him like clouds, covering the entire area where his sword qi extended. The sword qi, not reaching its full length, collided with the flowers and was pushed back a long way.

“Kuak!”

Unable to move forward or backward, his internal qi turned his insides upside down. Blood gushed from his throat.

‘Damn it!’

This couldn’t be happening.

He was a famous swordsman, renowned throughout the world since before the young woman in front of him was born. Was he being pushed back in a sword fight and spitting blood?

This was absolutely impossible. Absolutely not.

“This fucking bitch!”

Hwang Seong pulled his sword back slightly. Soon, seven rays of black sword qi began to spread out.

An incredibly quick sword strike that sent victims to the afterlife without them even realizing their own death.

It was the moment when the poisonous sword technique, Deadly Twelve Thunders, was unleashed.

But.

Tat!

As soon as the sword was drawn, Tang Soso kicked the ground and stepped back. The sword of Hwang Seong, which had been rushing ahead at incredible speed, was withdrawn just an inch before Tang Soso’s neck.

‘What...’

The Nine Hold Swordsman glared.

Coincidence? Or not?

‘This is ridiculous.’

That young woman had never seen his sword technique before. So how could she predict instantly how far the sword she was seeing for the first time would extend and retreat exactly that distance?

Was that possible? That too in the middle of such a fierce battle?

No, even if it was possible, wouldn’t her neck be pierced if her calculations were even a bit off? What kind of guts did she have to stop it?

‘Is she even in her right mind?’

At that moment.

The eyes of the swordsman who was pulling back the sword and the emotionless eyes of Tang Soso met for a moment.

Creep!

He felt goosebumps rise through his body for a moment.

The eyes, which didn’t contain a shred of emotion, were watching him like a snake hunting its prey. As if they wouldn’t miss even the slightest movement.

‘This... this...!’

Hwang Seong gritted his teeth and shouted.

He couldn’t admit that momentary chill down the spine came from a child’s eyes, and he couldn’t dare admit that a young swordsman had read his moves.

“DIEEEEE!”

As an evil scream burst from his mouth, 12 flashes came through from his sword.

Stronger, faster.

The sword attacks, which he mustered with all his might, turned into 12 lightning bolts and rushed to Tang Soso.

‘There is no use stepping back.’

He wasn’t going to suffer the same result again. If she decided to do the same thing, her throat would...

It was then.

Tat!

Tang Soso spun around on the spot and jumped up lightly. The sword qi pouring down towards her body collided with her sword, which she had briefly swung.

At that moment, Tang Soso used the recoil to fly higher.

‘Idiot!’

That was the worst move because they couldn’t move their body freely in the air.

‘I will pierce you like a skewer!’

He secretly cheered and tried to deploy the same sword technique on Tang Soso, who was rising into the air.

But.

Prrrr!

With a sound like a dragonfly flapping its wings, the red sword qi bloomed from the tip of her sword.

One flower, another flower, and soon a dozen flowers.

Hwang Seong forgot that he was about to unfold a technique and just stared at that sight.

The red flowers, which had bloomed in profusion, covered even the body of Tang Soso. The vast sky that he could see was covered by the flowers.

And.

Wheik!

The blooming flowers suddenly scattered and began to rain down on the world like rain.

Rain of red flowers.

“Sky...”

At that moment, a thought struck through his mind.

A symbol of the Sichuan Tang family.

A legendary martial arts technique, which was handed down in the Tang family, had unfolded here and looked like the sky was raining flowers.

“Rain of Thousand... Flowers.”

Amidst the flower rain, which poured down with nowhere to run, the vivid green threat on the tip of Tang Soso’s sword was briefly visible. A voice, as if groaning, came from his mouth.

“Tang family...”

The flower rain swept through his entire body mercilessly and dazzlingly. The entire sky was covered with flower petals.

“... damn it.”

Those were the last words he said in the world.

“Ackkkk!”

A desperate scream echoed through the air.

“That...”

Wei Lishan’s mouth was open.

The swordsmen of Mount Hua were fiercely attacking the enemies, who seemed to be three times stronger. He could clearly see those who got pushed back by Mount Hua’s sword were getting cut down.

“... oh my.”

This was the kind of time when the word one-sided would get used.

And the one-sided nature of the battlefield brought Wei Lishan more shock than joy.

He knew their skills best, having experienced them firsthand.

How could those who were fearsome enough to be called the Gods of death when he faced them directly get pushed back so helplessly?

Wei Lishan suddenly looked around.

As expected, the other sect leaders in Xi’an were also looking with the same bewildered expressions.

“M-Mount Hua...”

“Like that...”

It had to be so.

Even Wei Lishan, who was proud to be one of the people who knew Mount Hua’s strength best, couldn’t help but be shocked at the current appearance of Mount Hua. How much more so for the others?

Paat!

The red qi of Mount Hua’s sword technique filled the sky.

“That...”

Someone’s half-hearted voice trailed off.

“We should help too...”

Laughter erupted around them.

“Help that?”

“...n-no, even so...”

“With our skills, it would be more of a hindrance than help. Helping has to be at the right level, too.”

“...right.”

Everyone stared blankly at Mount Hua’s swordsmen. Some gulped. They couldn’t help but bite their tongues, watching the swords draw flowers in the air.

‘No matter what, I thought they would be difficult to compare with Southern Edge.’

The fundamental limitations of Mount Hua.

The fact that the disciples of this sect were young and inexperienced.

That was why they thought so. Even if they didn’t know what would happen in a few decades, they believed they wouldn’t be able to keep up with Southern Edge, who was fiercely fighting back with their great disciples and elders.

But the scene happening here was enough to shatter their perception.

And.

“Ackkkk!”

The long-suffering men of Xi’an clenched their fists, watching the sight of Hwang Seong being enveloped in red petals and screaming.

‘It seems pointless to discuss the future. They can face the Southern Edge right now.’

‘I didn’t know Mount Hua would become this strong...’

An indescribable sense of excitement and pride surged through their hearts. Those who had once been looked down upon had grown dazzlingly.

At that moment, Wei Lishan limped forward.

“Sect leader...”

Then Hyun Jong, who had been carrying a pouch on his back, observed the situation and turned his head to Wei Lishan.

“Clan leader Wei. Are you alright?”

“...Yes. I am alright, sect leader.”

“We were too late. Forgive us.”

“How can you say that, sect leader? Just the fact that you came here is enough to move my heart as a disciple.”

Hyun Jong gently patted Wei Lishan on the shoulder with warm eyes.

“You have had a hard time.”

Wei Lishan felt emotional for a moment and then glanced around the battlefield. It felt strange to be having such a conversation amidst such a fierce battle.

However, it wasn’t his fault. Hyun Jong’s relaxed attitude, which seemed to show no concern even though his disciples were fighting, made him relax.

Wei Lishan hesitated for a moment and then asked.

“Are you alright?”

“What?”

“They have gained so much momentum. If there is anything we can do to help them...”

“It is alright.”

“But...”

“It is alright.”

Hyun Jong shook his head with a faint smile.

“They are not the type of kids to cause problems to that extent. So just trust them and keep watch.”

“Ah...”

Wei Lishan hesitated as if he had more to say but soon nodded after seeing Hyun Jong’s calm face.

This was because he felt Hyun Jong’s firm trust in the disciples.

In the past, Hyun Jong was a person who couldn’t hide his anxiety even when the disciples were facing evil sect members who were stronger. But now, he didn’t show anxiety even though the disciples were facing an extremely powerful enemy.

Has Hyun Jong changed? Or...

“Is it strange?”

“Ah...”

Hyun Jong asked with a slight smile, perhaps noticing Wei Lishan’s feelings. Wei Lishan flinched and lowered his head.

“I am sorry. It is difficult for a disciple to guess the sect leader’s intentions...”

“It just naturally happened that way.”

“... Uh?”

Instead of answering right away, Hyun Jong stared blankly at the battlefield.

The Plum Blossom Sword beautifully colored the battlefield, but what they saw on the outside could not be everything. Inside, it must be a dangerous and fearful place where lives are at stake.

But.

“Anyone who has watched the time these kids have spent would feel the same. To be so anxious about this would be to ignore those kids and the time they have spent.”

Upon hearing those words, Wei Lishan looked around the battlefield with a blank face.

‘What...’

Hyun Jong’s words were filled with utmost certainty. So, he couldn’t help but wonder what kind of training they had been doing all this time.

“You don’t need to worry.”

At that moment, Hyun Jong’s calm voice pierced his ears.

“Because Mount Hua is strong.”

Wei Lishan couldn’t say anything for a moment. Indescribable emotions swirled in his chest and surged up.

How long had he waited to hear these words?

Words he thought he might never hear in his life finally came out of Hyun Jong’s mouth.

‘Sect leader...’

The disciples of Mount Hua were incredibly strong.

And the sect leader of Mount Hua, who looked extremely serious and dependable.

Anyone who used the name Mount Hua, whether they were a disciple of the main sect or a disciple of the sub-sects, would feel the same emotions.

“And.”

“... Uh?”

At that moment, Hyun Jong gestured to someone with his chin.

“If he had felt even a little bit of danger, he would have already gone wild. But since he is holding back, it is still far from dangerous.”

Where he pointed, there was the back of a person. The figure had arms crossed behind.

Just by looking at that waving hair tied up, you couldn’t help but recognize who that person was.

‘Taoist Chung Myung.’

Looking at that firm back, flawless and strong, Wei Lishan bit his lips without realizing it.

Chapter 880: Die Like A Bug (5)

The cold eyes of Chung Myung looked forward.

In the past, Chung Myung would have swung the sword more fiercely than anyone else, but now he stood firm.

Instead, his eyes were fixed on the area ahead as if he would not miss anything.

There was not a hint of hesitation or reluctance at the tips of the swords. The movements of the swordsmen clearly knew what they had to do.

Iron became hard when struck by a hammer, and swordsmen grew strong when they bled. The blood and sweat shed by the disciples of Mount Hua were more than enough to train them.

And now, all those layers of effort were bursting out here.

“Fresh out of the box...”

The corners of Chung Myung’s lips turned up.

No, the phrase ‘fresh out of the box’ no longer suited them. Anyone who could feel the qi contained in those swords, anyone who could understand the perfect sword path drawn by those sword tips, would never be able to call them that.

Paat!

At that moment, someone had just unleashed the Plum Blossom Sword Technique, causing red flowers to bloom in the middle of the fierce battlefield. Chung Myung clenched his fist slightly as he watched the plum blossoms blooming, turning the place red.

It was a strangely nostalgic sight.

The sword was something that was practiced throughout one’s life. There was no such thing as perfection. It was convenient to say third level and twelfth level, but even if one did achieve the 12th, it didn’t mean that there was no higher place to go.

So he wouldn’t dare say that they had mastered it, but what was certain was that the plum blossom sword the disciples of Mount Hua were currently using had reached a level that wouldn’t shame the ancestors of the past.

After all this time, finally.

Chung Myung gently rubbed his nose, marked with a handful of small wounds, and calmed his eyes.

‘This isn’t the time to be arrogant.’

In any case, the priority now was to punish those insane people who dared to invade Xi’an and massacre the sub-sect of Mount Hua.

At that moment, the Seven Killers of Jiangxi rushed forward, clearly visible to Chung Myung.

“Those bastards!”

Just as Chung Myung tensed, ready to spring into action, an angry voice erupted from the corner.

“Errrr!”

“Woah, woah, calm down now!”

“No, sahyung! Those bastards...!”

“Shh. Wait.”

“What!”

“Wait. Didn’t you hear what Chung Myung said? He told us not to move.”

“Ughh.”

Chung Myung slowly relaxed his stance, watching Jo Gul twitch as if he might bolt at any moment.

This battle wasn’t meant for them in the first place.

“Ugh.”

Chung Myung sighed like Jo Gul. At that moment, several people leaped towards the Seven Killers of Jiangxi.

When he recognized one of them, Chung Myung’s eyes widened.

“No, that bastard is?”

He realized he hadn’t called that one person out, but somehow, it ended up like this. That blood-crazed monk?

“That man... that man would make the Buddha cough up blood.”

Chung Myung’s face twisted at the sight of the shining head.

A strange expression, hard to describe, crossed the face of the Flame Axe King, Go Song.

It might seem easy to make an expression in the middle of a fight, but his situation was understandable.

Think about it.

Even though Mount Hua was famous for its sword techniques, it wasn’t just about swords, so it wouldn’t be strange if there were swordsmen who learned other martial arts.

And this one swordsman was capable of throwing the strong and capable Flame Axe King, Go Song, to the ground? That wouldn’t be normal at all.

But... it was a bit strange that this swordsman had a dazzlingly shiny bald head, and even stranger was that this bald man emitted a golden light.

And above all...

“Amitabha.”

“...”

What on earth? What were these people?

In a state of indescribable absurdity, Go Song tightly gripped the axe in his hand.

“You... a Taoist or a monk?”

“Amitabha. Of course, I am a monk.”

“Then why... are you wearing a Taoist robe?”

The monk in a Taoist robe, Hae Yeon, turned red in the face.

“... everyone has their own circumstances, so I hope you don’t blame me for this.”

“... is this a situation where I should be silent? I’ve been around the world for decades and seen all sorts of crazy people, but I’ve never seen a monk wearing a Taoist robe. Is this something anyone in their right mind would do?”

“...”

Hae Yeon tightly shut his eyes, his face bright red. Of course, he knew how strange this must seem.

If the Shaolin abbot saw this, he would be furious, saying, ‘This guy has finally lost his mind. A Shaolin disciple wearing the clothes of Mount Hua? Why, why is he even putting a plum blossom on his head? There’s no hair to put it on; it will fall!’

In the first place, clothes symbolized belonging. The fact that he, a Shaolin disciple, was wearing Mount Hua’s clothes was a strange sight even for the Evil Sect.

But what could they do?

The training that damned Taoist ordered wasn’t something his clothes could withstand. When he trained, the clothes would get torn apart at times.

It would only take a day or two to somehow find a monk’s robe and wear it, but it was impossible to do that for three years.

Still, Hae Yeon tried to fulfill his duty.

But...

“A monk’s robe? A monk’s robe? Did this brat lose his mind because of being beaten so much? Are you telling me to get a monk’s robe and offer it to him so that he can train comfortably? Are you insane? Uh? Fucking mad? It is ridiculous that he crawls in while I am training and helping someone’s sect and sticks his butt in, and what? A monk’s robe?”

There was no way that devil would understand.

At first, he tried to find some normal clothes that weren’t a robe and wear them. When one gave up many things, life became easier. In the end, he had no choice but to pick up the black robe lying around and wear it.

“A s-shell.”

“What?”

“A shell is just an empty shell. What is important is what is inside, right?”

“And so you are wearing a Taoist’s robe?”

“...”

“I have seen all sorts of insane people...”

At that moment, Hae Yeon stepped forward and threw a punch.

Kwaang!

The body of Go Song fell back as if it would break. He managed to block it with the axe in an instant, but if that strength had been directed at his head, it would have exploded like a watermelon.

Cold sweat formed on his forehead. If that force was briefly swung and held such power, then what would happen if this kid gathered his strength and struck him right?

“Surely... your appearance is funny, but...”

Hae Yeon said with a hardened look.

“That doesn’t mean I am going to mess around with you. You will pay the price for neglecting your duty as a person who has learned the martial arts and chosen to oppress the weak.”

“This insane monk...”

Go Song grabbed his axe as he gritted his teeth.

His appearance was funny, but his strength was enough to make his guts shiver.

“Phew.”

What was even more annoying were those cold, unresponsive eyes.

‘Shaolin?’

He had never really dealt with Shaolin, but not all of them would be like him. This man’s gaze was closer to a swordsman’s than a monk’s.

The moment he saw those cold, blade-like eyes, his blood ran cold.

‘Damn it.’

But there was no time to ponder for long. Even now, the battlefront was collapsing. Not to save those dying from attacks but to help himself survive.

If this continued, all the other people would fall, and only the brothers would have to handle so many opponents.

“YOUUUU!”

Go Song rushed forward and swung the axe with explosive force.

As befitting his title of the Flame Axe King, the strength in his axe was truly shocking. It was a power that couldn’t be compared to the sword qi, which was generally considered good. No weapon in the world could dare to compare to the axe in terms of its power to cut through things.

Wheeing!

The sound of the axe cutting through the wind pierced the ears as if it would rip the eardrums. The noise and force alone would make any ordinary warrior pee their pants and collapse.

But Hae Yeon’s eyes simply darkened as he watched the axe fall toward his head.

Tat.

Hae Yeon’s feet moved forward slightly.

Not the cool and refreshing technique he always showed, but an extremely practical technique that minimized body movement.

Thud!

At the same time, he firmly stepped into a stable stance and raised his fist toward the falling axe with all his strength.

‘Insane!’

Go Song, who was attacking, was even more surprised by that sight.

Blocking an axe with a fist? With a fist? It was an idea that no one with even a little bit of sense would consider or could accomplish.

‘Right, this ignorant bastard. I will cut you in half!’

Go Song pushed all his force into it. It seemed like he would end up splitting Hae Yeon into two at any moment. Soon, his axe and Hae Yeon’s fist clashed.

Kwang!

But unfortunately, the situation that Go Song had expected didn’t happen. The axe, which collided with the fist made of flesh and blood, bounced back as if it had struck a steel wall.

‘What?’

Go Song’s eyes widened as if they would tear apart. And at that very moment, Hae Yeon, who had not missed a chance, rushed at him like a bird.

“Euk!”

Startled by Hae Yeon’s foot flying toward his knee, he threw himself to the side and changed direction. But, at that moment, Hae Yeon’s body shot up into the air like a lightning bolt and kicked the wrist holding the axe.

Crack!

A terrible sound rang out. Even without feeling the pain being transmitted, he knew his wrist had been shattered in one hit.

But what shocked Go Song was not Hae Yeon’s technique that broke his wrist.

It was the ridiculous movement where Hae Yeon instantly twisted his extended foot towards Go Song, kicked the air, and then lifted his body up.

He could step in the air and change the direction of his body. But Go Song had never heard or seen anyone use air as a stepping stone naturally, without any preparatory movements, while fighting at high speeds.

‘How...’

But it was too early to be surprised. Hae Yeon, who had broken the wrist, twisted his body in the air and rushed right in front of Go Song.

At the same time, a fist flew towards his face.

A fist unique to Shaolin extended vertically with the thumb facing upward. It flew fiercely as if it would break Go Song’s face.

“Kuak!”

Go Song gritted his teeth and twisted his waist backward. Hae Yeon’s fist passed right in front of his nose.

‘I-I dodged...!’

“Kuak!”

But that joy didn’t last even a moment. As the fist passed Go Song’s face, Hae Yeon’s elbow folded and lodged into his chest.

Blood gushed from Go Song’s mouth at the shock of his sternum being crushed.

“Uh.... Uhhhh!”

Go Song swung the axe in a fit of rage. But Hae Yeon didn’t back down; instead, he dug into Go Song’s arms. No matter how sharp the axe was, it couldn’t cut down the teeth that had dug in like a hug.

“Sir.”

At that moment, Hae Yeon’s hand gently touched Go Song’s stomach.

“Evil deeds have a price to pay.”

“N-Noo...!”

Thud!

The power that emerged from Hae Yeon’s fingertips, which touched his dantian, shattered Go Song’s insides.

“Cough!”

Red blood poured from Go Song’s mouth like a waterfall. As his vision suddenly blurred, Go Song tried to look at Hae Yeon’s face somehow.

“Too... too much...”

Thud.

It was unclear what he was trying to say, but the emotion he felt at the end was evident.

“Amitabha.”

Hae Yeon chanted a short prayer.

“Please live as a saint in the next life.”

After glancing at the fallen Go Song, he turned his head and went in search of the next opponent.

Go Song, the Flame Axe King.

It was an unexpectedly futile death for someone who had been the second of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi and infamous for so many things.

Chapter 881: Those Drunk In The Past Are Dead (1)

“Accck!”

The sword sliced through a shoulder, leaving a long line of blood on the side. Those who had been causing havoc suddenly found themselves facing the harsh reality of what happened when their opponents responded calmly to emotional attacks.

“N-No.”

“R-run now!”

They weren’t stupid, just evil.

If they hadn’t been able to read the situation, they wouldn’t have maintained their infamy for long in the challenging Southern Lands. The moment they sensed they were at a disadvantage, the only thought that occupied their minds was to escape.

But to where?

The enemy was on all sides. Surrounding a larger group with a smaller one was a foolish move that no one who had read even a single word of strategy would have chosen. Still, now they were proving with their swords that what mattered was not the number of people, but their skills.

Paat!

A cold face, and a sword even colder than the face, slashed and stabbed vital points without a moment of hesitation.

Their breath caught in their throats, their hearts sinking.

The sight of a hundred or so peak-level swordsmen, all trained in the same swordsmanship and advancing in perfect sync, reminded everyone here why a famous sect was a symbol of fear.

There was no way they didn’t realize that if they continued like this, annihilation would be the outcome. Of course, after the initial fierce fight, most of them were subdued and fell rather than being killed...

‘Damn it, what is the difference!’

Even if they were subdued, these Mount Hua people wouldn’t let them go just like that. They would definitely lose their martial arts and be locked up, which for them was worse than death.

“Ackkk!”

Unable to withstand the pressure, someone attempted to leap over the heads of the Mount Hua swordsmen. But before they could even jump, a flying sword severed their leg.

“... damn it.”

The eyes of the evil sect members were filled with despair.

If they had only been violently pushing forward with their youthful looks, it wouldn’t have seemed so hopeless. In that case, there would definitely have been an opening. But these damned bastards were so cold and calm that their age seemed insignificant.

From the beginning to the present, their sword-swinging form hadn’t wavered at all.

The one who knocked down the opponent by cutting across the chest stabbed the fallen opponent’s wrist without blinking an eye, completely disabling him, and then moved on to the next opponent without care. There was something about the sight that made the hearts of those watching feel creeped out.

“... they say that you cannot trust the rumors around a sect.”

The words someone let out like a groan represented everyone’s feelings.

The world had misunderstood Mount Hua.

If they had known even a little bit about who they were, they would never have set foot on this land. Where was the insane man who put his own head into the tiger’s mouth?

But as always, regret was too late, no matter how fast it arrived.

There was no way for them to turn back from a wrong choice.

A half-bloodied beast with a hardened face looked around.

‘This isn’t looking good.’

The situation was getting worse.

The problem wasn’t that the opponent was strong. The real problem was that these fools were losing their will to fight.

When the Evil Sect gained momentum, they attacked more harshly than anyone else. Still, once they lost momentum, they crumbled like sand.

It was expected. Those who were of the Evil Sect were there for a reason.

Why would those who care most about their own lives, the wealth that was given to them, and their own feelings risk their lives and fight?

If they felt that their life was in danger, no matter what situation it was, it was in their nature to seek a way to live.

In such a situation, there was just one way to reorganize the battle line: the presence of a strong leader.

But... they looked only to see Gal Cheon-rip frozen in place.

‘He was wrong.’

This situation couldn’t be resolved with such a fool.

Well, if he had the ability, he wouldn’t have been just one of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi.

That guy seemed to think he could become Jang Ilso if he put his mind to it, but that would be an insult to Jang Ilso for this kind of idiot to even mention such a name. They were different from the start.

‘The situation?’

The Soul-breaking Killer turned his sharp gaze to assess the situation around him.

‘Fucking moron.’

The Spear Ghost was retreating without thinking against the two Mount Hua swordsmen.

It was a pitiful thing to do compared to his reputation. Still, if they really had to be picky, it wasn’t a situation where one could criticize him. First of all, that hole in the shoulder was preventing him from displaying his skills.

And...

‘It isn’t just two.’

On the surface, he was fighting only two, but in reality, he was fighting three. No, maybe he was fighting more than three. He seemed to feel tremendous pressure from the man who was quietly glaring at the swordsmen swinging their swords in front of him.

This young man with curly hair. The man who must have made that hole in that shoulder. Even from a distance, an eerie atmosphere could be felt.

The same went for the Bloody Blade Killer.

He had no advantage against the three Mount Hua swordsmen. If he had rushed in just to be wounded, he would’ve fallen while leaking blood.

But they didn’t think it was necessary to take that risk, or they judged it to be more efficient, so they were leisurely wearing the man down slowly.

And...

‘Again?’

There was another person standing behind the disciples of Mount Hua.

A female swordsman standing with an expressionless face that didn’t fit on the battlefield. The moment he saw her, the Soul-Breaking Killer felt his entire body tense up and his palms sweat.

“Ugh... that damned Gal Cheon-rip,”

Those two weren’t the only ones.

Anyone who looked at the battlefield calmly would realize that Mount Hua’s strength that was currently attacking them was less than half of its actual power.

Those who could be called the true core of Mount Hua were not even swinging their swords.

The key figures who infiltrated the battlefield were like a mother tiger training her cubs to hunt, throwing suitable prey in front of them, and observing the battlefield from behind.

They were ready to step in at any time if someone was in danger.

This meant that if they had been at the forefront, this battle would have been over.

‘You are saying that they aren’t at the level of the Nine Great Sects even now?’

What nonsense.

No matter how great the Nine Great Sects were, it was impossible for them to have a hundred swordsmen like this. No sect in the world would be able to clear the battlefield without a single casualty against Mount Hua.

There might be a limit to their numbers, but they had already surpassed the level of small and medium-sized sects. How many sects in the Nine Great Sects could guarantee to defeat them?

The eyes of the Soul Breaking Killer remained cold.

Would he personally step forward and clear the battlefield right now?

Not possible.

Someone might be able to turn the tide by their presence. But, the battlefield was now swirling like a torrent, so if they jumped in with meager skills, they would be swept away without even screaming.

Could he completely turn the tide of the battlefield with his skills?

It was impossible.

The Soul Breaking Killer, having conflicted thoughts, took a step back.

He criticized the nature of the Evil Sect, but he was one of them too. The attributes couldn’t be much different. In particular, the tendencies of those who got pushed to the north were similar after all.

What he desperately needed now was to escape with his life.

Fortunately, they were not paying attention to him yet. It might be impossible to escape quietly without making a sound. Still, if he could take down one or two of them in one swift move while they were distracted, it wouldn’t be difficult to open a path and escape.

The pursuit would follow, but hadn’t he survived countless pursuits so far? If he could escape the siege for just a moment, he was confident he would survive.

So...

‘Over there!’

It was the moment when the Soul Breaking Killer, who had found a tiny opening, kicked the ground and moved.

“Where are you running off to so quickly?”

The strength in his legs, which had been ready to push, faltered, and his knees buckled.

He bit his lip and slowly straightened his bent body. Then, he looked back at the person who had come up behind him.

“... you realized?”

“If you look around creepily, wouldn’t it be stranger if we didn’t realize?”

A deep sigh escaped the mouth of the Soul Breaking Killer.

‘Damn it, that is right.’

There was no way things would get resolved so quickly.

Kuaak.

He clenched his fists and opened his mouth to address the person standing in front of him.

“If you are quick-witted, you will be quick to give orders.”

“Hahaha, I have lived long enough, so who cares about it?”

“... if the guys are acting right, I will just sit back and watch. Is there really a need to step ahead and act along?”

“When the guys are fighting so hard, how can I just stand back and hold the burden? I don’t know about others, but there would be no one who would stay to watch and hold back in Mount Hua.”

The face of the Soul Breaking Killer gradually darkened.

At first glance, it wasn’t easy. And the feeling was bound to be different. Unlike the Mount Hua swordsmen currently fighting, the one standing in front was someone who had brightly answered.

“Who?”

When asked softly, the one standing in front of him brightly smiled and answered.

“I am an elder of Mount Hua, Hyun Sang.”

“... elder.”

It was unfair. The others were fighting second and third-class disciples, but he had to face an elder.

But then again...

The Soul Breaking Killer glanced behind Hyun Sang. There were no other disciples behind him.

“If I kill this old man, the path will open up, right?”

“Right. If you kill me, you will be able to escape.”

Crack.

The Soul Breaking Killer’s fist made a cracking sound. At the same time, both hands began to turn red.

“You picked the wrong opponent, old man. I am different from those morons.”

“I know.”

Hyun Sang nodded with a quiet smile. At the same time, he slowly began to draw the sword at his waist.

“That is why I have to face you. Of course, there are many people here stronger than me, but I wouldn’t be able to save my face as an elder if I handled a weak one.”

“Save face...”

The Soul Breaking Killer, who was about to sneer, closed his mouth for a moment.

No, to be precise, it closed on its own. Hyun Sang’s sudden change of demeanor made him suffocate for a moment.

Before he pulled out that sword, he seemed like an old man, but the moment he held the sword, his entire nature changed.

The aura of a swordsman who had endured the storms of time with his entire body pressed down on him like a force. Unlike those young disciples who were like blades, his aura felt majestic, heavy, and serious.

It was an aura that didn’t deviate an inch from what the world thought of as the right path.

“Is this the sword of Mount Hua...?”

“There is no such thing. Everything is the sword of Mount Hua.”

“...”

“It is just a little bit more worn and a little bit heavier. But... I won’t disappoint you.”

The Soul Breaking Killer bit his lips tightly.

‘Damn it, I shouldn’t have come here.’

The Soul Breaking Killer thought that this might really be the last time, and he brought up his internal qi as much as he could.

“I will end that tough life here for you, old man!”

He pulled out the qi from both hands like blades and struck Hyun Sang in one swift motion.

Chapter 882: Those Drunk On The Past Are Dead (2)

“Why did it end up like this?”

Gal Cheon-rip’s face was filled with confusion.

Clearly, until he had stepped into Xi’an... no, up until he was slaughtering those blocking his path at Eunha, everything was going as planned.

Then why did it end up like this?

The intervention of Mount Hua?

Of course, he expected it. When those damn bastards of the Ten Thousand People Clan invaded Xi’an in the past, it wasn’t Southern Edge but Mount Hua who stepped ahead and stopped them. Just three years ago, Mount Hua was a sect that acted as if the world was small and roamed around the Yangtze River as if it were their home.

There was no way those people would sit back and do nothing because something happened in their town.

But...

Crack.

Gal Cheon-rip gritted his teeth until they broke. What he hadn’t expected wasn’t Mount Hua stepping in; it was the strength of Mount Hua.

“This is impossible.”

How could they get this strong in just three years?

This wasn’t possible by normal means. The Mount Hua that the world knew and the Mount Hua he was witnessing with his own eyes were entirely different sects.

If he had known they had this kind of strength, he wouldn’t have set foot in Xi’an at all.

He had already figured out Mount Hua’s strength three years ago. So, was his assessment back then wrong?

That couldn’t be.

If they had been this strong since then, the Yangtze River massacre wouldn’t have happened. And even if it had happened, the Southern Lands wouldn’t have been abandoned in the treaty.

Even among the great sects, the one who resisted the most fiercely while suffering humiliation from Jang Ilso in order to survive was Mount Hua.

In other words, Mount Hua at the time was a sect that didn’t have the strength to change the world despite its resistance.

Then...

“They really changed this much in just three years? They got this strong?”

Who would believe it?

“If that was possible, who would go through so much trouble!”

If anyone could easily become strong through training and hard work, then where in the world would some weren’t skilled, and where would there be a sect that wasn’t great?

Yet, these people were now completely upending the common sense of the murim world. Those who had barely escaped the status of small or medium sects had returned with the strength of great sects in just three years.

No, in truth, he still couldn’t determine the full extent of their skills. Any ability can be accurately measured with the right benchmark. Still, the skills of those he brought along were far too weak to serve as such a benchmark.

Even though he had brought the strongest of the Evil Sect who had moved north, and even though he was convinced that if he led them to Qinghai, he could build a formidable force that would shake up the murim world!

‘Where did it all go wrong?’

Where did it start to unravel?

‘ACKKKK!’

At that moment, a sharp scream pierced his ears. It was the scream of his younger comrades who had rushed ahead to fight.

Until then, there was nothing they hadn’t accomplished on their own. They could do whatever they wanted in their lands.

But here, even they were being trampled and swept away.

Everything was starting to fall apart.

All his plans to lead them to Qinghai, build a force, and seize the land while the Justice and Evil sects were fighting—his ambitions were collapsing around him.

“This...”

Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes were red with frustration.

“Mount Hua!”

It was all because of them. These people...

Step. Step.

At that moment, the sound of slow footsteps could be heard. Slow and regular footsteps that didn’t fit in with the battlefield filled with screams and clashing weapons.

Gal Cheon-rip turned his head with a terrifying expression.

A man was slowly approaching him.

“....”

The man’s appearance signified several things.

It also meant that there weren’t enough people from the Evil Sect to fight against the Mount Hua disciples, and the remaining ones didn’t even have the strength to stop the Mount Hua swordsmen from moving around.

More than anything...

“How dare you!”

Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes were red with fury.

It also meant that he was underestimated enough that someone decided to face him alone.

Against the ruthless Gal Cheon-rip.

“You...”

It felt like blood was rushing back to his throat.

“...who are you?”

The man held back a small grudge at the question he asked while gritting his teeth.

“I don’t know how to answer. I don’t really have a title to be proud of.”

“A titleless kid?”

“That is how it is.”

Although his tone seemed flustered, his expression looked confident. That only made Gal Cheon-rip even angrier.

“...Not Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, Mount Hua’s Righteous Sword... a nameless swordsman bastard dares to confront me? Not a normal guy, but a one-armed bastard?”

This time, the opponent shrugged his shoulders without getting angry.

“I feel a little sorry for you. Anyway, since you have asked me, I will answer. I am the first-class disciple of Mount Hua, Un Geom.”

Un Geom stretched out his only hand and slowly pulled out the sword at his waist.

“The teacher had to take someone else, so I was bound to end up as a commoner. Don’t blame me too much for being an unknown person.”

Gal Cheon-rip closed his mouth.

He was no longer angry. They say that when people get too angry, they become cold-hearted, and that seems to be true.

“Do you know who I am?”

“I don’t know.”

“...You don’t know?”

Looking at Gal Cheon-rip’s bewildered look, Un Geom calmly answered.

“Don’t look at me like that. Unfortunately, the situation didn’t allow me to hear who you were. I came running as soon as I heard the news.”

“...”

Un Geom shrugged.

“And no one here told me, so I couldn’t know either.”

Gal Cheon-rip burst into laughter.

Was he being treated like this by these guys?

“I am the Ghost Blade Warrior, Gal Cheon-rip. Eldest of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi.”

“Ah, I see.”

Un Geom nodded briefly.

Gal Cheon-rip clenched his teeth at the calm words.

“This fool had never even heard of the name.”

“I know.”

“... what?”

Un Geom looked at Gal Cheon-rip with calm eyes.

“I know. No matter how narrow-minded I am, I am not so deaf as to have not heard at least one of your two titles.”

“And yet you have come to face me?”

“Is there a reason for me not to?”

Gal Cheon-rip’s lips twitched at that.

He reigned over the south of the river like a god. How could he get insulted like this?

“That arm.”

“... hm?”

“Who cut it?”

Un Geom stared at Gal Cheon-rip without answering, as if he wanted to confirm the sincerity of that question.

Then Gal Cheon-rip opened his mouth first with that gaze.

“I heard that when the Ten Thousand People clan invaded Mount Hua, there was a guy who almost died and came back to life. Is that you?”

“Yes.”

“Then it means that you were the man who couldn’t even handle a single leader of the Ten Thousand People clan, and yet you have come to face me? If the Ten Thousand People clan wanted to face me, Jang Ilso would have come to face me!”

The moment those words came out, Un Geom’s face collapsed.

But it wasn’t a face of despair or humiliation. It was, so to say... a face holding back laughter. He, who had been so calm till then, bit his lips tightly, trying to suppress the laughter.

At that reaction, Gal Cheon-rip’s face hardened coldly.

“... what is so funny?”

“Ah, I am sorry. I know it is rude, but I cannot stand it when the name Jang Ilso comes up.”

“....”

“I have a question. When was the last time you saw the Hegemony Lord?”

“... last time?”

Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes narrowed. He didn’t know why he was asked this, but it had to have been at least 15 years since they met in person. No, it had to be more than that.

But what did that mean...

“I guarantee you, if you had seen the Hegemony Lord with your own eyes, you wouldn’t have dared to speak such a thing.”

“This fucking brat....”

Gal Cheon-rip’s face turned red and blue. Un Geom just looked at him coldly.

Even if the opponent was from the Evil Sect, it was not permissible for him, a Taoist, to lose his manners in front of others. But when Jang Ilso’s name came out of his mouth, even Un Geom couldn’t hold back his laughter.

Jang Ilso, who had driven Chung Myung and the Five Swords to the brink of death, was clearly noted by Un Geom.

Gal Cheon-rip was no insignificant warrior. But Jang Ilso was on a different level. Wasn’t Jang Ilso the embodiment of all things that made one’s body shiver in fear at the thought of him?

On the contrary, Gal Cheon-rip, who was from the Evil Sect, didn’t even truly know Jang Ilso.

“Perhaps your words that you can deal with the Hegemony Lord are not groundless lies if that Jang Ilso stayed in the past that you remembered.”

Gal Cheon-rip, who understood the meaning of Un Geom’s words, gritted his teeth.

“This guy dares to insult me....”

“Even though he was an enemy, he was someone who never stopped improving himself. But... What about you? It has been a while since I first heard of the name of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, but you haven’t really changed much, have you?”

“....”

“Those who are drunk on past glory are dead.”

Those words stabbed Gal Cheon-rip’s heart like a knife.

“And those who are satisfied with what they have achieved and gave up will not stay where they are, but will fall behind.”

Un Geom’s gaze slightly turned to the side. He saw the disciples of Mount Hua standing proudly against their enemies, the Five Swords guarding them.

‘It is difficult to constantly improve oneself.’

However, there were those who had accomplished the difficult task. There were those with great reputations and outstanding skills but who had actually worked harder than them.

So, as their teacher, how could he show himself as inferior?

Un Geom raised the sword and aimed it at Gal Cheon-rip.

“That is good timing. Didn’t you say earlier that I couldn’t even match one of the Ten Thousand People clan’s leaders?”

“...So?”

“Then how about we check it out? Let’s see how much I have changed since I couldn’t fight even one of the leaders of the Ten Thousand People clan, and how much you, who were once compared to that Hegemony Lord, have fallen behind.”

“....”

Gal Cheon-rip stared at Un Geom with a blank look.

The boiling anger had subsided in an instant, replaced by a cold killing intent. No matter the situation on the battlefield, it seemed this feeling would not go away unless he tore this man open and killed him right away.

“That sword...”

Srrng.

Gal Cheon-rip slowly drew his blade. His heart felt colder than ever as he held the weapon that had taken the lives of many people.

“I hope that sword is as sharp as your mouth. If not, you will end up begging me to kill you.”

Even as he received the killing intent of Gal Cheon-rip, Un Geom simply smiled broadly.

“You will not be disappointed.”

Un Geom, who had been performing the Plum Blossom Sword technique in a neat form, stopped and spoke briefly.

“Come.”

“I will tear that mouth open!”

Gal Cheon-rip charged towards Un Geom like a storm.

Chapter 883: Those Drunk On The Past Are Dead (3)

Woong!

A sword with great force had fallen on his head.

The Soul Breaking Killer frowned and took a step back. The sword, which had passed before his face, suddenly stopped midair as if it were a lie and then rushed toward his neck at lightning speed.

‘Kuak.’

The Soul Breaking Killer twisted his body and slashed at the flying sword with his hand. The sword bent with a thud, but the tip quickly found its place, and its direction didn’t change at all.

The sword passed by his shoulder, drawing a thin red line.

At that moment, the eyes of Soul Breaking Killer were filled with killing intent.

Woong!

With a sound like hundreds of bees flying at the same time, a deep, blood-red qi shot out from both his hands.

Paat!

Soon, that qi rushed towards Hyun Sang like lightning.

Thung!

Hyun Sang then twisted the sword and slashed away the flying qi with his blade. But this was something even the Soul Breaking Killer had expected.

Paat!

Soul Breaking Killer unleashed a dozen or so punches in succession. The bloody red qi flew in as if it wanted to envelop Hyun Sang.

Hyun Sang’s eyes darkened for a moment. His sword, which had briefly stopped, simultaneously created a dozen or so afterimages and collided with the fist qi flying in.

Boom! Kwang! Kwang! Kwang!

A series of explosions erupted.

The strong force made Soul Breaking Killer take four to five steps back. He could endure it if he wanted to, but he didn’t feel the need to force himself.

“...”

What caught his eye was the figure of Hyun Sang, who hadn’t even stepped back a single step and stood firmly in place.

They must have felt the same force, but one side had stepped back while the other didn’t. Of course, this alone wouldn’t be enough to evaluate their skills, but it was clear who benefitted from this exchange.

The Soul Breaking Killer’s eyes went stiff.

Strong.

It felt as if he had pushed against a huge wall. The sensation he felt at his fingertips was as solid as a mountain.

‘Why has someone like this been hiding all this time?’

He had never heard of a skilled veteran warrior in Mount Hua. Any veteran warrior who recently joined Mount Hua would have had enough fame.

That wasn’t the only suspicious thing.

“You.”

Hyun Sang met his gaze with the man who sounded like scraping iron.

“... the sword technique you use seems to be different from that of your disciples.”

Hyun Sang felt a sense of awe upon hearing those words.

“It couldn’t be helped. I don’t have the youth those kids have.”

The sword of Mount Hua was splendid and sharp. When he saw the sword th,e kids, even Hyun Sang, felt shocked.

But...

When Hyun Sang was learning it, there was no such sword technique left in the sect. When the real sword of Mount Hua returned to Mount Hua, he was at an age where he couldn’t learn new techniques.

Those kids were like blank sheets of paper. That was why they could learn and accept everything. One can write anything on a white sheet of paper.

But Hyun Sang wasn’t like that. He had already learned a different sword technique than the kids for decades.

Wasn’t it impossible to write new letters on a sheet of paper that was already filled up? It was also nearly impossible to completely renew everything he had learned and fully accept new martial arts.

But...

‘What does it matter?’

Hyun Sang didn’t despair.

He didn’t necessarily have to be like these kids. It was alright, even if gorgeous plum blossoms didn’t bloom at his fingertips. He was no longer the one who bloomed. He was the one who should be the solid foundation for the kids who could bloom.

“A little late, a little dull. But a little stronger.”

“...”

The eyes of the Soul Breaking Killer sank in.

He knew from long experience that these people were more difficult to handle.

He didn’t have the luxury of time to deliberate on the outcome. The situation was becoming increasingly unfavorable, and if he delayed any longer, he wouldn’t have the chance to escape, even if he managed to win.

But.

Crack.

The sound of his knuckles cracking resonated around him.

That didn’t mean he was afraid. The fact that he possessed such skill yet failed to gain fame indicated that he had little experience in actual combat.

‘No matter how old he is, if he has no actual experience in combat, he is nothing more than a kid.’

He lowered his stance slightly. He knew better than anyone when these people panicked the most.

“You should say that after you survive, old man!”

Thud!

The tips of his feet pushed off the ground at once.

Soon, the Soul Breaking Killer, who was almost crawling on the ground, rushed toward Hyun Sang. Then, he touched the ground and changed direction.

As soon as he turned his body, he kicked off the ground again and shot forward. His eyes, moving violently like a snake, rushed diagonally and gleamed with a fierce light.

Of course, when he saw the swords of the young kids, it was clear they had undergone unimaginable hardships. But training was just training, after all. In a real battle where life and death are decided in an instant, a moment of confusion could easily lead to a fatal mistake.

‘And people become less adept at improvising as they age!’

With bizarre movements, his hands, which had closed the distance, turned an eerie crimson color and bent like the talons of an eagle. Then, they stretched out, aiming for Hyun Sang’s ankles.

Swish!

At that moment, Hyun Sang’s sword flew in like lightning, targeting the right hand of the Soul Breaking Killer, which was aiming for his ankle. He intended to pierce the hand and pin it to the ground.

But the hand of the Soul Breaking Killer twisted as if waiting for that moment, pushing the side of the sword away with his palm.

Kakakak!

The creepy sound of metal being scraped echoed as the sword’s surface and hand met.

The sword qi sharpened even the surface. The skin on the hand was cut, and blood splashed out. But in return, he succeeded in making the sword, which had been thrust downwards, land on the ground instead of his hand.

At that moment, the man’s eyes had an eerie light in them. He instantly flipped his body up and swung his hands like lightning.

Kwakwakwak!

A red form erupted from below. It was as if blood was bursting out of the ground.

But the oddness itself was more dangerous than the eeriness of the sight. Even if they had been through a fair amount of battles, would they ever experience a force rushing in like a storm from beneath their feet?

With that one move, the Soul Breaking Killer clearly proved who he was.

Kwang!

As expected, Hyun Sang couldn’t find a way to deal with the attack, which seemed to rise from below, so he kicked the ground and drew his sword to jump into the air.

At the same time, the sword hurriedly struck the qi that was fiercely pursuing him.

But that was exactly what the man wanted.

Kwang!

The Soul Breaking Killer slammed his hand hard on the ground. In reaction, he chased Hyun Sang, who had shot up in the air like an arrow. He could see the venom in his eyes, indicating he wouldn’t give up the win he had once seized.

The one running and the one chasing.

At that moment, Hyun Sang kicked the air once, slightly increasing the distance between him and the Soul Breaking Killer, and then raised his sword.

Woong!

In the end, Hyun Sang’s sword, which contained the strength of sword qi, drew a clean arc from top to bottom.

With just the qi from the sword slash, the human body made of flesh and bones seemed like it would be torn in two in an instant. But the moment he saw that dangerous sword, pleasure filled the eyes of the Soul Breaking Killer.

‘Caught you!’

Woong!

As he pushed with all his strength, his hand turned red, almost black.

“Ahhh!”

The Soul Breaking Killer let out a loud roar and ran toward the flying blade. He crossed his hands and grabbed the sword.

Kakakang! Kakakang! Kaakang!

The sharp blade scraping against the rust-colored hand resonated eerily. Hyun Sang’s sword, caught in that bloody hand, swung like a live fish caught by a fisherman, literally cutting into the hand.

But even as the palm was being cut open, the eyes of the Soul Breaking Killer held onto Hyun Sang’s sword and didn’t let go.

‘A swordsman is nothing without a sword!’

When a swordsman could not firmly plant his foot on the ground as he usually did, the sword slash was bound to lose strength. It might be sharp, but it would be difficult to have strength. If that was the case, he could sufficiently subdue it with his own hands.

He had faced such people many times. The sight of their faces turning pale as he demonstrated his techniques never got old for him.

Crack.

And so he looked at Hyun Sang, who had driven the blade into the hand bone firmly and fixed it perfectly. At that moment, there was no way to check what kind of expression was being made.

But... it didn’t go as he expected.

What he saw wasn’t Hyun Sang’s face but something bluntly approaching his face.

Before he could even confirm its identity, his eyes widened. At the same time, he realized it was Hyun Sang’s elbow. The tremendous impact hit his face instantly and knocked him unconscious.

Kwaang!

Blackout.

The world turned black for a moment, then regained light. All the Soul Breaking Killer saw was a pure blue sky.

“Kuak...”

Soon, unimaginable pain struck his face as if crushing it.

‘This...’

Before his thoughts could be organized, his instincts kicked in. The moment he twisted his body to avoid the following attack, his hand was pulled as if tied by something.

‘What?’

The moment the body, which had been bent as if it might collapse at any second, was forcibly pulled back, the very elbow he had seen just moments ago came into view.

Kwaang!

The Soul Breaking Killer’s body flew back like a cannonball. He bounced off the ground, leaving a large mark, and fell back down, spraying blood everywhere.

“Cough! Cough!”

Before he could even groan in pain, clumps of blood that had been crawling up his throat were continuously pushed out of his mouth. With just two attacks, his insides were completely turned inside out.

“Kuak...”

The body, which had tried to lift itself by pushing against the floor with his hands, fell down again. In his blurred vision, he saw the cross-section of his hand, neatly severed.

“Cough! Cough!”

However, what tormented the man wasn’t the pain from his completely sunken face, nor the shock of losing the hand that had always protected him.

It was the sheer incomprehensibility of the situation itself.

‘Like this...’

He understood it intellectually.

The moment he had the sword stuck in his hand and fixed it, the man had pulled the sword, dragging him in, and simultaneously smashed his face with the elbow of the left arm that wasn’t holding the sword.

Since the sword was fixed and stuck into the bone, there was no way for the man to escape.

Right. He understood what happened. But how did it happen?

How could that old man respond so perfectly in an instant? Normally, when someone used a sword, their mind would go blank, and they wouldn’t know what to do when the sword was overpowered by their opponent...

Thud!

At that moment, Hyun Sang, who had come down in front of him, stared at the Soul Breaking Killer with serious eyes.

“Not a bad move, but you overlooked one thing.”

“....”

The man looked at Hyun Sang, trying to lift his head, which was engulfed in pain. His eyes were filled with questions.

Overlooked?

Hyun Sang opened his mouth calmly as if he was ready to answer at any moment.

“From my perspective, your attack was neither strange nor irregular. Rather, it was horrifyingly obvious. Unfortunately, there are those in Mount Hua who don’t mind biting the sword with their teeth.”

“....”

“It would have been a good match if you hadn’t rushed so quickly.”

Hyun Sang shook his head slightly and raised his sword.

“Your defeat was because you underestimated your opponent. In battle, the one who lets his guard down is the first to die. Just like that guy says.”

The face of the Soul Breaking Killer contorted. No, it was already so distorted that it couldn’t be more distorted; it was grotesquely contorted.

‘Obvious?’

His shoulder was drenched in blood.

‘Am I the one not used to fighting?’

A groan escaped his mouth.

“Ugh...”

The pain from Hyun Sang’s words was greater than the pain he felt in his body. Eventually, the groan turned into a howl.

“T... this fucking dog! UHHHHHHH!”

Slash!

At that moment, Hyun Sang’s sword cut off the neck of the Soul Breaking Killer in one swift motion.

Tak.

The head, with its contorted expression, fell to the ground and rolled.

“But it will be a fitting end for you. Go to hell and apologize for all the sins you have committed.”

Hyun Sang, having shaken off his sword, glanced at the headless, collapsed body of the Soul Breaking Killer and turned around without regret.

Chapter 884: Those Drunk On The Past Are Dead (4)

Tak.

The head of the Soul Breaking Killer separated from his body, fell to the ground, and rolled away.

Despite the battlefield being filled with screams and clashes, the sound spread clearly.

The eyes of those who turned their heads in a trance to check the headless corpse trembled as if they were shivering.

‘The S-soul Breaking Killer is...’

It was a battlefield, so death wasn’t a strange thing.

But the Soul Breaking Killer was not just anyone; and he had his throat cut by an old swordsman whose name wasn’t even known. Those who knew how great he was couldn’t help but be shocked.

“Ugh...”

Anyone who wasn’t a fool couldn’t help but know that the situation was beyond repair. Those from the Evil Sects were more sensitive to the situation on the battlefield than anyone else.

There were two reasons why they had been able to endure it until then.

One was that there was nowhere to run, even if they tried to escape.

And another was the last hope that someone’s efforts could change the flow of the battlefield.

But the warrior Soul Breaking Killer, who should have been leading the situation, was lying on the ground with his head facing the ground.

The Evil Sect people were all filled with despair.

There were still a few warriors, including some of the Seven Killers of Jiangxi, but they were engaged in their own battles.

The sight of the world’s best Seven Killers of Jiangxi being pushed back by a nameless swordsman of Mount Hua was like having cold water poured onto them, and they realized the situation.

It was obvious where the eyes of those who had lost hope were headed. The reason they were able to gather here. And the only one they could finally hope for.

However, the sight before their eyes miserably crushed their last remaining hopes.

Kwang!

The clashing swords and blades bounced off at the same time, creating a loud noise.

A tense clash where neither side could seize the chance to win. Yet, anyone with even a basic understanding of martial arts would easily guess that some were not satisfied with the outcome.

Gal Cheon-rip’s face contorted.

“You!”

Gal Cheon-rip, who had fiercely kicked the ground and shot out like an arrow, swung the blade again.

Kwaang!

The cracking sound rang out as if the air was being torn apart. It was truly an all-out attack with incredible speed and power.

But soon, the sword, which drew a sharp arc from below and flew up, collided head-on with Gal Cheon-rip’s blade.

Kwaang!

Another loud explosion erupted, and Gal Cheon-rip’s body swayed backward like a reed hit by a typhoon.

Crack! Tak!

His feet, unable to handle the pressure, stepped back several times.

Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes turned bloodshot with anger.

Of course, the sword that blocked the attack also retreated a similar distance. But the fact was rather humiliating for him.

“Uh...”

Gal Cheon-rip bit his lips until they bled.

The blade he swung with both hands collided with the sword swung with one hand, and they were evenly matched. Considering that the blade was twice as thick and heavy as the sword... this was a miserable defeat.

Because this wasn’t a defeat of internal qi and technique, but a defeat of brute force.

‘What kind of strength does a one-armed man even have...’

He had heard that people who have only one arm develop stronger force and grip than those with both hands since they have to do everything with just one arm.

But wasn’t that the story for people who hadn’t learned martial arts?

There was no way that someone with internal qi would experience the same inconvenience as ordinary people while living with one hand. Then, where could he get this brute force from?

“...if I learned the way of Tao, I would have reached the peak stage.”

Un Geom slowly shook his head at Gal Cheon-rip’s words.

“That isn’t innate; it is what I learned.”

“...a swordsman?”

Un Geom smiled faintly.

When he considered that he had to reestablish martial arts from the beginning, only one scene came to mind.

The moment he first engraved the name Chung Myung in his mind.

The boy was torturing his body with a large boulder hanging on him.

It was as if he was ignoring the common belief that a swordsman should be nimble.

At first, he thought it was just a means to forcefully lead the older swordsmen. But, as far as he knew, Chung Myung had never stopped training his body since.

Right. That boy was right in front of him.

A person who seemingly had no talent, who was once so weak and small, had now reached a level that was beyond imagination. How could he not follow the shining path when it was so clearly visible?

And the things he had gained while walking that path were now completely present at the tip of his sword.

“Why don’t you check it out for yourself?”

Killing intent rose in the eyes of Gal Cheon-rip.

“You speak well!”

In an instant, he kicked the ground and closed the distance between himself and Un Geom. He confirmed that this one-armed, left-handed man was stronger than he had anticipated. If it were him, he might have tried to take his time and explore the opponent a little more leisurely.

But now was not the time for leisurely actions.

One reason was the words that the arrogant guy had said before the fight started, which were burning inside him. But the bigger reason was that he couldn’t waste any more time.

He had also clearly seen the Soul Breaking Killer decapitated before he could even fight properly. If he continued to hesitate now, these dogs would eventually lose the will to fight and surrender.

In that case, Gal Cheon-rip wouldn’t even be given a chance to fight. Even if they didn’t know of other things, Mount Hua surely wouldn’t spare him, the one who did this.

“DIEEEEE!”

Paaang!

The blade tore through the air and came down towards Un Geom, who was holding his reddened sword.

This time, Un Geom didn’t immediately fight back and swiftly moved to the side to avoid the blade attack.

Kwaang!

The ground, struck by the blade slash, exploded and burst apart. It was just a blow into the earth, yet a huge hole was gouged out, large enough to fit a person.

The force was enough to make several onlookers gasp in frustration. It was a blow that proved his title was not given without reason.

However, there was no change in the eyes of Un Geom, who had dodged the attack. He simply looked at Gal Cheon-rip with a cold expression.

“You!”

At that moment, the blade of Gal Cheon-rip began to emit a creepy crackling sound and slashed horizontally.

It was a devastating blow, making one wonder if it was possible to block such a formidable blade with such a thin sword.

Gal Cheon-rip was certain that this time, the damned one-armed man wouldn’t dare to face the sword.

But at that moment, Un Geom planted his foot onto the ground and swung his sword fiercely at the blade flying towards him.

‘Moron!’

Was it a mistake made because the advantage he had in the first clash was still vivid in his mind? Such recklessness.

Kwang!

A huge crash rang out soon after. As expected, the sword bounced back without a fight. Gal Cheon-rip found hope in this and tightened his grip on the blade. He was determined to cut Un Geom in half!

But at that moment.

The sword that had bounced back struck the blade with more force than before.

Kwang!

The blade hesitated as it was struck again.

Kwang! Kwang! Kwang!

In the blink of an eye, a dozen or so sword slashes erupted, completely disrupting the momentum of his blade.

Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes widened as if they were going to tear.

‘W-what is that?’

How fast did a sword have to move to do that?

No, that wasn’t simply speed. It was an impossible feat that each blow didn’t completely dissipate the shock that spread through his body the moment the sword and blade collided, using the strength to send the sword flying.

Paaat!

The moment Gal Cheon-rip’s blade bounced off, Un Geom’s sword transformed into a ray of light, generating a dozen or so red lines of sword qi.

Gal Cheon-rip’s thoughts were cut off by the sword flying toward his entire body. It wasn’t a time to be surprised by the opponent’s skills. Wasn’t that sword aimed to take his life?

He gulped the empty wind and twisted his body, moving the blade. He swung the blade again, feeling like he would puke out blood after slashing away the sword.

‘This cannot happen.’

This could not be happening.

‘I am Gal Cheon-rip!’

He should have led all these people to Qinghai and been a leader of the Evil Sect.

Right. Of course, he should have. What he lacked was awareness, not skills. Now that he realized that fact, even if it was late, he should have naturally gotten it.

Fame. Honor. Glory. Power.

All of that!

‘This Gal Cheon-rip should have risen to the same position as Jang Ilso. I am not someone who falls to a nameless swordsman!’

Paat!

At that moment, the sword qi of Un Geom soared high, cutting past the blade qi and biting into his side.

Swish!

He felt a sharp pain rise in his side as red blood poured down his body.

“T-This fucking bastard!”

A beastly howl burst out of Gal Cheon-rip’s mouth, and his face contorted like a demon’s.

“UGHHHHH!”

The blue blade qi that Gal Cheon-rip released swept out like a storm.

It seemed like just a reckless swing of the blade with leftover internal qi. But it was an attack that proved how a reckless blade could deliver a strong attack if it held speed and strength.

The storm-like blade qi erupted all around, centered on Gal Cheon-rip, and moved to devour Un Geom.

Kakang!

Un Geom quickly stepped back and swatted away the flying blade qi. The blade qi, filled with evil energy and killing intent, chased Un Geom like a devil from hell.

“DIEEEE!”

The blade qi flew more fiercely. This time, it seemed determined to take down Un Geom.

“Kuak!”

A groan escaped Un Geom’s mouth for the first time. As if responding to the sound, the blade qi, with more force, moved to engulf him.

But it wasn’t Un Geom who was hit by that blade qi.

“Eukl!”

One of the Evil Sect members fighting against a Mount Hua disciple turned around as he felt an eerie sensation.

And then.

“EUKKKKK!”

Gal Cheon-rip’s blade qi devoured the Evil Sect members from behind.

“ACKKKKK!”

“That basta... Ackk!”

Those cut down by the blade qi could only leave behind desperate screams.

There was no need to explain what happened to those swept away by the storm of blade qi that engulfed everything. Red blood splashed around the battlefield.

“Euk!”

“Insane bastard!”

The disciples of Mount Hua, who witnessed the sight of their opponents being swept away by the blade qi, stepped back in shock.

The Evil Sect members were also busy avoiding it.

Weeng!

The spinning blade qi stopped with a whirring sound. The sight that was finally revealed was horrific beyond description.
