The ground was drenched in blood, and the clothes and pieces of flesh scattered around were so badly torn that it was hard to guess who they belonged to.

“Weik, euk... Euk!”

Gal Cheon-rip stuck his blade into the ground to catch his breath. Because he had suddenly drawn up a huge amount of internal qi, his face was pale as a sheet.

Everyone was speechless and looked at him.

At that moment, Gal Cheon-rip gritted his teeth to say something.

“Even a beast...”

It was Un Geom.

Un Geom, who had witnessed the tragedy this man caused, had killing intent visible in his eyes.

“... wouldn’t bite down on those who follow it.”

“...”

“I will give you a death that suits you.”

Un Geom stepped forward, trampling on the puddle of blood that had gathered under his feet.

Chapter 885: Those Drunk On The Past Are Dead (5)

While dealing with Gal Cheon-rip, Un Geom never let his manners slip.

No matter how much the opponent was a leader of the Evil Sects, he believed that certain manners had to be observed when facing someone risking their life and fighting with a weapon. That was Un Geom’s way of teaching Mount Hua’s disciples.

However.

Now, the respect for his opponent had completely vanished from Un Geom’s eyes.

He also knew that there were people in the world who didn’t deserve even the minimum of respect.

And...

The eyes of Mount Hua’s disciples, who had stopped their swords and were looking at Gal Cheon-rip, were fiercer than Un Geom’s.

Stabbing your own comrade in the back?

At least in Mount Hua, such an act was unforgivable.

As one hundred swordsmen from a single sect glared at him with killing intent, Gal Cheon-rip flinched as he looked around.

“What?”

As if he did not understand the situation, his face twisted in pale confusion. But there was not a single person who would kindly open their mouth to explain the situation to him.

They just glared at him with ice-cold eyes.

“What the hell! These damned bastards!”

When Gal Cheon-rip shouted, Un Geom lowered his sword and slowly spoke.

“You...”

A cold and indifferent tone pierced Gal Cheon-rip’s ears.

“You are not qualified to lead people.”

Even though his intentions were not good, these people had gathered here and were all people who believed in and fought for him. If Gal Cheon-rip cared about them even a little, he wouldn’t have stabbed his allies in the back while being drunk in his fight.

At Un Geom’s cold words, Gal Cheon-rip’s gaze turned to the side without his knowledge. The eyes of the Evil Sect people who had stopped their blades and weapons were looking his way with killing intent.

“...fucking morons.”

It was something he couldn’t understand.

“You morons! Are you so stupid that you don’t get it? If I don’t defeat this one-armed bastard here, we all die! I need to win at any cost for you all to survive!”

And dozens of them have stabbed others in the back, yet they still looked at him with those eyes?

‘You morons don’t even know what is right and wrong!’

He had only hit five of them at most.

When attacking on the battlefield, wasn’t it common to get hit by your own comrades’ weapons?

But the Evil Sect people who heard his words only bared their teeth at Gal Cheon-rip.

“This moron...”

“So hopeless.”

Just as Gal Cheon-rip was about to curse, Un Geom cut him off as if he couldn’t listen to him anymore and started to approach him.

The tip of jos sword was covered in a reddish glow.

“This...”

Un Geom, the disciples of Mount Hua, and even the Evil Sect people all looked at him with hostile eyes. It was as if the entire world hated him.

Feeling cornered, Gal Cheon-rip clenched his teeth.

No.

This wasn’t it.

This wasn’t the gaze he should be receiving.

The eyes looking at him should be filled with respect and fear. And jealousy along with horror. The gaze of someone who dared not even think of rising to that position, looking at the absolute.

“You cannot match Jang Ilso.”

Clench!

Those emotions that were in his eyes whenever he thought of Jang Ilso should be in the eyes of everyone here.

But...

“But why are you looking at me like that! Why! WHHYYYY!”

A beastly roar burst out of Gal Cheon-rip’s mouth.

He could not admit it!

He absolutely could not!

“I will kill you!”

Gal Cheon-rip roared and rushed towards Un Geom. A violent killing intent and a storm-like force. The sight of Gal Cheon-rip rushing ahead with his eyes rolled back and his face covered with nerves was terrifying in itself.

Kwakwakwang!

The Evil Sect’s blade qi, which he released, was gushing out with incredible force. No matter how much one hated Gal Cheon-rip when they saw this, they couldn’t help but acknowledge his skills.

‘Fucking dog!’

The blood vessels in Gal Cheon-rip’s eyes strained to the limit, began to burst. His eyes quickly turned bloodshot.

In martial arts, the strong were respected. Only the strong were right.

The weak feared the strong and followed them. The qualification to lead someone? Strength was what that was.

Didn’t Jang Ilso create the Ten Thousand People Clan with his own strength and seize the Evil Sect?

‘Then what is this nonsense you are talking about!’

Kwakwakwang!

The blade qi spun more violently. A new blade qi began to create a trajectory above the extended blade qi, and dozens of blade qi irregularly entangled, creating a fierce storm.

A human body would be reduced to nothing more than a chunk of meat in the blade qi storm, which swept in with such incredible power.

Meanwhile, the sight of Un Geom standing in front of that ferocious storm of blade qi with a single hand and sword looked like the sight of a man standing before a typhoon.

But.

Kuak.

Kwak Ho, who had been watching the scene, clenched his fist quietly.

No one stepped forward. No one even shouted out to tell him to be careful.

Everyone simply looked at Un Geom’s back with eyes filled with deep trust.

If they were the disciples of Mount Hua, if they had seen the kind of training Un Geom had done over the past three years, they all knew. Worrying about Un Geom now would be nothing more than belittling the blood he had shed and the effort he put in.

What should fill them was not a sense of worry.

But faith in their teacher.

“Hoo.”

Un Geom took a short, deep breath.

It would be a lie if he said he wasn’t afraid. That formidable blade qi could tear his body apart at any time.

In the past, he wouldn’t have dared to confront it and would have retreated again. And then...

Un Geom seemed to see a sight in his two eyes. The figure of a person jumping ahead to protect him. A slightly smaller shoulder standing proudly in front of him.

Un Geom’s lips curled up into a smile.

He was standing here to avoid seeing that sight.

Kuak.

The tips of his feet pressed firmly against the ground. Everything always starts from below.

He kept his mind calm like a lake, and his two eyes observed the opponent with intelligence.

He could see it.

‘Internal qi isn’t everything. Even technique isn’t everything.’

It was fierce and strong beyond comparison.

But it was definitely off.

Once upon a time, these blade attacks would have been intricately intertwined like gears, slaughtering their opponents. But not anymore. These attacks had become rusty, covered in dust and creaking.

Gal Cheon-rip’s gap and twisted attacks.

‘Right. Chung Myung. Guess you were right.’

Gal Cheon-rip must have believed that he had gotten stronger. As time passed, his internal qi would have grown stronger, and the power he carried in his sword would have become stronger.

But that wasn’t everything for martial arts.

No matter how powerful a technique was, if it was not managed delicately and consistently, it would eventually rust.

This attack was ultimately just a gear that was twisted and forced to turn.

Un Geom took a step forward.

It seemed reckless to approach that fierce blade qi storm, but Un Geom’s eyes as he extended the feet were as calm as the lake.

Woong.

His sword, filled with his internal qi, trembled.

‘Can I be an example to those kids now?’

To his shame, those kids still call him their teacher. They still showed him their respect and admiration, even though there were more than a few who had surpassed him and gone before him.

That fact was powerful, but sometimes it felt like a burden that weighed him down.

What should he show the kids? What should he do to help those consistent kids? How could he not be ashamed to be called a teacher by those kids?

Without a shred of fear, Un Geom took another step forward.

The force of the blade qi made his hair stand on end and shook his skin as if it would tear at any moment.

At that moment.

Paat!

The plum sword in his left hand pierced through the storm of blade qi like a ray of light.

Kakakak!

As if a metal bar had been pushed into these heatedly rotating gears, the seemingly delicate blade attacks suddenly shook violently.

Paat!

The sword of Un Geom, which had been retrieved in an instant, released light.

Wasn’t it obvious what he had to show those kids?

The will to face things fearlessly. And the determination not to be dazzled by flashiness and to keep to his original intention!

And!

‘It is all just a façade.’

If one evaluated the strength contained in the tip of the sword, if they evaluated the depth of its process, if they evaluated how much more flashy and strong the attack could be, the world would naturally consider the opponent to be strong. Un Geom would have been the same.

But now he knew. This wasn’t what martial arts was.

Now, his disciples had taken their first proper step into the murim world. In the not-too-distant future, they, too, would face a crisis and be shaken.

He had just one thing to say to those kids.

‘You need to believe.’

The feeling of the sword in his hand vanished.

But the dull weight was clearly felt. The weight in the hand wasn’t the weight of the sword. It was the confidence and trust in himself that he hadn’t been generous to himself for even a moment in the past.

‘Just the effort.’

The sword of Un Geom became a ray of light again and cut through the storm of blade qi.

One sword became two, and two to three.

Within a moment, Un Geom’s sword, which had released seven sword forms, stabbed through the gap between the red blade qi without shaking an inch.

Kakakaka! Kakakak!

A creepy noise erupted as the sword and blade became entangled with each other.

Clench! Clench!

A pain that felt as if his wrist would break. A shock that twisted his internal organs.

Un Geom, who forcefully gulped down the blood rushing from his throat, gripped the sword so tightly that his knuckles turned white.

Observe.

No matter the situation, both eyes must watch everything.

The blade attack, spinning like a storm, was like a gear with an indestructible foreign substance stuck in it, shaking violently.

And the Plum Blossom Sword of Un Geom, which had not rested even once for three years, didn’t miss that moment.

‘Now!’

Lightning Movement.

The tip of the sword held effort.

The tip of the sword held time.

And the sword was a person. It held a person too.

The moment everything was contained in a sword, without a single doubt, the sword became the person, and the person became the sword.

Paaaat!

In an instant, the sword qi, which had become a ray of light, dug into the swirling porcelain.

Kakaka!

The gears, twisted as much as they could be, collapsed like an illusion before the powerful attack.

The red sword qi and the bright blue blade qi vanished.

The sword, filled with patience, effort, qi, and a human’s grasp, deflected the fluttering blade. Soon, it dug into the body of the person, who opened his eyes wide in shock.

Puaak!

The strange feeling in his entire arm disappeared as if it had been washed away, and the world returned to him. A dull and heavy sensation was felt at his fingertips.

‘And...’

If there was one more thing to believe.

Un Geom quietly turned his gaze. He saw his disciples clenching their fists as if they wanted to scream. And behind them were their teachers, who seemed even more excited than the kids.

‘These are the ones standing behind you.’

Believe in yourself, and believe in those who were with you.

That was enough.

Chapter 886: We Will Meet Again Soon (1)

Gal Cheon-rip lowered his head and looked at his chest.

More than half of the sword with a faint white glow was embedded in him. The tip of the sword was probably poking out through his back.

Despair, fear, frustration, anger, and more.

Countless emotions flashed through his eyes in an instant. But if one had to pick the most prominent of those emotions, it would be shock.

“You...”

His mouth opened with difficulty.

But his trembling jaw prevented him from speaking properly. In the end, red blood flowed out before the words.

“You...”

The blood that was flowing out had clogged up in his throat.

Un Geom tried to twist the sword, but he let go and faced Gal Cheon-rip.

“Once...”

“....”

“You, too, could have achieved what you wanted.”

Gal Cheon-rip looked at Un Geom with blurry eyes.

“If only you hadn’t been satisfied with the small achievement.”

“....”

Un Geom, who was about to say something more, shook his head.

What he originally wanted to say was, ‘I am not that great either, but a warrior should never stop moving ahead until the moment of death, right?’

But those words meant nothing to this man. Gal Cheon-rip was not someone who deserved to be treated as a warrior.

“Death is fair, whether you are good or bad. If there is an afterlife, atone for your sins and live.”

However, he never forgot his duty as a Taoist till the very end.

Gal Cheon-rip stepped back hesitantly.

It wasn’t because he was overwhelmed by Un Geom’s force. He was so close to death that he could barely stand.

“I... am...”

The blood that surged up blocked his mouth. The world did not give him what he wanted until the very end.

His head turned to the side with difficulty.

Not only the disciples of Mount Hua who were surrounded, but also the Evil Sect people he had led, all watched his death with cold eyes. There was not even the slightest warmth there.

“Cough...”

Gal Cheon-rip’s body slowly collapsed.

“Fucking dog...”

Thud.

In the end, Un Geom’s sword dug deeper into Gal Cheon-rip’s chest as he fell forward. That was enough to sever the lifeline that he had been barely holding on.

The corpse of Gal Cheon-rip, who had just closed his eyes and was breathing his last, slowly began to cool.

Only then did a deep sigh escape Un Geom’s mouth.

It had been a strong opponent. If he had been rusty, unable to use his full abilities, it would have been a tough battle.

But he was the one who emerged victorious.

“Dorm leader!”

“Senior Sasuk.”

The people who had come near him checked if UN Geom was injured. It was a short battle, but it was fierce enough to make the onlookers tense.

“I am fine.”

At that moment, Un Geom nodded to them.

Step. Step.

One person slowly walked forward.

The Evil Sect members held their breath as if they had forgotten how to breathe in his presence.

Step. Step.

He approached with unhurried steps, flipped Gal Cheon-rip over with his toe, and pulled out Un Geom’s sword, which was embedded in the chest.

Sskk.

The eerie sound of the sword being pulled out pierced everyone’s ears.

Srrk!

Chung Myung, who had wiped the blood off the sword, turned his head and looked around at the Evil Sect members, who wore tense expressions.

Flinch.

Those who met Chung Myung’s gaze were shaken by his presence.

“Should we continue?”

There were many words in the world.

But at this moment, could there be any more appropriate words to break their fighting spirit?

As the battle paused for a moment and the blood boiling in their heads cooled down, the surrounding situation became clear.

Gal Cheon-rip lay dead with eyes wide open. Behind him were the Soul Breaking Killer and other warriors, their corpses cooling, and those who were not dead but subdued and collapsed, groaning....

Even Mount Hua disciples continued to glare at them with cold gazes.

The situation was too obvious.

Tak.

The sound of a weapon falling from someone’s grasp echoed clearly in the quiet garden.

Ripples in a calm lake tend to spread farther and larger. That small sound completely extinguished the remaining will to fight.

Weapons fell one after another from the hands of the Evil Sect members.

They had taken up arms without any great purpose in the first place. There was no reason left to risk their lives and fight when there was no one to lead them or shout commands.

“Those morons.”

Chung Myung glared at them with a cold gaze, as if despising them. If only he could cut off their heads, whether they fought back or not.

When they had strength, they went wild and ran, but when they sensed a disadvantage, they begged for their lives. These were the people Chung Myung despised the most.

As he gripped the sword tightly, someone tapped his shoulder lightly.

When he turned around, Un Geom was shaking his head.

“We have shed too much blood.”

“... I know.”

He clicked his tongue lightly and politely handed the sword in his hand to Un Geom.

Un Geom, with the sword back in his hand, turned his gaze to the person approaching him.

“Disciples, listen up!”

“Yes, sect leader!”

Hyun Jong spoke with a heavy voice.

“Crush the dantian of the criminals and subdue them, imprison them all. Their punishment will be decided later.”

“Yes!”

“Move the wounded to the physician hall. Look around to see if there are any enemies around, and prevent any harm to the civilians!”

“Yes, sect leader!”

As soon as Hyun Jong finished speaking, Mount Hua’s disciples moved in unison.

The Evil Sect members, who had thrown away their weapons and knelt, were lost in thought at the words that their dantian would be destroyed. But they couldn’t dare to rebel, recalling the sight of those who had swung their swords with killing intent.

“Kuak!”

“Ah-ack!”

The terrible feeling of their dantian breaking and their internal qi vanishing was overwhelming.

However, they didn’t even have time to groan amidst the exhaustion and the strength draining from their bodies. A vicious hand pinned them to the ground and tied their entire bodies tightly with ropes brought from somewhere.

Hong Dae-Kwang, who was watching the scene, completely collapsed as if his legs had lost strength.

Watching Mount Hua’s swordsmen subdue the remaining members of the Evil Sect and Hyun Jong carrying out the orders, he felt so empty that he couldn’t even help them.

“... huh, huhu. So easily...”

Everything happened in a flash, from Mount Hua’s appearance to the beheading of Soul Breaking Killer and Gal Cheon-rip.

‘Does this make sense?’

Hong Dae-Kwang knew the true value of Mount Hua better than anyone else. He was the one who recognized the value of Mount Hua, which had neither fame nor skill and came here, even turning down the role of the Nanyang branch leader.

But the scene unfolding before his eyes was absurd even by his standards.

The vicious demons who were so notorious in the South.

They fell like mere third-rate warriors, not by the hands of Shaolin, Wudang, or Southern Edge, but in the hands of Mount Hua.

They got stronger... it was surprising, but it could happen. Didn’t Mount Hua always easily surpass Hong Dae-Kwang’s expectations? However, the reason he was flustered now wasn’t because of how strong Mount Hua got.

Hyun Jong, who was watching his disciples subdue others, turned around. And he began to walk briskly towards Hong Dae-Kwang.

Gulp.

Watching that sight, Hong Dae-Kwang unconsciously gulped.

As he watched Hyun Jong approaching him with steady steps and the elders of Mount Hua with disciples following behind him like guards, his body shrank down.

‘What has changed?’

In the past, Hong Dae-Kwang would have greeted Hyun Jong with a smile. He might have even teased him for arriving too late.

But now, Hong Dae-Kwang was straightening his posture, ignoring the pain throughout his body. It was as if he were meeting the abbot of Shaolin or Wudang.

If he were to describe what he felt from Hyun Jong at that moment in crude terms, he would have to say, “attack.”

Hyun Jong, standing in front of Hong Dae-Kwang and the people of Xi’an city who had gathered, slowly took them all in.

Although there was no sense of hostility, a strangely large presence weighed down on those facing Hyun Jong.

“Sect leader....”

In the end, someone who couldn’t overcome the weight of the situation hurriedly opened his mouth to express his gratitude.

Hyun Jong slowly bowed his head. Very deeply.

“S-sect leader!”

“Why are you doing that!”

Those who saw that were shocked and shouted. Some rushed to raise Hyun Jong up but couldn’t bring themselves to do so, standing still while others trembled, going pale.

And a low voice came from Hyun Jong’s mouth as he bowed down.

“I am sorry.”

“...”

At that moment, everyone fell silent.

“The sect arrived too late, and the damage to the various sects was too much. I hope you will understand and forgive me for not doing what Mount Hua should have done.”

Someone bit down on their lips at those unexpected words.

“... don’t do this, sect leader.”

Full Moon Clan leader Nam Ja-Myung opened his mouth, sighing.

“If Mount Hua hadn’t come, everyone here would have died.”

Nam Ja-Myung, who was about to express his gratitude, bit his lips and then clenched his fists as he said,

“We, the Full Moon Clan, are Southern Edge’s sub-sect. If things were as they were, the sect leader wouldn’t have been the one to save us. But... you bow your head to us. No one would have criticized Mount Hua if it hadn’t come down from Closed Gates...”

His words trailed off, and soon, he clasped his hands and extended them ahead. Then he bowed deeply, truly expressing his gratitude and respect.

“... I am truly grateful for Mount Hua.”

“Thank you!”

“Thank you, sect leader!”

The clan leaders of Xi’an, the sub-disciples of Mount Hua, and the allied clans all bowed their heads. Even those who weren’t in good shape, despite their injuries, showed their sincere gratitude without caring about their own bodies.

Only then did Hyun Jong slowly straighten his bowed back.

“I am grateful that you consider us, who are lacking, with such generous hearts.”

At those words, the faces of the clan leaders all flashed with emotions that were tough to express.

‘It is done.’

Hong Dae-Kwang realized it at that moment.

Perhaps the clan leaders of Xi’an, no, everyone belonging here, would never forget this sight for the rest of their lives.

Sometimes, a single word could capture a person’s heart more than a strong force. It had to be like this.

‘Even the sect leader has changed from the past.’

Gentleness and tolerance. And an overwhelming presence. None of them are lacking in the qualifications a Great Sect’s leader should have.

The fact that Mount Hua had become a completely different sect than before was realized anew by others. After realizing this, Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes naturally wandered to another person.

The one who had created all these changes.

“Uh?”

At that moment, Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes narrowed.

‘What is this?’

Chung Myung, who would normally be busy running around or at least guarding, was blankly standing in the center, staring into the distance.

“What is he looking at?”

Baek Cheon, who seemed to have the same doubts as Hong Dae-Kwang, approached Chung Myung and asked.

“What could you be looking at?”

“Uh.”

Chung Myung snorted softly and twisted his lips at this odd look.

“I was just looking at how big the little ones had become.”

“... your little ones are in front of you.”

“No, not those ones. But the real little ones.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon asked back, not knowing what was happening, but Chung Myung didn’t answer and turned away.

“Let us get this sorted out. I want to finish this quickly and head back to Mount Hua.”

“... maybe because we haven’t seen anyone in a while, he is speaking such nonsense.”

The corners of Chung Myung’s lips slightly turned up, so Baek Cheon tilted his head with a puzzled expression.

“You always seem to be a step behind, sahyung.”

“Uh.”

There was a subtle discomfort in the snort.

“Isn’t that a pity?”

“What are you even saying?”

“You haven’t seen how strong Taoist Chung Myung has become. Your sajae...”

“If you have time to babble, go back and swing your sword. We haven’t lifted the closed gate yet.”

“Yes, sahyung. I will do so.”

The outskirts of Xi’an.

A group of warriors who had entered a path that was not noticeable to people turned around without hesitation and hurried back.

The one who was following behind stopped in his tracks and looked back.

“We will meet again, Taoist Chung Myung.”

Lee Song-Baek, smiling brightly, looked at Xi’an as if he was longing for it, then turned around and hurried on. Every step felt lively.

Chapter 887: We Will Meet Again Soon (2)

The situation unfolded as swiftly as lightning, and the recovery and resolution were equally rapid.

The disciples of Mount Hua, having completely destroyed the dantians of the Evil Sect members who surrendered or were injured, confined them to the warehouse in Eunha.

They had the opportunity to enter the treasure-filled warehouse they had long desired. Still, it was uncertain whether this brought them happiness.

Mount Hua’s first priority was naturally to treat the injured and collect the bodies.

“How is it?”

“... Whether it’s fortunate or not, it seems no more people will die.”

“Ah?”

Since the injuries couldn’t be treated solely in a physician’s hall, Hyun Jong converted an entire hall of Eunha into a medical center and gathered people from Xi’an to assist.

Hyun Young asked with a puzzled expression at the words of their trusted physician, Noh.

“Then it should be fortunate. What is unfortunate about this?”

“... that is...”

The physician glanced at Hyun Young’s expression and cautiously spoke.

“Those who are seriously injured will require a very long treatment period. At the very least, they will need to remain still for at least three months while receiving treatment.”

“So?”

“Even if they recover, they will not be able to use their bodies as they once did.”

“Ahh.”

Hyun Young nodded, understanding the situation.

“Don’t worry about that.”

“That... to be precise, as you know, treating such serious wounds requires expensive medicine. No matter how much you try to save them...”

“Don’t worry, physician.”

Hyun Young firmly waved his hand.

He understood that the cost of these expensive medicines was beyond what ordinary people could afford. It was natural for them to feel anxious when treating others. They might have gathered the medicines, but they might not even receive a proper price for what they were using.

“Mount Hua will cover all the treatment costs for those injured here.”

“I-Is that true?”

“Yes.”

Hyun Young nodded as if it was the most natural thing in the world.

“If you do that...”

This decision had already been agreed upon with Hyun Jong.

In truth, there was no obligation for Mount Hua to pay for their treatment. Initially, the threat wasn’t directed at Mount Hua but at Xi’an. Whether Mount Hua was involved or not, the attack would have targeted Xi’an.

However, such situations shouldn’t be clouded by petty reasoning or profit and loss concerns.

-If we only adhere to logic, they will do the same to us. While it’s not bad to have a relationship where both sides follow logic, it’s not the kind of relationship I desire.

It was true.

It must have been even more accurate since he noticed that a certain individual, who always became attentive when money was mentioned, nodded silently.

“We also don’t want to charge exorbitant fees for those who fought against the Evil Sect. But if we skimp on medicine and don’t provide proper treatment, there could be many aftereffects.”

“Don’t worry about money, and treat them as best as you can.”

Hyun Young glanced around at the injured people lying on the white sheets.

“They showed their will. We shouldn’t let such people go without the treatment they deserve.”

Admiration escaped from physician Noh’s lips.

It was easy to applaud those who showed the will and spirit to fight. However, it was not easy to spend one’s own money on them.

Especially since Mount Hua wasn’t obligated to take responsibility for this.

He thought they might, at best, provide a place to stay, but he couldn’t help being amazed at how generously the sect was acting.

“Then, I will proceed accordingly.”

“Please take care of them. Our physician hall will also do its best to help.”

“Yes. That is a great help...”

Puak!

“ACKKKKK!”

“Ah, what kind of joke is this! Our sahyung doesn’t even groan when we stab them with a needle bigger than this!”

“L-lady... it hurts a lot...”

“Be quiet. Turn over now... uh? Uh? Not this place? Uh... it has been so long since I did this, I am getting confused...”

Just hearing that made the bones shiver down; the sound flowed out calmly.

“Well, it doesn’t really matter. I will poke it back in.”

“S-save me...”

Puaak!

“ACKKKKKK!”

The foot of the person who had been stabbed with a needle stretched out to the ceiling as if it would come out. Hyun Young and the physician, who had been blankly staring at the trembling foot, turned their heads away without saying a word.

“That...”

Hyun Young, who was about to say something, put his fist to his mouth and coughed.

“...please take good care of us.”

“Yes... elder.”

The conversation started off warmly but ended awkwardly.

“Have you collected all the disciples’ bodies?”

“Yes, father.”

Wei Lishan remained silent for a moment and nodded heavily.

Recalling the fierce battle they fought, it wasn’t as though there were many casualties. It was a fierce battle that wouldn’t have been surprising if they were all dead.

But, as the leader of a clan, it wasn’t something they could be happy about just because the casualties were fewer.

“... it is embarrassing to have survived.”

“Don’t say that, father. All of the disciples saw you fight more fiercely than anyone else as a leader.”

This wasn’t just a word of comfort for Wei Lishan.

Wei So-heng really thought so. He was proud to be a son of Wei Lishan.

And as far as he knew, the disciples of the Hwa-young sect didn’t differ much in their hearts from Wei So-heng.

At least in this battle, Wei Lishan had proven that he wasn’t simply leading the clan with the name of Mount Hua. All of the disciples must have known that he wasn’t just gentle and calm, but also had the heart of a warrior.

“What are you going to do with the bodies?”

“Shouldn’t we send them down to their hometown?”

“... Yes.”

Most of the other clans had sacrificed disciples from Xi’an, but the hometown of the Hwa-young clan was far from here, in Nanyang. At the very least, the kids had to be allowed to rest in their hometown.

“I should go there myself.”

“Father, you are not feeling well. I will go in your place.”

“No.”

Despite Wei So-heng’s dissuasion, Wei Lishan firmly shook his head.

“This is something I should do as the head of the Hwa-young clan.”

“Father....”

Wei Lishan smiled faintly.

“Don’t look at me like that. It is sad and regrettable. But you should be proud of your sahyungs. Weren’t they more righteous than anyone else?”

“Yes. I will never forget their end.”

As a stern light appeared in Wei So-heng’s eyes, Wei Lishan nodded lightly.

Wounds made people stronger.

It was heartbreaking, but this incident would make the disciples of the Hwa-young clan even more of a warrior. That was the only consolation.

“Then we should hurry. It is a long way from here.”

“N-No.”

“Hmm? Before the body gets damaged...”

“Mount Hua’s Sect leader has decided to procure ice crystals. He said it would take a day or two, so you can wait.”

“... Ice crystals?”

Wei Lishan bit his lower lip at this.

He said he would use the precious ice crystals to protect the bodies... This was enough to surprise Wei Lishan, who knew well about Hyun Jong’s nature.

“If there are ice crystals, the bodies won’t get damaged. Of course, we indeed need to leave as soon as possible, but since it will take two days for the crystals to get here, it would be better to rest....”

Wei Lishan quietly closed his eyes.

If the ice crystals were in Mount Hua, it wouldn’t take two days to retrieve them. However, if there was no ice and they had to obtain it, returning within two days would be impossible. This must have been Hyun Jong’s concern, worried that Wei Lishan might leave without fully recovering.

“... let us do that.”

“Yes, father.”

Wei Lishan said, giving a quick glance.

“So-heng.”

“Yes.”

“Have you met Taoist Chung Myung?”

“...”

Wei So-heng shook his head. Wei Lishan smiled faintly.

“It is said that this is something anyone living in Murim needs to experience.”

“Yes, I understand.”

“So there is no need to suppress it.”

“...”

“It is the order of the clan leader, so go and express your gratitude to Taoist Chung Myung and the disciples of the main sect.”

“But...”

“That is also your duty as the young clan leader.”

Wei So-heng nodded, realizing he had no other choice.

“Yes. I will.”

“Good. Do so.”

“Yes.”

Wei So-heng carefully got up, bowed his head, and left. He slowly walked out of his dwelling and headed to the Eunha manor, where Mount Hua stayed.

Knock! Knock!

Initially, his pace was slow, but it soon became faster and faster, eventually turning into a brisk walk.

‘Taoist, Chung Myung!’

After Chung Myung saved him, he hadn’t been able to properly thank him. The situation hadn’t allowed for it.

‘I should thank him.’

As the young clan leader, it was crucial to manage the clan’s affairs, but it was also natural to express gratitude to the person who saved his life.

He increased his pace until he was out of breath, running to the edge of the Eunha in a single breath.

But...

Wei So-heng’s feet, which were about to reach Eunha Hall, began to slow down.

Then he stopped.

A strange expression appeared on his face, accompanied by hesitation.

If it had been like before, he would have jumped right in and run to where Chung Myung was, but strangely, his feet felt heavy.

‘Why now?’

Wei So-heng tilted his head, unable to understand this change in himself.

But he had to say thank you, so he stepped into Eunha. Yet, no matter how much he looked, he couldn’t find any Mount Hua disciples.

“Excuse me.”

“Uh?”

Wei So-heng asked a passing laborer with a slightly stiff face.

“Where are the Mount Hua people now?”

“They are probably in the diner. It’s time to eat. It’s the big pavilion over there; do you see it?”

“Ah, thank you.”

Wei So-heng bowed and slowly walked toward the diner. But this inexplicable heaviness kept tugging at his ankles.

When he almost reached the diner, he saw a familiar face. The way he hesitated, unable to enter, was similar to Wei So-heng....

“Branch leader?”

“Uh? Young clan leader.”

Hong Dae-Kwang looked at Wei So-heng and nodded with a slightly shocked expression.

“What are you doing here?”

Wei So-heng forced himself to swallow the words that were about to escape him, ‘like a puppy that wants to poop.’ Then Hong Dae-Kwang sighed.

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon... No, I came here because I had something to say to Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Yes.”

“That... should I say it is a bit awkward?”

Wei So-heng opened his eyes wide.

“I know that Mount Hua has grown stronger... well, it’s nice, and I feel proud, but somehow, the appearance of Mount Hua I saw this time was so different from what I know, so it feels awkward....”

As he listened to Hong Dae-Kwang’s hesitant words, Wei So-heng finally realized the source of the awkwardness he felt along the way.

‘They feel so far away.’

If he met them for no reason, he was afraid they wouldn’t be like the ones from the past.

He thought it was a trivial concern, but seeing Hong Dae-Kwang feeling the same way, it seemed that the sight Mount Hua showed him was unfamiliar.

“... shouldn’t we go in anyway?”

“Ugh.”

Hong Dae-Kwang groaned.

If he were alone, he wouldn’t care, but he couldn’t act nervous in front of Wei So-heng.

“Let us head in.”

If Wei So-heng hadn’t come, he would have put his ear to the wall and listened to what was being said inside, but now that things had come to this, he had no choice but to head in.

Hong Dae-Kwang, trying hard to look calm, opened the pavilion door in one swift motion.

“Uh, Mount Hua’s Sword...”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s mouth, which was about to call out, closed on its own.

As soon as he opened the door, everyone inside fell silent and turned to him.

No, it was definitely that sight even before he entered.

‘T-this is...’

It wasn’t the noisy Mount Hua he knew. The faces of those looking in his direction were frozen.

It was a sight that couldn’t be found in the Mount Hua of the past, which always welcomed outsiders.

Hong Dae-Kwang was about to let out a groan as he realized his guess was right.

“NOOOOO!”

‘Uh?’

The familiar back of a head suddenly shot up and screamed.

“W-what! What kind of bastards are those who are trying to feed them again! Huh!”

“... How many times have I told you? They will starve to death like that!”

“Whether I starve to death, boil, or die, is it my business? Anyway, these people are full! Do you know how many people in the villages still cannot get even a proper meal a day? And what? Food? FOOD? Are you going to give food to those people who cannot even drink right? If you give food to them, I will feed them to the dogs on the streets! Dogs!”

“No... they will really die! They will really die, I am telling you!”

“Ah? Ah... ah!”

Chung Myung clapped his hands loudly.

“I understand what you mean.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon, who had been persuading him with a tired face, suddenly felt ominous and looked at Chung Myung.

Chung Myung asked with a refreshed expression.

“So, are you worried about dying?”

“R-right. Even if they are Evil Sect’s bastards, starving them to death....”

“Then let us just kill them in advance so they don’t starve to death.”

“... Uh?”

“If I go and cut all their heads off right now, wouldn’t that solve everything?! Just wait! I will make sure they never have to eat again!”

“Catch him!”

“Catch that bastard!!”

“Ah! Please, let me eat right, you bastard!”

“If you have time to grumble, catch him!”

Chung Myung, who was about to run away with his sword drawn, was suddenly attacked and held down by the disciples of Mount Hua.

“Let go! Will you not let go! Have you all lost it? Are you attacking me now to save the Evil Sect? Since when have you been fighting alongside them? It wasn’t like this in my time! In my time!”

“Ah, shut up! Shut it! Your mouth!”

“Ah! Hold him right there! There!”

“Please... please just let me eat. Please....”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who was blankly staring at the scene, suddenly heard a snickering in his ear.

“...”

Wei So-heng was laughing out loud like he had never been nervous.

‘Nothing has changed.’

Even if they got stronger and thus had a different status than before.

Mount Hua was Mount Hua.

“Taoist Chung Myung!”

Wei So-heng smiled brightly upon realizing that fact. And he ran while calling Chung Myung loudly.

Chapter 888: We Will Meet Again Soon (3)

“Abbot!”

Beop Gye, who hurriedly opened the door and barged in, let out another deep sigh.

In some ways, Beop Gye was more serious than the abbot of Shaolin. This was because he knew better than anyone the weight of assisting the abbot of Shaolin.

There was a reason why Beop Gye was acting so different from usual.

“Abbot! News from Shaanxi has arrived!”

The abbot tightly closed his eyes.

His prediction was right, but instead of feeling good, he felt dizzy.

“... was it Mount Hua?”

“Uh? H-how did you know?”

The abbot sighed.

“I had a feeling it would be as such.”

Beop Gye nodded and said.

“This is news from the Beggars Union. The Evil Sect stepped into Xi’an and attacked Eunha. The small to medium clans had joined forces with them in Xi’an to stop them.”

“... the small and medium clans? Is this true?”

“Yes.”

“... are they the sub-clans of Mount Hua and Southern Edge?”

“Even those who didn’t belong to them had fought together.”

The face of the abbot darkened.

“So, what happened?”

“Of course, they couldn’t stop them alone. But Mount Hua came running down in time and crushed them all.”

“... what did Southern Edge do?”

“Southern Edge... didn’t show up.”

The abbot stared at the ceiling in silence. After a long time, he finally spoke with a heavy voice.

“If what you said is indeed true....”

There was a bitter smile on the corner of his lips.

“So a great crisis hit Xi’an, and the Xi’an sects came together to fight back, and right before they were about to step back and retreat, Mount Hua appeared and took down the evil sect people?”

“Right.”

The abbot opened his mouth again with an understanding expression.

“Then the Xi’an sects will feel the greatest gratitude to Mount Hua, and the news of Mount Hua subduing the Evil Sect and saving the common people will spread through the world in an instant.”

“....”

“While the other sects have struggled and sacrificed over the past three years to quell the chaos and suppress the Evil Sect, this sect, which shut its gates and stayed uninvolved, will now be praised the moment they reopen.”

The abbot continued to voice his disapproval of the situation.

“Even if one meticulously plans, things never go this smoothly. Why are such favorable outcomes happening only for Mount Hua?”

At this quiet thought, Beop Gye closed his mouth, hesitating to speak further.

In truth, Shaolin’s own actions were partly to blame.

No matter how distant Shaanxi was, if they had dispatched their disciples and headed to Xi’an with all their might upon hearing the news, they might have reached Xi’an before anyone else.

In fact, considering that they burned and massacred villages while on their way to Xi’an, they could have certainly arrived before the Evil Sect and joined forces with the smaller clans to defeat the enemies.

However, it was the abbot himself who decided against sending Shaolin monks to Xi’an.

So, when looked at objectively, the abbot’s words made little sense. Yet, Beop Gye couldn’t bring himself to point this out to the abbot.

“What about the damage to Mount Hua?”

“That...”

Beop Gye hesitated, struggling to answer, and finally spoke.

“I haven’t fully checked, but... there seems to be no significant damage. They reportedly defeated the enemies with ease.”

“With ease?”

The abbot’s face was filled with shock.

“Those led by the Seven Killers of Jiangxi came in droves, and you’re saying they were subdued without any damage? By Mount Hua?”

“Yes...”

The abbot’s lips trembled.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“Uh?”

“Yes, right. That child would have been there. Three years is enough time for a sleeping dragon to become a full-fledged dragon. If that child had come and stood ahead...”

“That...”

Beop Gye hesitated, as if it was still difficult to speak, then opened his mouth.

“According to the news from the Beggars Union, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint didn’t participate in this battle.”

“... What?”

The abbot’s eyes widened.

“Not only Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, but the Five Swords of Mount Hua, who were famous in the past for their fights, did not participate. So... the ones who subdued them this time were purely other disciples of Mount Hua...”

Thud.

The abbot’s hand fell on the table, and a small echo spread out. To Beop Gye, this was shocking.

He knew very well that the abbot wouldn’t be showing such behavior unless he was shaken.

“... What is this...”

A look of shock crossed the face of the abbot as he mumbled softly.

What kind of news was this? What had happened?

If it was the strength of Mount Hua he knew from the past, they should have fought fiercely against those who had come to Xi’an. Even if they were lucky and won, they should have taken significant losses.

Of course, since they had young and fast-growing warriors and closed their gates for three years, they should have been stronger than before, but even so...

‘I cannot tell for sure because I haven’t seen the battlefield with my own eyes, but it would be possible if they got three times stronger than before.’

Even if they were young, fast-growing warriors, was it possible for them to achieve this much growth in strength in just three years?

If what Beop Gye had said was true, then Mount Hua’s strength was now comparable to that of the Nine Great Sects. No, even within the Nine Great Sects, they could enter the middle-rank positions.

That Mount Hua...

The abbot mumbled blankly.

“When I... first saw them at the Worldly Martial Arts Tournament.”

“Yes, abbot.”

“I thought they would breathe new life into the murim world. The sects that had led the murim world for the past 100 years had grown too complacent. I believed that this young, new sect, led by a warrior like the Sword Saint, would be enough to put them all on alert.”

“....”

“But...”

A hollow laugh escaped the abbot’s mouth.

“I was complacent, too.”

That was true.

Now, Mount Hua was no longer a sect over which the abbot had any control.

“...send a message to Mount Hua.”

“What should we say?”

“We should congratulate them on opening their Closed Gate.”

“... I will do that.”

The abbot closed his eyes without responding, and Beop Gye quietly got up. The abbot seemed uncomfortable with the news, but there was no need to press the issue now.

As he carefully stepped away, the abbot opened his eyes again. A frustrated grumble escaped his lips.

“Amitabha...”

If the news of Mount Hua spread, it was clear that the murim world, which had just calmed down, would start to stir again. Whether they wanted it or not, Mount Hua had already become a sect that attracted the attention of the entire murim world.

However, that was not what the abbot was worried about.

‘What will happen now?’

The past three years had been a time of chaos, but in some ways, it wouldn’t have been wrong to call it a time of recovery rather than stability.

From the moment Mount Hua went into Closed Gates, no major incidents occurred in the murim world.

That was why it seemed audible to the abbot—the loud noise of a large wheel that had stopped starting to roll once again.

“... Amitabha.”

And now, no one could stop this flow.

Xi’an quickly regained stability.

The civilians who had left returned to Xi’an after hearing that the chaos had been suppressed and the turbulent atmosphere had calmed down.

Of course, it was a major crisis. Still, most of the fighting happened in the Eunha merchant manor, and the civilians were not harmed, so it was manageable.

Nevertheless, there were some who continued to feel anxious, but what reassured them was the news that Mount Hua was in Eunha now.

“So the Mount Hua sect opened their Closed Gate?”

“Yes. Isn’t that why we are here?”

“That is amazing. How could they open their gates at such a precise moment?”

“Don’t be foolish.”

“Uh?”

“It wasn’t a coincidence. Originally, Mount Hua had intended to continue their training. But when they heard that the Evil Sect people were coming, didn’t they boldly step out from their Closed Gate?”

“Right. Right. This makes more sense.”

The eyes of those who were talking turned to the main gate of Eunha merchant.

“Anyway, he is a great person.”

“Who are you talking about?”

“Who else? Of course, the sect leader of Mount Hua, Elder Hyun Jong! It must have been a decision to close the gates, but how easily did he decide to break them open and come out?”

“Ah, that sounds a bit odd. But since they are a Justice faction sect, shouldn’t they put everything aside and come help when the people are in danger?”

“What about Southern Edge then?”

“... uh?”

“Southern Edge. If what you said is right and obvious, what did Southern Edge do?”

“That....”

“I heard that your family has a member belonging to the Southern Edge too. Is it alright for you to say something like that about them?”

“Ugh.”

The person who glanced around whispered in a low voice.

“You aren’t the only one. I am the same. Where in Xi’an is someone not related to the Southern Edge? If you cross a few people, everyone has some kind of connection to Southern Edge.”

“Right. This is Xi’an, after all.”

“But you know... from now on, whenever something goes wrong, people will probably go look for Mount Hua instead of Southern Edge.”

“... do you think people would go to that extent?”

“If a robber broke into your house, who would you turn to?”

The person who heard the question couldn’t answer right away. For him, the two letters “Mount Hua” came before “Southern Edge.”

“I am not saying this just because Mount Hua came earlier than Southern Edge. Think about it. If it was Southern Edge... no, even if it wasn’t Southern Edge, would any sect have done something like this? Wouldn’t they have been troubling us a bit?”

“Right. But isn’t that entirely wrong, right? Didn’t they do something like that?”

“Right. It isn’t wrong. That is why Mount Hua is so great. You see, you cannot even see the members of the Mount Hua sect.”

“Wait, now that you mention it...”

The two people’s eyes turned to the gate of Eunha.

“They say a chivalrous man doesn’t show off his good deeds, but I didn’t know there was a place that kept that promise entirely. I didn’t know they would act like nothing happened after helping us all.”

“... they are so great.”

“Yes, great. Truly amazing.”

Everyone passing by Eunha’s manor said similar things.

Even those who didn’t particularly like Mount Hua couldn’t help but feel goodwill towards those who rushed down to save them and didn’t even bother taking credit.

“In the end, the only one who thinks about Xi’an is Mount Hua.”

“Be it Shaolin or Wudang, not a single one of them came to help Xi’an, right?”

“There is no need to go that far. Isn’t that the same for Southern Edge?”

“Oh my. Didn’t Southern Edge close their gate?”

“What about Mount Hua? Didn’t Mount Hua also do it? Both of them did it, but if only one broke it open, wouldn’t the evaluation be different?”

“That is true, but...”

The ordinary people didn’t understand how strong the Evil Sect people who attacked Xi’an were, so they couldn’t evaluate the power of Mount Hua.

However, they clearly understood that Mount Hua had rushed down barefoot to save Xi’an.

Even if Xi’an had been turned into a wasteland after the attack, the arrows would have been aimed at Southern Edge and not Mount Hua. Yet, how could they not praise those who came to save the common people on behalf of the sect considered their enemy?

As a result,

Mount Hua’s name was now more prominent than Southern Edge in Xi’an, and no one in Xi’an denied it.

At that moment,

The elders of Mount Hua, having completed some of the post-battle administration, sat around in the meeting room of Eunha Manor.

Chapter 889: We Will Meet Again Soon (4)

Hyun Jong, sitting at the head of the table, stared blankly at the entrance.

All kinds of boxes and bundles were piled up to the ceiling.

Hyun Jong, blinking slowly, turned his gaze to Hwang Jongi, sitting next to him.

“All of that...”

“They are gifts.”

“Gifts?”

Hyun Jong frowned.

After Mount Hua became famous, gifts from the locals would come flying in from time to time. Hyun Jong had always been reluctant to accept gifts because of one old man and one young man who would yell at him for refusing gifts when they were struggling financially.

But when he heard that gifts were flying in even to the Eunha merchants, not just Mount Hua, he felt a bit uncomfortable.

“... is there really a need to pile them up like that?”

Hwang Jongi smiled pleasantly as he watched Hyun Jong forcefully swallow the words, telling him to send them back.

“It isn’t what you are guessing. Those gifts are from the people of Xi’an, expressing their gratitude to Mount Hua.”

“... the people of Xi’an.”

“Yes. They are things like crops harvested from farming and items sold at stores. They are not valuable in terms of expensiveness, but we brought them inside since the sect leader would feel good.”

“Ahem. I see.”

Hwang Jongi’s eyes clearly reflected Hyun Jong’s slightly raised lips. When he guessed that the rich and powerful sent gifts, he couldn’t hide the displeasure, but when he heard that it was the commoners who expressed their gratitude, he couldn’t hide the joy.

‘Normally, it would be the other way.’

This was why the Mount Hua sect was so interesting.

“I am grateful.”

“Fortunately, it seems we weren’t too late.”

Hyun Jong nodded his head at Hyun Sang’s words.

At that moment, Hong Dae-Kwang, who had been watching from a corner, raised his hand slightly. Then a thunderous rebuke poured out.

“No.”

With just the word ‘no,’ everyone there immediately knew who had opened their mouth.

“This isn’t a market, and there are even some neighborhood beggars!”

“Ah, a beggar from the neighborhood? I am a member of the Beggars Union!”

“So why is that man, who is a member of the Beggars Union, here? Are you a member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance? Did the Beggars Union withdraw from the Nine Great Sects and join the Heavenly Friends Alliance without me realizing it?”

Hong Dae-Kwang smiled happily.

‘Even the neighborhood idiot will become serious after three years, you brat.’

How could three years have passed, and this man had not changed at all? It’s much too embarrassing to even call him consistent at this point.

“T-that isn’t important...”

“Woah, now you are deciding important things on your own. You should just gather all the beggars around and break down into something big.”

Hong Dae-Kwang desperately turned away from Chung Myung and asked Hyun Jong.

“Where have you been? I went up to Mount Hua, but there was no one there....”

“Ah.”

Hyun Jong answered calmly as if it was nothing.

“We sealed the place, but it was becoming hard to practice and train there, so we moved.”

“No, why...”

Hyun Young answered with a contorted expression instead of Hyun Jong.

“Every day, something breaks and shatters there, so what kind of practice could they even be doing there?”

“....”

Ah... right.

When Hong Dae-Kwang looked inside, it was so broken that he thought someone had destroyed it.

“It would have been better if we had moved then. If we stayed the same, we would have had to rebuild the entire Xi’an, forget just Mount Hua’s buildings. Even if there were only walls left, it would have been a closed gate still. But what kind of extreme training is this! Yah!”

Hyun Young clicked her tongue as if she was still upset at the thought of it, and Hyun Jong smiled bitterly.

“There were those problems, but even after the Closed Gate, people still came, making it hard to focus on training. So we had no choice but to vacate the sect land and go to a remote mountain where no one could visit us.”

As soon as he finished saying that, the Mount Hua disciples began to groan.

“While picking up grass roots.”

“Catching wild boars.”

“What? Would it be better to cut down the trees and build a shrine?”

When Hyun Young glared, the Mount Hua disciples turned their heads slightly.

Hong Dae-Kwang still had some questions left to ask.

“Then how did you know about this and arrive on time? Did you see the civilians of Xi’an fleeing after the Closed Gate was opened?”

“Hmm?”

Hyun Jong looked at Hong Dae-Kwang as if he couldn’t understand the question.

“What do you mean?”

“Uh?”

“Didn’t you come up to Mount Hua and look for us?”

“H-how can you know that? There was no one on Mount Hua.”

The moment Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes widened, Chung Myung’s chest bulged, and something white quickly popped out from inside. It stood on both feet on the table and thrust its stomach forward.

“...t-that thing.”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face went blank.

Baek Ah, who had climbed onto the table, looked proud. It was surprising how a beast could make such an expression like she looked proud. If she could speak, she would have definitely said, ‘Ahem!’

“That, that ugh. Look at it acting all high and mighty just because it paid off for one meal. I should have shaved off its fur a long time ago.”

When Chung Myung clicked his tongue, Baek Ah, who had been sticking out her belly, became sullen and lowered her gaze to look at Chung Myung with her large dark eyes.

Baek Cheon burst into laughter.

“It has earned enough to pay for its own food. If I could save even one more civilian, I could feed that guy for the rest of her life.”

“This guy eats as much as an ox! Did you even earn that much?”

“... if you say so.”

Chung Myung shook his head and glanced away.

“Well... there are people who cannot even pay for their own food.”

Flinch.

“No, no matter what it’s like. How can this man, who is as good as a Beggars Union member, not feel the presence of a single animal... what? There was no one? No one around?”

“....”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had nothing to say even if he had ten mouths, stared blankly at Baek Ah. Looking at the snow-white fur that stood out clearly, he couldn’t think of an excuse to say.

Fortunately, Hong Jong saved Hong Dae-Kwang.

“We couldn’t leave Mount Hua so empty. So I made that one stand guard. We had to take the dogs with us because we had to feed them, but this guy could come and eat when the time comes.”

“So... that is why I didn’t see it, but it saw me, realized something was wrong, and reported to Mount Hua?”

“Right.”

Hong Dae-Kwang slumped in his chair in despair.

“No... why am I being compared with an animal...”

He couldn’t bring himself to say that.

Well, thanks to the little one, he was able to avoid the bloody storm, so he should be thankful...

“Kiik!”

Why was the way it pounded the floor with its front paws so annoying? Just why?

“Anyway... I am glad.”

The fate of Xi’an was saved by a spirit beast. Even though he knew it, he couldn’t say anything.

“So, did you lift the Closed Gate seal now?”

“Yes.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

“Even if Baek Ah hadn’t brought us the news, I thought the preparations were almost done. The Closed Gate was taking longer than I thought, so I couldn’t delay it any longer.”

“But why did it take so long?”

Hyun Jong’s face turned pale at those words.

“... there was a guy who said that rice had to be cooked thoroughly to be cooked, but that damned rice was made of some kind of sand, and it wouldn’t cook even after boiling for 3 years. What can I do then?”

“Ah, is that my fault? It’s sahyung’s fault! You should say that your skills have improved already!”

Had a lot changed, though?

No, excessively improved skills?

Hong Dae-Kwang shivered as he recalled the sight of the Mount Hua swordsmen overpowering the Evil Sect members.

“But Sect leader.”

Hwang Jongi slowly asked Hyun Jong.

“Have you achieved the goal of the Closed Gate?”

Hyun Jong smiled faintly as if he understood.

“I don’t know what to say...”

A firm voice emerged from Hyun Jong’s mouth.

“At least now I have returned with the confidence that no one in the world would dare ignore the skills of Mount Hua.”

It was a remark filled with clear pride.

Hyun Jong wasn’t someone who got cocky easily. Wasn’t he always humble, even in situations where others would feel pressured? Just hearing him speak like that, you could guess the hardships and effort Mount Hua had to endure in the past.

‘No. I have already seen and confirmed it with my own eyes, so there is no need to even guess the hardships.’

Furthermore, the fact that even the Evil Sect, who would have attacked them immediately, kept their mouths shut was proof of it.

Of course, his expression constantly conveyed, ‘If you put pressure on yourself just because your skills have improved a little, you will get hit somewhere, and your shoulders will drop’. But wasn’t it amazing that he wasn’t saying it out loud but expressing it with his expression?

“Congratulations, sect leader!”

“Congratulations!”

Wei Lishan, who attended the meeting last with Hwang Jongi before leaving for Nanyang, congratulated them with a deep bow.

Hyun Jong accepted the congratulations with a smile, without any hesitation.

“This is all the result of the desperate efforts of the disciples.”

It was a heartwarming sight.

Even if the disciples of Mount Hua couldn’t hear him mumbling.

“If I didn’t do it, I would have died; what more can I do?”

“I am glad I came back alive. That is enough.”

“If I am going to do it again, I will just return to secular life and go home.”

“I dreamed of practicing while I was sleeping last night. When I woke up this morning, I wanted to jump into a well.”

“I almost went in and came out once.”

Hong Dae-Kwang desperately closed his ears and ignored their terrifying words.

‘Right, they have always been like this, right?’

Three years shouldn’t have been so long, so why was it so new and hard to adapt to this?

When the mood eased a bit, Hyun Young brought up the main topic.

“For now, we need to stabilize Xi’an a little more, but Mount Hua won’t have much to do.”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had naturally joined the meeting, casually asked Hyun Jong.

“Then what are you going to do after that?”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong smiled and looked at someone.

“Wouldn’t that brat know best about it?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s gaze slowly followed Hyun Jong’s to the side. Seeing Chung Myung clinging with a grumpy face, he somehow felt uneasy.

“But why do you keep looking so grumpy since a while back?”

“What about it?”

Baek Cheon snickered and patted Chung Myung’s head.

“Don’t worry about it, branch leader. He just came out of the Closed Gate, and he is pissed because he couldn’t swing his sword even once when facing the enemies.”

“I should have just peeled off those bastards’ skin!”

“...”

This bastard certainly hasn’t changed.

“More than that.”

Chung Myung slightly lifted his chin and spoke to Hong Dae-Kwang.

“I just came down from the mountain, so I think I should find out what is happening. Since the merchant lord and the beggar man are here, I guess I can ask them. Tell me.”

“From where?”

“First one.”

Chung Myung’s lips curled into a smile.

“That bastard, Jang Ilso, what is he doing now?”

His eyes were filled with blade-like light as he asked it.

Chapter 890: We Will Meet Again Soon (5)

Someone was running out of breath. No, to be exact, from the moment this person set foot in the huge hall, it became difficult to breathe in and out.

Hwang Se-Ak was by no means a man with excessive fear. If he had been such a weak person, he would have never created a great sect called the Red Pillar Association in the North of the River, where the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were running rampant with their eyes wide open.

But his guts and body, which were always as strong as iron, turned infinitely weak the moment he set foot in this hall.

He gulped, adjusted his clothes, and looked around.

The expression “splendid” clearly existed to describe a place like this.

The ceiling and walls were decorated with red and white silk, catching the eye, and everywhere he looked, he could see expensive decorations and elegant porcelain densely placed. Even the pillars that stood tall in the center of the hall were engraved with beautiful patterns in gold.

He couldn’t be sure that the palace where the Emperor resided would be this grand.

But it wasn’t this splendid hall that made Hwang Se-Ak feel intimidated.

Hwang Se-Ak, who carefully walked forward with tension, knelt down on one knee the moment he reached the end of the red silk on the ground.

Then he gulped and slowly raised his head, his gaze climbing the tall stairs in front of him. Finally, his eyes reached the feet of a man sitting on the throne atop the stairs.

He opened his mouth, somehow calming his trembling tone.

“Hwang Se-Ak, the leader of the Red Pillar Association, greets you, Alliance Lord.”

He bowed his head deeply and saw the man sitting on the throne.

This magnificent robe with gold thread embroidery, colorful jewels hanging from his body, pale white skin, and a strange languidness in his form and expression.

If one didn’t know who this person was in front of them after seeing that appearance, they didn’t deserve to live in the current Murim world.

The Alliance Lord of the Four Precepts Alliance.

The Hemeon of Ten Thousand.

‘The Hegemony Lord, Jang Ilso.’

Hwang Se-Ak’s heart began to pound uncontrollably.

When had he first heard the name of Jang Ilso? 15 years ago? Or maybe 10 years ago? He couldn’t exactly remember, but it must have been somewhere around that time.

At first, he was just a notorious warrior in the Guangxi region. But soon, he became famous throughout the southern lands and created a force called the Ten Thousand People Clan, ascending to the position of clan leader.

It wasn’t long before the excessive praise of the Shenzhou Five Clans was joined by that of the Ten Thousand People Clan...

‘The leader of the Four Precepts Alliance.’

He could say with certainty that Jang Ilso was the only one in the world who raised his status to such a degree in such a short duration.

“Hmm.”

The moment a faint nasal sound escaped from Jang Ilso’s mouth, Hwang Se-Ak flinched and crouched down as much as he could. He couldn’t even bring himself to face Jang Ilso.

Three years ago, Jang Ilso, who was the leader of the Ten Thousand People Clan, was someone he could never face. But the current Jang Ilso was incomparable to the past.

In just three years.

In just those three years, Jang Ilso subjugated the southern lands’ sects under his own. He killed all those who resisted and stabbed those who ran away with the sword.

He had finally achieved the absurd feat of unifying the Evil Sects, which everyone thought impossible.

Three years ago, Jang Ilso was just one of the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans, but now he controlled even the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans as his subordinates. He was the ruler of the Evil Sect, which no one could deny.

“Hwang Se-Ak....”

The moment his name came out of Jang Ilso’s mouth, Hwang Se-Ak lowered his head even further. He tried to look up slightly to see Jang Ilso. However, the high stairs didn’t allow him to glance at his complexion.

Flutter.

The sound of Jang Ilso’s long coat fluttering loudly reached Hwang Se-Ak’s ears.

Tap. Tap. Tap. Tap. Tap.

Then, the sound of Jang Ilso slowly descending the stairs echoed clearly. A thick, cold sweat began to run down Hwang Se-Ak’s back.

‘I should have arrived sooner.’

If he had known that the Four Precepts Alliance would take over the Southern lands, he would have come running, knelt down, and pledged his loyalty long ago. However, Hwang Se-Ak didn’t fully trust the Four Precepts Alliance and hesitated to make a decision.

The price for that hesitation was now this situation.

He must now give everything up and wait for Jang Ilso to decide. Even if Jang Ilso were to cut off his head because he was late in pledging loyalty, Hwang Se-Ak wouldn’t dare rebel now. Everything he had was at Jang Ilso’s fingertips.

Step. Step.

Jang Ilso came down the stairs and walked leisurely towards Hwang Se-Ak. The sound of the jewels dangling on his body, the sound of the large, long robe brushing against the floor, and the sound of the silk footwear stepping on the ground sounded like music coming out of hell.

“Get up and see.”

“....”

At the voice coming from just above his head, goosebumps ran all over Hwang Se-Ak’s body.

It felt like a snake with deadly poison was crawling up his back. No, it might have been closer to the feeling of a sharp razor blade scratching his neck very shallowly.

“Hurry up.”

Hwang Se-Ak slowly straightened his legs and stood up, raising his head. His trembling eyes met Jang Ilso’s eyes, standing right in front of him.

He forced himself to hold back the saliva that was about to drip down.

Jang Ilso’s eyes were pale and yet so deep that he couldn’t tell what he was thinking. Hwang Se-Ak realized it.

This man wasn’t someone he could dare try to understand.

He couldn’t even guess what he was feeling then, what he was thinking, whether he was being friendly or hostile... he couldn’t even dare to guess any of it.

There was just one thing that someone who was clearly on a different level could do.

“I-I am a little late, alliance lord. P-please, punish me for being this foolish!”

All he could do was surrender everything and ask for mercy.

At that moment.

The lips of Jang Ilso slightly curled up.

“... a little?”

At that cold voice that didn’t even convey a hint of emotion, Hwang Se-Ak felt his blood run cold.

“It is quite a bit late to be called ‘a little’. Isn’t that right?”

Hwang Se-Ak’s fingertips began to tremble.

All the bad things he heard about Jang Ilso came to his mind. How cruel he could be. How merciless he could be. How... much of a devil he was.

“A-Alliance lord...”

Jang Ilso smiled brightly.

“It is fine. It is alright. People are like that. It is foolish to rely on someone without thoughts. It is natural for people to slowly take down everything and then decide their minds on something.”

“T-thank you...”

“But you know.”

At that moment, Jang Ilso’s eyes narrowed like a snake’s.

Soon, he reached out and lightly grabbed the face of Hwang Se-Ak.

“What I want is not a person.”

He didn’t even apply any pressure. The extended fingers simply covered the face. But, at that moment, Hwang Se-Ak felt as if his face was being bitten by a giant beast.

“Uh...”

Jang Ilso’s eyes were visible between the fingertips covering the face.

His eyes, which had usually been calm until a moment ago, were now indescribably seething.

“You must have been wondering whether or not it was worth following me with that shallow mind. I don’t like people like you. If I am even a little cornered, the first ones to try and stab me in the back are such people.”

Hwang Se-Ak’s face turned pale.

He knew he wouldn’t be welcomed, but he never thought they would persecute him so openly. The moment he felt Jang Ilso’s obvious hostility, his entire body froze, and his legs tingled.

“What do I do? Would it be better to accept a guy who was contemplating whether to stab me in the back? Or would it be better to kill him here and eliminate the trouble? Which is a better choice? What do you think?”

Hwang Se-Ak’s whole body began to tremble like a tree. Jang Ilso’s face, smiling with the corners of his mouth twitching, was the very picture of fear. It seemed as if he would burst his head or snap his neck at any moment.

“L-Loyalty! I will be loyal! Alliance Lord! If you tell me to be your dog, I will be a dog! If you tell me to lick your feet, I will lick your feet!”

Let it be known that Hwang Se-Ak was by no means a cowardly or mean person. If the opponent hadn’t been Jang Ilso, he wouldn’t have said such words even if a sword had been put to his neck.

But now, his head was filled with fear.

“A chance! Just give me a chance! I will be loyal to the Alliance Lord, I will definitely prove my loyalty!”

“Uhh.”

As he shouted desperately, holding onto his face, Jang Ilso slowly lowered the hand that had been holding Hwang Se-Ak’s face. This time, he lightly grabbed his Adam’s Apple.

“You are wrong.”

“...”

The color drained from Hwang Se-Ak’s face.

Jang Ilso’s hand, holding his neck, tightened slightly. It was as if he was barely holding back the urge to snap his neck right away.

No words could convey a more certain warning than this. Hwang Se-Ak’s clothes were soaking wet with sweat.

At that moment, Jang Ilso opened his mouth with a meaningful expression.

“I don’t want people, but I don’t want dogs either. Dogs are loyal but stupid.”

“T-then...”

“I will ask, Hwang Se-Ak.”

“....”

“Can you protect my back?”

Hwang Se-Ak nodded without thought.

“I-I can! I will risk my life to protect you.”

“No matter what happens?”

“Of course! I will definitely be a shield that protects the Alliance Lord.”

Jang Ilso stared at Hwang Se-Ak silently after what felt like an eternity for Hwang Se-Ak; Jang Ilso’s red lips finally curved into a smile.

“Nice.”

“...”

“Remember, Hwang Se-Ak.”

“Yes, yes, Alliance Lord!”

“Whatever you did before you entered the Four Precepts Alliance, from this moment on, all of that means nothing.”

“...”

“There is just one thing you have to remember. You die for me. Only for me. Do you understand?”

“I will keep it in mind, and I will keep it in mind carefully.”

“Your answer is wrong.”

Jang Ilso lightly tapped his fingernails on the cheek of Hwang Se-Ak.

“You can do it?”

Hwang Se-Ak’s eyes trembled for a moment, but then he looked straight.

“I will.”

“... then.”

A faint smile appeared on Jang Ilso’s lips.

“I will die for you too.”

Hwang Se-Ak slowly nodded with a pale face.

Jang Ilso let go of Hwang Se-Ak’s neck and shouted as if he liked that expression.

“Alcohol!”

“Yes, Alliance Lord!”

The servants who had been waiting quickly brought a tray with alcohol and glasses.

“Hmmm.”

Jang Ilso smiled and picked up the bottle of alcohol, filling the glasses.

Pour.

After filling both glasses to the brim, he placed his hand on Hwang Se-Ak’s neck again. His long fingernails slowly scratched the neck.

The feeling of the nails digging into the neck was creepy, but Hwang Se-Ak didn’t dare flinch now. He just stood there like an ice statue, waiting for Jang Ilso’s decision.

Pour.

Red blood flowed down the neck. Jang Ilso placed the blood-soaked fingers on the glasses.

Puak!

A drop of blood fell into one glass, and another drop fell into the other glass.

Swat!

He waved his hand and brushed off Hwang Se-Ak’s blood and bow. Then, he cut his finger with his nail. This time, Jang Ilso’s blood fell into the glasses.

Drop.

Jang Ilso mixed the blood into both glasses and smiled as he held one out to Hwang Se-Ak.

“Drink.”

“H-How dare...”

“This.”

Jang Ilso paused for a moment.

As if to tell him to remember what was about to come.

“The blood you will shed for me someday, and the blood I will shed for you someday.”

“....”

“What are you going to do?”

Hwang Se-Ak, who bit his lip slightly, accepted the glass Jang Ilso offered him. Without a moment’s hesitation, he drank the alcohol in one gulp.

“Good.”

Jang Ilso also poured the remaining alcohol into his mouth with a satisfied look.

“As of now, there is no longer a name called Red Pillar Association. There is only Hwang Se-Ak of the Four Precepts Alliance.”

Hwang Se-Ak knelt down on the spot and bowed. He bowed his head deeply as if he were going to dig into the ground.

“I will present my life to the Alliance Lord.”

“That is such a wonderful gift.”

Jang Ilso patted Hwang Se-Ak on the shoulder and smiled kindly as if everything he had done just a moment ago was an illusion.

“Let’s go. One glass of alcohol isn’t enough. Let us drink all night.”

“Yes!”

“Nice. I feel really good. Hahahahah!”

Jang Ilso’s loud laughter reverberated throughout the hall.

It was the moment when the Southern lands’ Red Pillar Association swore complete obedience to the Four Precepts Alliance... no, to Jang Ilso.

Chapter 891: It Is Better To Die Than To Not Possess It (1)

A man hurried his steps.

He didn’t really show off his authority. He simply walked quickly, as if he had something to do. However, everyone who saw him as he passed by was busy bowing their heads in thought.

The reason was simple.

He was none other than Jang Ilso’s general, Ho Ga-Myung.

Within the Four Precepts Alliance, Ho Ga-Myung wasn’t even in the top ten.

But among those who were part of the alliance, there was no one foolish enough to think that Ho Ga-Myung’s actual power wasn’t formidable.

When Ho Ga-Myung reached Jang Ilso’s residence with a cold face, the guards who had been on duty bowed their heads. It was natural since the one who had approached was Ho Ga-Myung.

But.

Standing tall.

Ho Ga-Myung didn’t even move and stopped, making his hurried walk seem meaningless.

“....”

He narrowed his eyes and looked at the lowered heads of the guards.

“Come here.”

“Yes!”

The guards quickly approached Ho Ga-Myung.

“Anyone entering the residence of the Alliance Lord must be searched. Isn’t that the rule of the Four Precepts Alliance?”

“Yes, that is right.”

The guards, who had become thoughtful, quickly nodded.

“Then why are you not searching me?”

“H-How dare we search...”

“Dare?”

The faces of the guards gradually turned pale at the voice of Ho Ga-Myung, who asked swiftly.

“If someone kills Ho Ga-Myung and disguises as me, can you handle the aftermath of it?”

“...General.”

“Search.”

“Yes!”

Instead of further explanation, the guards searched Ho Ga-Myung’s body with tired faces. With trembling hands, they searched through his clothes one by one and even touched his face to check if he had been using a mask.

“Nothing odd.”

As the guards retreated, Ho Ga-Myung stared at them with an emotionless face.

The guards, whose faces were drenched in sweat, trembled with nervous expressions like criminals waiting for a judge’s verdict.

“This time, I will let this pass.”

“Thank you....”

“But if something like this happens again...”

Ho Ga-Myung’s sharp eyes glared at them.

“You will think that it would be better to die.”

“Yes, I will keep that in mind!”

“The only one you should be loyal to is the Alliance Lord. As the Alliance Lord’s guards, no one in the Four Precepts Alliance, not even me, needs to be bowed to by you.”

“....”

“Remember.”

“Yes!”

Finally, Ho Ga-Myung turned his gaze and walked inside the residence.

The guards, who barely made it back alive from the mouth of the tiger, gradually dragged their weakened legs.

Clack.

Opening the door and entering, Ho Ga-Myung looked around with sharp eyes.

After taking in everything in the room with his eyes and taking several breaths, he nodded lightly. He approached the bed in the center of the room.

It was at that moment that his face, which had been frozen like ice, began to loosen up.

“.... Alliance Lord.”

“....”

“Alliance Lord. Stop sleeping. The sun is high in the sky.”

“....”

“Alliance Lord!”

“Ughh,”

Jang Ilso, who was lying on the bed, stirred around.

“You need to get up now!”

“B-be quiet... isn’t your head hurting?”

Jang Ilso groaned and pulled the silk blanket over his head.

He didn’t say that it was wrong and rude, but Ho Ga-Myung let out a deep sigh.

“You need to get up.”

“... One moment.”

“Uh?”

“Come back after one hour. One hour... just one more hour.”

“Alliance Lord!”

Since Jang Ilso showed no signs of waking up, Ho Ga-Myung finally grabbed the blanket himself and pulled it down.

“Get up!”

“Ughh....”

In the end, Jang Ilso slowly raised his upper body. His disheveled hair and slumped shoulders made him doubt whether this Jang Ilso was the same lord as yesterday.

“Ga-Myung... didn’t I say that my head hurts badly?”

“Yes, you did. Why did you think of drinking so much then?”

“... a rat-like guy drinks a sip of alcohol and doesn’t even blink an eye. So, what do you want me to do? It’s not like I can just back out and say I cannot drink in front of him, right?”

“Why did you learn martial arts then? You can just release the toxins of the alcohol with your internal qi!”

“Ahhh... I told you to speak softly. My head is hurting.”

Jang Ilso groaned again and continued.

“You are usually smart, but you are stupid at times like this. If I had to use my internal qi while drinking, he would have thought I was better than him in martial arts alone.”

“... then?”

“But.”

Jang Ilso giggled.

“Now he can’t help but admit that I can drink better! After all, he passed out first! Hehehehe. You should have seen the look on his face before he fainted.”

Ho Ga-Myung pressed his fingers against his temple.

His head hurt.

This wasn’t a joke; his head really did hurt. His blood pressure was rising.

“... right, so cultivate now. You have proven that your alcohol tolerance is stronger, so there is no need for you to have this headache anymore.”

“Not yet.”

“Uh?”
