Jang Ilso clicked his tongue and waved his hand. At that, the maid who was waiting behind him came running like the wind and offered him honey water.

Jang Ilso drank the honey water he received and shook his head.

“I need to see Hwang Se-Ak again today. I have to show him how to cower.”

“... because of that one insignificant person.”

“Tsk tsk tsk, Ga-Myung. Ga-Myung, why are you saying such obvious things these days, which isn’t normal for you? The world is unpredictable. Someday, that insignificant person might decide my life.“

“....”

Ho Ga-Myung tried to say something bluntly but then closed his mouth. Jang Ilso’s expression changed completely.

“Regret is not something you experience because you failed to do what you could. It is something you experience because you failed to do what you should. So, of course, you should have done what you could have done a long time ago.”

A sigh escaped from Ho Ga-Myung’s mouth.

This was the true theory. But was it easy for someone to stick to that theory?

Now that he was in a position where he didn’t have to pay attention to such small matters, Jang Ilso was no different than when he was the leader of the Ten Thousand People Clan.

As a result, Jang Ilso’s work was almost like overwork. Ho Ga-Myung was naturally worried, but on the other hand, he couldn’t deny that Jang Ilso was able to completely control the Four Sects Alliance because of that kind of attitude.

“No matter what, you have to preserve the body.”

“Ga-Myung, how come you are getting more and more stuck in your ways as days pass?”

“Alliance Lord.”

“I know. I get you.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand as if he was getting sick of it.

“If someone saw me, they would think I was the most pathetic person in the world. Ugh.”

Listening to the grumbling, Ho Ga-Myung sighed.

Pathetic?

Even if he saw someone full of ill intent toward the Four Precepts Alliance, they wouldn’t dare say such a thing about Jang Ilso.

It was too unbecoming of someone who had gotten the Southern lands under him and established a new alliance in three years to be looked at like that.

The fact that Hwang Se-Ak came to him on his own feet and pledged loyalty was quite significant. That was because it was clear evidence that the Four Precepts Alliance’s influence had begun to shake not just the south, but the northern lands too.

“I have something to report.”

“What is it?”

Jang Ilso spoke with a calm expression. His face was filled with the intention of getting Ho Ga-Myung out quickly and going back to sleep.

“Mount Hua is...”

However, the moment Ho Ga-Myung spoke, Jang Ilso’s face, which had been filled with laziness and annoyance, shifted. His pale face contorted, and a heart-stopping qi was released.

“... has released their Closed Gate.”

But the momentum disappeared in an instant. It was as if Ho Ga-Myung had imagined it.

However, it was by no means a mistake.

His face turned hazy again, but the dull expression had completely disappeared. A small flash of madness flickered in his dim eyes.

“Mount Hua?”

“Yes.”

“It took a long time. So fucking long. That damned bastard.”

Jang Ilso smiled.

Of course, Ho Ga-Myung knew who the damned bastard Jang Ilso was referring to.

‘Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.’

He had no idea, but the only person in the world who could make Jang Ilso look in such a way was Mount Hua’s Sword Saint alone.

“I was starting to get annoyed and even thought about dragging him out myself...”

“It was because that moron moved as we expected.”

“Right. That... that...”

Jang Ilso scratched his head.

“What was it again? His name...?”

“... Gal Cheon-rip. It is Gal Cheon-rip, the Ghost Blade.”

“Ah, right. Gal Cheon-rip.”

“Gal Cheon-rip.”

“Let us just ignore the small fry. He is nothing.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand as if there was no reason to remember that guy’s name.

“Anyway, that Gal one seems to have gotten away with it in Shaanxi as expected. He didn’t do any real damage, right?”

“He was unilaterally destroyed.”

“Of course. Because they are idiots.”

Jang Ilso giggled.

“Well, all the roles and situations are finally set. Now all we have to do is start the game, right?”

His face seemed happy at first glance.

Did the world know that the one who had been waiting the most for the return of Mount Hua was Jang Ilso?

“Nice!”

Jang Ilso jumped up from his place.

At the same time, his body emitted pure white steam, like boiling water. He had expelled the toxins of the alcohol from his body in one breath.

“Clothes!”

“Yes, Alliance Lord!”

The servants rushed over and began to dress Jang Ilso. As they worked, Jang Ilso, waiting impatiently, frowned in annoyance.

“Now, now get out of my way.”

He pushed the servants aside and began to put the rings on his fingers himself.

Ho Ga-Myung gulped at the sight.

He had thought that the mention of Mount Hua would prompt Jang Ilso to act, but he hadn’t expected such intensity.

Yet, alongside his surprise, a strange anticipation bloomed in Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes.

Three years.

The promised three years were over. That meant the world would soon be engulfed in a storm once again by the hands of none other than Jang Ilso.

“Cancel all the plans.”

“... What are you planning to do now?”

“If the rice is cooked, we need to start eating it before it gets cold.”

Jang Ilso smiled, twisting his red lips.

“I have a place to go.”

Ho Ga-Myung’s heart sank at the sight of that sinister smile. He knew what happened every time Jang Ilso made that expression.

It wasn’t a smile of satisfaction for something accomplished.

It was a smile he made when he was about to throw his life into a pit of fire.

“It is time to play the last card. I wonder, too. Will I be able to use this card, or will I have to give up my life playing it? Hahahahahaha!”

Jang Ilso opened the door to his room and stepped out, bursting into mad laughter. Despite the pouring sunlight, he glared at the sun high in the sky, undaunted.

Jang Ilso bared his teeth as he received the blinding sunlight floating in the middle of the blue.

“It is better to die than to live without possessing it.”

His hand extended out and covered the sun. He pretended to grab it and then burst into laughter, taking long strides.

The black snake, filled with madness and venom, finally woke up from its long sleep and began to move.

Chapter 892: It Is Better To Die Than To Not Possess It (2)

The faces of those sitting around the room strangely hardened as if they were shocked.

Hong Dae-Kwang, who saw that expression, was slightly taken aback but soon realized one thing and opened his mouth in shock.

Actually, a Closed Gate didn’t mean that one entirely shut down their ears to outside news. No, to be more precise, they could not do it.

How could one completely turn their back and block their ears when they had to open the same Gate at some point? So, it was common to secretly gather the outside news while training.

But, the disciples of Mount Hua were all reacting as if they had never heard this concept before.

‘These people have no future at all.’

It seemed like they were stuck in the mountain valley, practicing with the intention of dying regardless of whether the outside world was going on or not.

Thinking about it, he could finally realize how great that marten was. If that marten wasn’t there, wouldn’t they have been swinging their sword without a care in the world even if Xi’an was destroyed?

And along with that, he realized how helpless Mount Hua was.

“Anyway, so.”

Hong Dae-Kwang scratched his head.

“Forget the southern land; now the Evils Sect has crossed into the northern lands over the Yangtze river, and are gathering under the Four Precepts Alliance.”

Hyun Jong’s expression turned serious.

The weight of the words gathering made him feel gloomy.

“That is how it happened.”

It was expected. However, it didn’t feel good to confirm that the expectation was right.

“Then should we consider the Four Sects Alliance to have been stabilized?”

Hong Dae-Kwang scratched the back of his head at Jo Gul’s question.

“Well. I don’t know how to respond to that.”

“Uh? What are you even saying?”

Hong Dae-Kwang groaned for a moment, choosing his words, and then said.

“This is a bit more complicated than we imagined. Externally, the Evils Sect has completely unified. There are no longer any forces in the southern lands resisting the Four Precepts Alliance. Even the Evil Sect here recognizes the Four Precepts Alliance as the force representing them in the central Plains and is joining under their common banner.”

“Right.”

“That is the problem.”

“What?”

When Jo Gul tilted his head as if he didn’t understand, Chung Myung asked indifferently.

“What will they do after the hunt is over?”

“Make a stew? Eat the dog by boiling it?”

Chung Myung opened his eyes wide and looked at Jo Gul.

“No... after the hunt is done, you distribute what you have hunted. Why would you go after a healthy dog? Are you mad? Do you not feel bad?”

“....”

Jo Gul blankly looked at Chung Myung and glanced around at his sahyungs. Then, a heartwarming stream of good words came out.

“Disgusting guy.”

“Dirty.”

“A person with a tainted personality.”

“...”

In an instant, Jo Gul, who had become a piece of trash beyond redemption, leaned his head against the wall with a gloomy face. Chung Myung continued speaking, ignoring Jo Gul, who kept mumbling to himself.

“Now that they have grown big enough, they probably want to split up. I don’t know about others, but the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans aren’t the type of people who would bow their heads obediently to Jang Ilso.”

“The Beggars Union thinks so too.”

Hong Dae-Kwang nodded as if Chung Myung’s words were right.

“He was too quiet to the point where it was strange. They are not the most docile of people. Even if the Alliance Lord is Jang Ilso, this is too much.”

At that moment, Baek Cheon asked with a face that seemed not to understand.

“Can I ask you something?”

“Of course.”

“If you think that way, isn’t it strange that the problem is happening now?”

“Hmm? What do you mean?”

Baek Cheon said in a clear voice, looking at Hong Dae-Kwang.

“Since the Four Precepts Alliance has unified the southern land, war with the Nine Great Sects in the south will be inevitable.”

“Right.”

“But wouldn’t it be difficult for them if internal strife happened before the war? Even if they have an ulterior motive, wouldn’t it be reasonable to cause trouble after unifying the South? If they cause internal strife for no reason and lose, everything will be lost.”

Hong Dae-Kwang nodded his head as if he agreed.

“You are quite knowledgeable.”

“...it is nothing.”

“Right. That is reasonable. If you don’t consider one thing.”

“One thing?”

“Hegemony Lord.”

“....”

Baek Cheon’s face naturally hardened at the thought of Jang Ilso.

“Over the past three years, the Hegemony Lord has completely established himself as the leader of the Evil Sect. It is no exaggeration to say that the forces currently flocking to him are actually subservient to Jang Ilso.”

“Are you saying that he has become strong enough to subdue the other Shenzhou Five Clans?”

“Then there cannot be any internal strife for them. But think about it. What if a great war that will determine the fate of the North and South breaks out, and Jang Ilso leads the war to victory as the leader of the Four Precepts Alliance?”

“Ah...”

Baek Cheon nodded as if he understood what was being said.

If that happened, it would be really hard to approach Jang Ilso’s dignity again.

“I understand. So if there is a problem, it would be rather....”

“Yes. I am talking about it now.”

Hong Dae-Kwang mumbled with a stern face.

“The calm before the storm is used in situations like this. It wouldn’t be strange if war broke out tomorrow, and it wouldn’t be strange if war broke out anywhere.”

Hyun Jong, who had been listening, groaned softly and asked.

“What’s going on with the Nine Great Sects? The Nine Sects need to know this.”

“Of course, the Nine Great Sects must be aware of this. But they’re currently too preoccupied to manage the chaos. Particularly, Hunan is just filling the gap between Wudang and Namgung...”

“Hunan?”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang paused and slowly turned his head.

His voice, familiar yet foreboding, was Chung Myung’s, who was now staring at him with wide eyes.

“Where?”

“... Did I say something wrong?”

“No. I just asked where it was.”

“I said Hunan.”

“Ah, Hunan?”

Chung Myung smiled brightly.

“Shaolin is in Hunan?”

“S-Shaolin is in Hunan, of course. Mount Song is in Hunan...”

“Isn’t it near the Yangtze River?”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang fell silent.

In truth, defending the mouth of the Yangtze River, where the Four Precepts Alliance might invade at any time, was the responsibility of the Nine Great Sects. The Yangtze River massacre and the non-violence treaty were initially due to Wudang’s foolish actions.

However, the Nine Great Sects had now withdrawn from the Yangtze River, citing a lack of resources, leaving the Tang family struggling to manage it.

But, as Chung Myung probed further, Hong Dae-Kwang, who was also part of the Nine Great Sects, couldn’t help but feel embarrassed.

“R... right. They’re currently in Hunan...”

Hong Dae-Kwang, trying to evade the situation, clearly saw Chung Myung’s neck twist to the side.

“Hunan.”

“... Uh. R-right. In Hunan...”

“But you were just sitting back and watching as the Evil Sect people rushed in?”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang shut his mouth.

It felt like a runaway cart had suddenly veered to the side and hit him on the head.

“So, he wasn’t in the Yangtze River that far away, but in the neighboring places, and people didn’t care whether the Evil Sect was burning Shaanxi or turning it into farmland, and just continued to chant?”

“That... Shaolin is short on people...”

“And the beggars who were playing around and rolling in Hubei, begging and falling down, were what?”

“....”

“Sigh...”

“Hahahah.”

Chung Myung laughed brightly. But it wasn’t a genuine laugh. One could clearly see the bright blue veins on Chung Myung’s forehead.

“The bald bastard looked away?”

Chung Myung suddenly got up from his place.

Then the Five Swords, sensing something ominous, rushed at him like lightning and grabbed him.

“W-where are you going?”

“Where else! Where will I go? I am going to Shaolin!”

“What will you do by going to Shaolin?”

“Are you asking because you don’t know? You crazy bastard! No matter what happens, they are still acting like that! Are they messing around with the lives of other people? If even Avalokitesvara saw this, he would come smashing their heads with lotus stems! Since he cannot do it, I should smash their heads!”

“N-no, no matter what, you are speaking about Shaolin’s abbot, and you are talking too much...”

“What of the abbot? A Shaolin abbot is born with armor around the head or what? If we hit him, it is bound to break!”

“Y-You bastard! Calm down!”

It was at the moment when Baek Cheon was cursing and grabbing Chung Myung.

“This... is an outrage!”

All of the Five Swords slowly turned their heads back at the sudden angry voice. One person was seen there, shaking in rage.

Of course, it was a situation where they would be angry enough. If Hong Dae-Kwang’s explanation was indeed right, it was clear that Shaolin pretended not to know about Shaanxi.

Even if they had no power, if Shaolin had moved, Gal Cheon-rip would have never touched Shaanxi.

This situation would normally be infuriating, and there would be nothing to say even if they cursed Shaolin.

That is if the person cursing didn’t have such a brightly shining head.

“Does this mean Shaolin didn’t send troops to Shaanxi? What kind of madness is that!”

“...that, monk...? That is indeed crazy. But he is the abbot.“

And he belonged to Shaolin.

He seemed a bit confused and misunderstood because he had been wearing the Mount Hua robe for too long now....

But Hae Yeon’s face was filled with rage. His face, which was rarely contorted, now looked a terrible sight.

“How can a temple that is supposed to save people turn its back on suffering civilians! This is absolutely wrong! I will let the abbot know my intentions...”

“Yah, you bastard!”

At that moment, Chung Myung quickly shook off the Five Swords and ran to Hae Yeon, kicking him.

Chung Myung kicked Hae Yeon like a ball, sending him rolling into the wall, his eyes wide as he kicked him.

“This brat is pretending not to be responsible for it? Yah, you moron! Aren’t you from Shaolin!”

“Ah! Ack! Taoist! I haven’t set foot in Shaolin for years... ack!”

Hae Yeon was desperately protesting, but if he sought someone to commiserate with, it wouldn’t be Chung Myung.

“Once Shaolin, always Shaolin, you moron! And you were speaking nonsense at the tournament and called us traitors, right? Traitors! You moron! Try again! Say it again! Traitors?”

“Ack! When did that even happen! Ack!”

Everyone trembled as they watched Chung Myung attack Hae Yeon.

‘This damned brat!’

‘I still remember that.’

‘So persistent.’

“Anyway, these damned kids!”

After venting his anger on Hae Yeon, Chung Myung started to approach Hong Dae-Kwang with his arms rolled up.

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face turned pale.

‘Won’t I get hit too?’

Even though this kid was a member of Shaolin, he had been away for years, and he was being beaten red and blue. So why wouldn’t Chung Myung hit someone who is from the Beggars Union, right?

Fortunately, Chung Myung didn’t seem to have any intention of beating up Hong Dae-Kwang, and he sat down in front of him.

“They are doing all sorts of insane things.”

The voice rang loudly.

The Four Precepts Alliance was running wild, and the Nine Sects were just thinking without acting.

“I didn’t expect them to do this in the first place. Those morons.”

Hong Dae-Kwang flinched as Chung Myung’s gaze turned to him.

“So, where is the Tang family now?”

“T-Tang family is now guarding the Yangtze River...”

“...Yangtze River?”

“....”

Hong Dae-Kwang licked his lips as if he had nothing else to say, and Chung Myung snickered.

“This is a mess, a total mess.”

The moment Chung Myung’s face seemed to go red, Hyun Jong opened his mouth.

“First of all.”

“....”

When Hyun Jong opened his mouth, Chung Myung gulped down the curses and took a deep breath to calm down.

“Let’s meet the Tang family lord. And we should let the Heavenly Friends Alliance know that Mount Hua has stepped out of the Closed Gate.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

Hyun Jong said with a small sigh.

“The situation isn’t easy, but on the other hand, we knew this would happen, so we have been struggling for a long time.”

“Right.”

“Let us fix them one by one. Mount Hua has the strength to do that.”

“Tsk.”

Chung Myung nodded with a slightly anxious look. Hyun Jong smiled brightly.

Now, it was time to fix everything that had gone wrong.

Chapter 893: It Is Better To Die Than To Not Possess It (3)

Fatigue wasn’t simply a physical problem.

Tang Gunak’s current state clearly proved that fact.

Those who had mastered the martial arts of Murim did not easily get tired even after working hard, and they always felt energetic. However, a dark shadow was cast over Tang Gunak’s face as he sat in his room.

‘It doesn’t seem like I read through it when I read it.’

Tang Gunak finally put the document in his hand down on the desk. He had already read it three times in a row, but the content didn’t stick in his head. It wasn’t like there was anything particularly bad written in it.

He quietly rubbed his eyelids.

‘I am exhausted.’

It seemed like he hadn’t had a proper rest in the past three years. He had to manage the chaos in the Yangtze River while also handling the issues in his home, Sichuan, so he didn’t have enough time.

He was trying his best not to miss out on training, even reducing his sleep, but now it felt like he had reached his limit.

“Are you alright?”

At Tang Chan’s question, Tang Gunak slowly nodded.

“Just a bit tired.”

“... Lord.”

A sigh escaped from Tang Chan’s mouth.

It was understandable. It would be strange if he weren’t exhausted, considering the hardships Tang Gunak had endured for the past three years.

“You need to rest.”

“It is alright for now.”

“Of course, it is fine. The Lord is always fine. You always have some energy left.”

“Hmm?”

Tang Gunak raised his head and looked at Tang Chan.

“That is why you won’t be able to rest till the end. You will not regret resting until the very end.”

Tang Gunak, who had been listening quietly, raised the corners of his lips and snickered.

“That is painful to say.”

“Father.”

It wasn’t Lord, but Father. Tang Chan sighed quietly with a serious look and continued to speak.

“Please get some rest. It is alright since my sister isn’t here. If Sister had seen this, Father, you wouldn’t have been so safe either.”

“... that is a bit scary to say.”

Contrary to the words, a gentle smile appeared on Tang Gunak’s face. He seemed to be in a good mood at the thought of Tang Soso.

“And before Father suffers, I will be the first to die.”

“....”

Tang Gunak didn’t deny what Tang Chan had said. It was a well-known fact that Tang Soso had always controlled Tang Chan like a rat.

Of course, when Soso was in the Tang family, Tang Chan’s strength was higher, but was a relationship something that could be established only through force?

If he had seen Tang Chan talking back to Tang Soso, Tang Gunak would have rushed over and stabbed Tang Chan between his eyebrows.

“Are you still afraid of your sister at this age?”

“It’s even scarier at this age. When I was young, I would have been alright with getting hit, but at my age, getting hit by Sister is a huge thing.”

“... I guess so.”

“In the past, if I had wanted to, I would run away. Now, it isn’t possible.”

“You cannot?”

Tang Chan sighed.

“With her nature, there is no way she would slack off from her training. She must have gritted her teeth and trained for the last three years. How can I beat her when she is training like that in Mount Hua with the Divine Dragon?”

“My son is...”

“Sister is also your daughter, Father; it is the same. You know.”

Tang Gunak laughed softly and agreed silently.

It was just idle chatter, but thanks to it, he felt a little better. Perhaps Tang Chan also had the same thought when he said those words.

“I know I need to rest. Let us just finish this work and rest.”

“I heard that more than ten times already.”

Tang Chan, who was about to say something, finally sighed and stopped himself.

What could he do?

Resting didn’t mean that the work would vanish. If one postponed the day’s work, they would only have more work to do tomorrow. There was no such thing as half-assing now.

But one could not expect Tang Gunak to do such half-hearted work. That was something the Tang family would never do.

‘I have to figure out a way....’

It was at that moment that resentment crept back into Tang Chan’s heart.

“Father, no, Lord!”

Burst!

Tang Pae kicked the door open and rushed in.

Looking at his thoughtful face, Tang Gunak instinctively felt something bad had happened and jumped up from his place. His eyes, which held brief comfort, turned cold instantly.

“What is happening?”

Tang Pae waved his hand as if it wasn’t true after seeing Tang Gunak’s reaction. But he didn’t seem to be able to speak easily and just continued to smile numerous times.

“... speak slowly. What happened?”

Even in the midst of all this, Tang Gunak maintained his composure and could be called a model of a lord leading the family.

That was certainly the case until the answer came out.

“Mount Hua has come out!”

“What?”

In an instant, Tang Gunak jumped over the desk and ran to Tang Pae, grabbing him by the collar.

“What now?”

Tang Chan’s mouth dropped.

Of course, it was shocking that Tang Gunak grabbed Tang Pae by the collar, but what was even more shocking was what happened before that.

‘He j-jumped over the desk?’

That Tang Gunak?

The one who always insisted on maintaining a proper form as a family?

“Kuak! Kuak! L-Lord! Let go of...”

“Ah! Sorry.”

Tang Gunak was startled and let go of Tang Pae’s collar, which he had been holding.

“What place did you say it was?”

“M-Mount Hua! Mount Hua sent us a communication. Here, the letter is right here....”

As soon as Tang Pae took the letter out of his robe, Tang Gunak snatched it away like a hawk.

Clack!

And without a moment’s hesitation, he tore open the envelope and took out the paper inside.

“....”

An expression that was hard to describe appeared on Tang Gunak’s face as he silently checked the contents of the letter.

It seemed absurd... or maybe even shocking. However, that strange expression soon turned into a gentle smile.

“W-what does it say?”

“Look at it.”

Tang Chan accepted the letter that Tang Gunak handed him. The letters written on the large piece of paper were barely a single line long.

-Why are you struggling for nothing? Just throw everything away and come to Mount Hua first.

“Huh....”

A squeak sound came from Tang Chan’s mouth.

“Hehehe.....”

For a moment, he doubted his eyes, thinking that he had seen it wrong, but the more he thought about it, the more he realized that it couldn’t have been wrong.

Who in the world could send such a letter to the head of the Sichuan Tang family? Even the head of Shaolin couldn’t dare send a single letter to the head of the Sichuan Tang family and tell them to come.

Only one person could do it in this world, Chung Myung.

“... What will you do?”

“Hmmm.”

As the smile on Tang Gunak’s lips grew deeper, Tang Chan found himself smiling back. It seemed like the first time in years he had seen Tang Gunak with this expression.

It wasn’t a smile that showed he could escape from these suffocating situations now that Mount Hua had returned. Tang Gunak wasn’t the kind of person who would smile so brightly just because he could throw away his work.

That smile right there was the smile of someone who confirmed the health and well-being of a close friend he had lost contact with for a long time.

“Anyway, I cannot stop him.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

Right.

“Are you leaving?”

Another loud voice was heard from the outside. Tang Gunak, who could guess the owner of the voice, turned his head.

“Enter.”

The door opened without delay, and a person rushed in.

“Did you receive the letter?”

Green Forest King, Im So-Byeong, asked without even greeting him. Tang Gunak silently handed the letter he received.

“As you can see.”

“... Ugh.”

Im So-Byeong groaned after checking the contents.

“Did you receive it?”

“Please check it.”

Im So-Byeong also handed Tang Gunak the letter he was holding.

-Take all the ledgers and come to Mount Hua.

“... Hahaha.”

It was even more concise than the letter Tang Gunak received.

“Mount Hua has finally stepped out.”

At Tang Gunak’s mumbling, everyone exchanged glances with each other, feeling a sense of emotion.

“Ugh.”

However, only Im So-Byeong was frowning.

“It seems that the Green Forest King isn’t happy.”

“It isn’t that I am not happy...”

When Tang Gunak asked casually, Im So-Byeong grabbed his head.

“I don’t know how many of the ledgers, which have been accumulated over the past three years, are there, and he is asking me to bring them all in! I kept sending copies to the Eunha merchant union to avoid this!”

“....”

“Five carts will start first! Five carts!”

“... calm down.”

“Oh my. What...”

Im So-Byeong groaned.

“He is making people suffer like this as soon as he steps out, so I am worried about what will happen in the future. If we meet face to face, he will scream.”

Im So-Byeong shivered as if trembling.

However, Tang Gunak, who was watching him, smiled broadly.

“You look happy for someone who has worries.”

Im So-Byeong touched his face at those words. He could clearly feel the corners of his lips curling up under his fingertips.

Im So-Byeong, who was slightly embarrassed, bit back at Tang Gunak for no reason.

“... But still, do you think I should come?”

Tang Gunak covered his mouth with his fist and coughed lightly. He realized belatedly that he was unusually excited.

Up until then, they had done what they had to do. However, it was also true that even when things went well, they couldn’t help feeling suffocated for three years.

The moment they received Chung Myung’s letters, they could clearly see what the suffocation that had been tormenting them had been.

“What should we do?”

“What can we do?”

Tang Gunak smiled brightly.

“If he tells us to come, we should go. What power do we have to refuse the words of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“... Since the Lord says so, I will have to be dragged along without fail. Ugh.”

Tang Gunak turned and looked at Tang Pae.

“We head to Mount Hua.”

“Yes, Lord. But... who will hold the position of Lord if you move?”

“Hmmm.”

Tang Gunak recalled the words written on the letter, ‘right away.’

“Tell the servants to stay back and not come out unless needed.”

“That could be a problem.”

“I know.”

Tang Gunak nodded.

“But Mount Hua’s Sword Saint isn’t the type of person to bother writing unnecessary words on a letter.”

“... Right.”

Im So-Byeong nodded as if he completely agreed with those words.

“You seem to have something in mind, so I will do as you say.”

“Yes, Lord. I will.”

“We leave.”

“A-already? No matter how urgent things are, leaving this swiftly?”

Tang Gunak spoke to him with an expressionless face.

“I need to check with my own eyes.”

“Check?”

Tang Gunak turned his head. Through the door that Im So-Byeong had opened just a moment ago, the blue sky was clearly visible. He mumbled in a small voice.

“How much they have changed in the past three years.”

An unconcealed sense of anticipation rose.

“I wonder if that bad-tempered imoogi really has come back as a dragon.”

No one opened their mouths at those words.

Only Im So-Byeong sighed, hitting his head with the fan.

“Even if he became a dragon, he would still be an evil dragon....”

“There is nothing we can do about that.”

Tang Gunak said while looking at Tang Pae.

“Then I will go.”

“Uh? Father? What?”

Tang Pae was still flustered, but Tang Gunak quickly left the room without even listening to the answer. He disappeared in an instant, and Tang Pae lowered his head.

Im So-Byeong shook his head and patted the kid on the shoulder.

“... you must be having a hard time.”

“He isn’t that kind of person...”

A strange place where the next head of the Tang family received comfort from the Green Forest King.

This was the Yangtze River.

Chapter 894: It Is Better To Die Than To Not Possess It (4)

Swish.

Even the moon hid behind the clouds, and a dark night fell. A black shadow slowly walked towards the warehouse at the top of Eunha.

He looked around and hid secretly in the shadows, avoiding the eyes of those passing by.

His martial arts skills seemed quite strong, and even the warriors of the merchant guilds, who must have trained quite a bit, could not detect his presence and passed by him calmly.

“...”

He waited for the warriors to completely pass in the darkness and cautiously approached the warehouse. His eyes flashed ominously as he looked at the large lock on the door.

Kiikik.

The man placed his finger gently on the keyhole of the lock. He held that position for a moment then took his hand away and took out a thin wire from his robe, bending it here and there.

The wire, folded into a shape, went into the keyhole.

And after a while.

Click.

The lock opened naturally without a problem.

He carefully opened the lock, looked around once more, fixed his mask, and then crept into the warehouse.

Swish. Swish.

The sound of the cloth laid on the floor to prevent the goods from being damaged quietly spread out.

The man, who succeeded in quietly closing the door, looked around the warehouse with sharp eyes. It was filled with all sorts of things, but he didn’t seem to be searching around rashly, so it seemed like he had something else in mind.

After a while, he quietly closed his eyes in the middle of the warehouse, as if he couldn’t find what he was looking for.

And then...

Blink.

A dazzling light emanated from the man’s eyes as he opened them.

‘Over there.’

He walked ahead without hesitation and lightly tapped the box stored in the innermost, coolest place with the back of his hand.

Thud! Thud!

The corners of the man’s lips, hidden under the mask, curled up.

‘This is it.’

He was careful with his hands as he unwrapped the cloth covering the box. When the box was fully revealed, he grabbed the tightly nailed lid and applied force.

Crack. Crack.

That was the moment when the lid of the box was finally ripped off.

Clack!

The warehouse door, which had been closed, opened violently, and a group of warriors rushed in.

“Stop moving!”

The one who opened the lid flinched. The two eyes revealed through the mask contorted.

“... damn it.”

His hands moved before his head. As soon as he confirmed there was a problem, the man stuffed the contents of the box into his arm sleeves and kicked the ground.

“Catch him!”

Several people with drawn swords rushed at him like lightning, but the man leaped up faster than those who were chasing him. He intended to escape by breaking through the ceiling.

But at that moment, a golden light quickly rose above the masked man’s head.

“Ohhh!”

Hae Yeon, who had flown, threw a fist of resentment at the masked man. The masked man flinched for a moment and tried to change direction by flipping his body in the air. But at that moment, Baek Cheon immediately pulled out his sword and attacked the masked man with his sword qi.

“Kuak!”

The masked man’s eyes became even more distorted.

He could fight if he wanted to, but if he stalled, more would come at him. He shouldn’t fight them one by one but should pull himself out....

That was the moment.

Clack!

He heard the sound of metals entangling each other from below, and then a large iron net flew in and covered the man.

“Ack!”

The masked man, crushed by the net, fell down.

“Hold him tight!”

“Don’t let go!”

“Caught him, this brat!”

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul held the ends of the net tightly and pinned him to the ground. The man, who was struggling for a moment, glared at those who had subdued him with poisonous eyes.

“This...”

A snapping sound came from the man’s mouth. Baek Cheon slowly approached him, looked down with cold eyes, and spoke.

“...take this off this man’s mask.”

“Yes, sasuk.”

Jo Gul reached into the net and removed the mask, revealing a very familiar face.

“Kuaak!”

Those who saw the face sighed in unison.

The masked man, or rather Chung Myung, glared at Hae Yeon with eyes full of resentment.

“That damn bastard! Did you really hit us?”

“...Amitabha! It is all a misunderstanding.”

“Misunderstanding? Misunderstanding, acckk! Ah, really? Misunderstanding? If you misunderstand twice, you could kill someone! Misunderstanding?”

“Ahem.”

Hae Yeon turned his head slightly, feeling shy. Baek Cheon saved him from further embarrassment.

“No matter what....”

He frowned, unable to comprehend the situation.

“Even if you want to drink, is it worth robbing it from the merchant house? Uh? You insane bastard!”

Just then, a pure white bottle rolled out of Chung Myung’s sleeve.

When Yoon Jong snatched the bottle, Chung Myung glared at him and shouted in a hurry.

“I haven’t even sipped it yet!”

Baek Cheon shook his head. Then he turned his gaze and praised Tang Soso.

“...the net is effective like you said.”

“You have to use a net to catch animals. It wouldn’t be of any use if they had swords.”

“Even if he isn’t in the right mind, he wouldn’t draw the sword and swing it at his sahyungs.”

“He swung it?”

“...let us just say he isn’t in the right mind.”

Baek Cheon crouched down in front of Chung Myung, looking at the situation behind him as if being rude.

“Please act like a human, okay? Where do you think this is...”

“No, then give me money!”

“....”

“I don’t have a penny! I cannot go out! I cannot even drink! Why am I even training for?”

“...you are a Taoist, Chung Myung.”

And a Taoist was someone who trained in the Way of Tao.

At that moment, Baek Cheon’s head drooped as if he had no strength to say anything.

Pung!

A refreshing sound rang out as Yoon Jong thoughtlessly opened the bottle of alcohol.

Instantly, a fragrant scent of alcohol spread through the warehouse.

Gulp.

Baek Cheon’s Adam’s apple moved noticeably. He gulped without realizing it and turned his head in surprise, only to see Chung Myung’s eyes completely red and drooling.

“... that....”

Even the others couldn’t take their eyes off the bottle.

Realizing he might have made a mistake, Yoon Jong quickly put the cork back onto the bottle.

“....”

“....”

There was an odd silence.

The first one to break the silence was, of course, the bravest man of Mount Hua, Jo Gul.

“Well... actually, Chung Myung did his part, but... wouldn’t one bottle of alcohol be alright to take?”

Of course, Chung Myung just watched the battle from behind, but no one pointed it out.

“Ah, right. Actually, if he endured this much with sahyung, he must have been through a lot. It’s strange that a tiger doesn’t go crazy when it’s tied up for so long and only given grass to eat instead of meat. Right?”

Tang Soso quickly took Jo Gul’s hand.

“Hmm. But we haven’t even paid tribute to the souls of the victims yet....”

“Isn’t it normal to drink at a funeral?”

Yoon Jong, who was trying to politely object, glared at Jo Gul, who suddenly interrupted. However, he was too speechless at this logic.

“Of course, you shouldn’t be drinking too much, right? But the right amount. Maybe one bottle... wouldn’t we just show gratitude to the dead?”

Baek Cheon was about to cut off Jo Gul, saying it was ridiculous.

“... we can have a drink while watching over.”

Baek Cheon looked back at Yu Yiseol with a face that seemed like he’d been hit on the back of his head. However, she clearly avoided his gaze.

Baek Cheon’s trembling eyes were fixed on the bottle of alcohol that Yoon Jong held.

To be honest, the ones who really needed alcohol weren’t Chung Myung, but them. Chung Myung must have sought out and drunk the alcohol Hwang Jongi had given him while training.

After leaving Mount Hua and entering the mountains, Chung Myung must have had a hard time, but in any case, the others had endured the longest.

‘N-no, even so...’

Baek Cheon shook his head, trying to demonstrate his superhuman patience and reason as the next Sect leader. Still, the gazes of the Five Swords pierced Baek Cheon like fiery arrows.

‘These bastards.’

Their decision was frozen. They had already made up their minds.

“Ahem.”

Baek Cheon coughed with a distressed face and wiped the corner of his lips. A faint wet spot appeared on the sleeve.

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong discussed Yu Yiseol interfering, while Baek Cheon coughed innocently.

A feeble voice escaped from Chung Myung’s mouth as he blankly watched this.

“...they are growing up well.”

Get rid of the net, you brats!

Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, Jo Gul, Chung Myung, Tang Soso, and Hae Yeon.

If any martial artists saw these people sitting together, they would all be amazed.

Mount Hua’s Sword, Chung Myung, received recognition from Jang Ilso, surpassing the skilled individuals of Mount Hua, including the Righteous Sword, Baek Cheon, who garnered attention as the next Sect leader. Then there are Mount Hua’s Five Swords, incomparable to their peers of other sects, and Hae Yeon, the best warrior in Shaolin.

These individuals were truly worthy of being called the future leaders in murim. It would certainly be impressive to see such people sitting together.

It would certainly be impressive. It would be shocking...

“Kuaak!”

‘Is this alright?’

No matter what, stealing alcohol from someone else’s warehouse was...

Gulp! Gulp! Gulp! Gulp!

“...”

But he soon ended up laughing happily.

From the moment he realized that the person drinking the bottle vigorously was not Chung Myung but Hae Yeon, such worries were meaningless.

‘Let us not think about it.’

But...

“Ugh!”

Ah, the alcohol tasted so good. It really stuck to the throat.

Baek Cheon, who had let go of his thoughts, downed the glass of alcohol and looked back at Chung Myung with a relaxed face.

Gulp. Gulp. Gulp.

He was drinking.

Chung Myung was pouring alcohol as if he had finally found a house after wandering around the desert and drinking water.

“Kuaaak!”

Finally looking at Chung Myung’s expression, he pulled the empty bottle from his mouth with a wet sound... he couldn’t help but think, what is happiness in life?

“Ah. I really thought I would die.”

“I almost got one for the first time.”

“... I said it wouldn’t happen.”

I should just stop talking.

Chung Myung wiped a drop of alcohol from his chin.

“Hmm.”

He then quietly placed the bottle of alcohol to the side. His eyes, which had been raised, were slightly dim.

Chapter 895: It Is Better To Die Than To Not Possess It (5)

They also knew that the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans were no ordinary people. Didn’t they already witness their strength with their own eyes in the Yangtze River?

They were not pushed back an inch by the leaders of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families who commanded the world. No, in some ways, they were even overwhelming.

But...

‘Will Jang Ilso be overthrown by them?’

Even if he was caught by them, he wasn’t the kind of person who would waste time there. Jang Ilso would cut off his own ankle and go on his way.

Baek Cheon’s face hardened as he thought this far.

He wasn’t thinking this way because he was taking the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans lightly. It was because Jang Ilso’s presence was so much greater than theirs.

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung with a mix of respect and frustration.

It was a clear fact that Chung Myung hated Jang Ilso more than anyone else in murim. But when he heard it like this, he started to think that maybe the person Chung Myung acknowledged the most in the world was also Jang Ilso.

‘It is a little annoying.’

Who else could make that damned guy say something like that?

Chung Myung took a sip from a new bottle of alcohol and continued speaking.

Chung Myung’s voice was filled with confidence.

Even Chung Myung of the past wouldn’t have been able to endure that training at their age. Of course, they would have done it if needed...

-Who? You? Youuuuu?

Anyway.

Those who had driven themselves harshly needed a break, and that was only right. It wasn’t like Chung Myung stole alcohol from the warehouse because he wanted to drink...

-You are being a liar.

It didn’t seem like Jang Ilso would start a war across the Yangtze River. Jang Ilso was the type who created a battlefield advantageous to him and manipulated it as he pleased.

The fact that the Evil Sect had been quiet recently suggests they were up to something. A decisive move to resolve the internal clashes within the Four Precepts Alliance and see the war against the Nine Great Sects to victory.

“... well, it makes sense.”

Chung Myung mumbled quietly, twisting the corners of his lips.

No matter how mysterious the strategy was, it was meaningless if one lacked skills. That was something Chung Myung had felt to his bones at the Yangtze River.

Now, Chung Myung... no, Mount Hua would show Jang Ilso what it meant.

Yes.

Chapter 896: I Thought You Would Come As A Dragon (1)

The inside of Eunha Merchant Manor was crowded with people.

The Xi’an sects, who had heard that Mount Hua was returning, gathered to see them off.

“... Did you come in person?”

“Ahem.”

In response to the question from the clan leader Dan Byung-Rip of the Great Wind Clan, clan leader Nam Ja-Myung coughed loudly.

He pretended to calm his expression and spoke in a solemn tone.

“Even though we belong to different clans, shouldn’t we remember to be grateful? Since we have received a favor from Mount Hua, it is only natural to show respect.”

“For that, you have a lot in your hands.”

“... It is just a token of sincerity.”

“If you show sincerity twice, you will have to pull out the roots of the sect. Today is the first time I found that the Full Moon Clan has such money.”

“T-this man is serious!”

When Nam Ja-Myung glared at him, Dan Byung-Rip turned his head away with a sour look on his face. Although he was teasing him, he also understood Nam Ja-Myung’s feelings.

‘I guess this is an inevitable choice.’

Not only the clans of Xi’an but also the people of Xi’an who received the grace of Mount Hua had come.

Now, everyone in Xi’an knew that those Evil Sect bastards killed innocent villagers on their way to Xi’an. Everyone could easily guess that Xi’an would have suffered the same fate if Mount Hua hadn’t come.

So naturally, Xi’an people’s favor towards Mount Hua soared to an unparalleled level. Three years ago, Mount Hua protected the pride of Shaanxi in the Yangtze River. Now they had saved their lives, so it would be strange if they were not happy.

This meant...

‘Now, if you want to do business in Xi’an, you have no choice but to align with Mount Hua.’

The disciples who were there now might be able to protect them. But now and in the future, why would anyone want to deal with Southern Edge’s clans when there were other sub-clans of Mount Hua, like the Hwa-young clan?

Public sentiment was sometimes cold to the extent of being cruel.

Now, the kids did not speak of the Thirty-Six Sword Movements while swinging their sticks. Since the words “Plum Blossom Sword technique” were coming out of the children’s mouths, the public sentiment in Xi’an was as good as gone.

Even if the Southern Edge were to release its Closed Gate, it would not be able to reverse this.

So, the representative of Southern Edge’s sub-clans, Nam Ja-Myung, would naturally be upset.

‘If I were to clearly switch to Mount Hua, my relationship with Southern Edge would be ruined, and if I were to stay as Southern Edge’s sub-clan, then my name would be ruined.’

A warrior clan that didn’t have disciples was no different from a store that customers didn’t visit.

On the other hand...

“Hello!”

“Ohh, young clan leader!”

“Hehe. I have only seen you for a few days, but you seem to have turned more devoted.”

The other side literally hit the jackpot.

Everyone was impatient because they couldn’t speak to Wei Soheng, who was from the Hwa-young clan. The fact that people were so humble to the next clan leader, who wasn’t even a fully fledged clan leader, showed the position the Hwa-young clan held in Xi’an.

‘Of course. It is the Hwa-young clan.’

The Hwa-young clan was not just a simple sub-sect of Mount Hua. It was a true friend that had stayed by Mount Hua’s side even when no one in the world of murim showed interest.

A friend who stayed by the side of Mount Hua during difficult times was treated much better than a friend who only came in good times.

Those who knew how much Mount Hua values the Hwa-young clan could not help but try to look good.

‘Xi’an has really changed so much.’

No, it wasn’t just Xi’an that has changed.

Now that Mount Hua has returned, all of Kangho must naturally be nervous.

Even before Mount Hua entered their Closed Gate, each of their actions had stirred the Murim world. It was clear that their skills had improved significantly over the past three years, causing quite a storm.

That was when.

“Come out!”

“Mount Hua’s people are coming out!”

As someone shouted, all eyes turned toward them. Indeed, Mount Hua’s swordsmen were seen walking out in a line.

Those watching them gulped momentarily, a strange look in their eyes.

When they had fought here before, they shuddered at the killing intent and presence of Mount Hua’s swordsmen. But now, they didn’t feel that at all.

There was a moderate amount of gentleness and calmness.

‘It is like the Southern Edge.’

‘No, it seems like they are more relaxed than Southern Edge.’

‘Now Mount Hua has truly acquired the dignity of a great sect.’

It was not just skills that made people look up to them. Those who were once again impressed by Mount Hua opened their mouths and greeted Hyun Jong, who was in the lead.

“Sect leader! I heard you are going back to Mount Hua!”

“I don’t know how to repay the favor that Mount Hua and Southern Edge have shown me.”

“We will also visit Mount Hua often. So please stop by Xi’an frequently.”

Hyun Jong, slightly surprised by the crowd of people, smiled gently.

“You are just going home, but I don’t know what to do with myself for sending you off like this.”

“Of course you should.”

Nam Ja-Myung stepped forward and bowed his head to Hyun Jong.

Hyun Jong smiled brightly and didn’t say anything, so Nam Ja-Myung’s eyes sent warning signals.

In addition, the disciples of the Mount Hua sect standing behind Hyun Jong were all looking at each other as if to say, ‘Not Southern Edge, not that place, you moron!’

Hyun Jong lightly waved his hand.

The disciples, who had been desperate to say a word, soon gave up and cleared the way.

Except for one person.

“... Did you do something again?”

At that moment, Hyun Jong turned around, making Chung Myung flinch.

Hyun Jong quickly turned his head as if he didn’t want to continue the conversation. Only then did Chung Myung look at Wei Soheng, mumbling.

Wei Soheng nodded quickly, understanding after seeing Chung Myung’s confident face.

“It isn’t a bother.”

“You are already doing a good job at that.”

“Yah, I am not denying that, but it doesn’t seem like something you should be saying.”
